===== BEGIN FILE node/discover.mjs | SHA256 374b65cc97b8193a2537ffcc441a46ba8c3d3961d20d8253895229f5ddc76c82 | BYTES 28559 =====
#!/usr/bin/env node
/** Read-only local inventory and private per-user node installation. No model is started by discovery. */
import { execFile, spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { constants } from 'node:fs';
import * as fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import { delimiter, dirname, isAbsolute, join, relative, resolve, sep, win32 } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { setTimeout as delay } from 'node:timers/promises';

const SOURCE_ROOT = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const TOOL_NAMES = ['claude', 'codex', 'gemini', 'deepseek', 'ollama', 'lms', 'aider', 'opencode'];
const PROVIDERS = new Set(['ollama', 'lms']);
const SLUG = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,99}$/;
const RESERVED = /^(?:all|broadcast|CON|PRN|AUX|NUL|COM[1-9]|LPT[1-9])$/i;
const HASH = /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/;
const inside = (path, root) => { const suffix = relative(root, path); return !suffix || (!isAbsolute(suffix) && suffix !== '..' && !suffix.startsWith(`..${sep}`)); };
const digest = bytes => createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
const json = value => JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) + '\n';
const fail = message => { throw new Error(message); };

async function runFile(file, args, timeoutMs = 2000) {
  return new Promise(resolveResult => execFile(file, args, { encoding: 'utf8', timeout: timeoutMs, maxBuffer: 32 * 1024, windowsHide: true }, (error, stdout) => {
    resolveResult({ ok: !error, timedOut: !!error?.killed, stdout: typeof stdout === 'string' ? stdout : '' });
  }));
}
export async function findExecutable(name, { env = process.env, platform = process.platform } = {}) {
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(name)) return null;
  const separator = platform === 'win32' ? ';' : delimiter;
  const extensions = platform === 'win32' ? (env.PATHEXT || '.EXE;.CMD;.BAT').split(';') : [''];
  for (const folder of (env.PATH || '').split(separator).filter(Boolean)) {
    for (const extension of extensions) {
      const candidate = platform === 'win32' ? win32.join(folder, name + extension.toLowerCase()) : join(folder, name);
      try { const stat = await fs.stat(candidate); await fs.access(candidate, platform === 'win32' ? constants.F_OK : constants.X_OK); if (stat.isFile()) return candidate; } catch {}
    }
  }
  return null;
}
function observedVersion(output) {
  const match = output.match(/\b(?:v)?(\d{1,4}\.\d{1,4}(?:\.\d{1,6})?(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]{1,40})?)\b/);
  return match?.[1] ?? null;
}
function observedEdition(output) {
  const editions = ['Enterprise Desktop', 'Enterprise Terminal Server', 'Enterprise Cloud Server', 'Enterprise Workstation', 'Small Business Server', 'Terminal Server', 'Workstation'];
  return editions.find(edition => output.toLowerCase().includes(edition.toLowerCase())) ?? 'unknown';
}
function noMachinePaths(platform, env) {
  if (platform === 'win32') return [win32.join(env.ProgramFiles || 'C:\\Program Files', 'NoMachine', 'bin', 'nxserver.exe'), win32.join(env.ProgramFiles || 'C:\\Program Files', 'NoMachine', 'bin', 'nxplayer.exe')];
  if (platform === 'darwin') return ['/Applications/NoMachine.app/Contents/Frameworks/bin/nxserver', '/Applications/NoMachine.app/Contents/MacOS/nxplayer'];
  return ['/usr/NX/bin/nxserver', '/usr/NX/bin/nxplayer'];
}
async function existingExecutable(path) { try { const stat = await fs.stat(path); await fs.access(path, constants.X_OK); return stat.isFile(); } catch { return false; } }

export function validateOllamaURL(value) {
  let url; try { url = new URL(value); } catch { fail('Ollama discovery requires an explicit loopback HTTP(S) origin.'); }
  if (!['http:', 'https:'].includes(url.protocol) || !['localhost', '127.0.0.1', '[::1]'].includes(url.hostname) || url.username || url.password || url.search || url.hash || url.pathname !== '/') {
    fail('Ollama discovery only accepts a loopback origin, without credentials, query, or path.');
  }
  return new URL('/api/tags', url).href;
}
async function ollamaModels(value, fetcher) {
  const url = validateOllamaURL(value);
  try {
    const response = await fetcher(url, { method: 'GET', headers: { Accept: 'application/json' }, redirect: 'error', credentials: 'omit', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2000) });
    if (!response.ok || !(response.headers.get('content-type') || '').includes('application/json')) return { status: 'unavailable', models: [] };
    const chunks = []; let size = 0;
    for await (const chunk of response.body) { size += chunk.byteLength; if (size > 256 * 1024) fail('Model inventory response too large.'); chunks.push(Buffer.from(chunk)); }
    const data = JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8'));
    if (!Array.isArray(data.models) || data.models.length > 256) return { status: 'invalid-response', models: [] };
    const names = data.models.map(model => model?.name).filter(name => typeof name === 'string' && name.length > 0 && name.length <= 200 && !/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/.test(name));
    return { status: 'inventory-read', models: [...new Set(names)].sort(), inferenceVerified: false };
  } catch { return { status: 'unavailable', models: [], inferenceVerified: false }; }
}

/** Only fixed --version and local service-status commands; raw command output is never returned. */
export async function discover(options = {}, dependencies = {}) {
  const locate = dependencies.findExecutable || (name => findExecutable(name));
  const runner = dependencies.runFile || runFile;
  const isExecutable = dependencies.existingExecutable || existingExecutable;
  const platform = options.platform || process.platform;
  const env = options.env || process.env;
  const extra = options.extraCommands || [];
  if (!Array.isArray(extra) || extra.length > 8 || extra.some(name => !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,40}$/.test(name))) fail('Additional commands must be a bounded list of simple executable names.');
  if (options.reportedNoMachineLicenses !== undefined && (!Number.isSafeInteger(options.reportedNoMachineLicenses) || options.reportedNoMachineLicenses < 0 || options.reportedNoMachineLicenses > 1000)) fail('Reported license count is invalid.');
  const tools = [];
  for (const name of [...new Set([...TOOL_NAMES, ...extra])]) {
    const executable = await locate(name);
    if (!executable) { tools.push({ id: name, installed: false, version: null, observation: 'not-found-on-path', authentication: 'not-checked', connected: false }); continue; }
    const result = await runner(executable, ['--version']);
    tools.push({ id: name, installed: true, executable, version: observedVersion(result.stdout), observation: result.ok ? 'version-command-completed' : result.timedOut ? 'version-check-timeout' : 'version-unavailable', authentication: 'not-checked', connected: false });
  }
  const components = [];
  const seen = new Set();
  for (const path of [await locate('nxserver'), await locate('nxplayer'), ...noMachinePaths(platform, env)].filter(Boolean)) {
    if (seen.has(path) || !await isExecutable(path)) continue;
    seen.add(path);
    const result = await runner(path, ['--version']);
    components.push({ executable: path, component: /nxplayer/i.test(path) ? 'player' : 'server', version: observedVersion(result.stdout), edition: observedEdition(result.stdout), observation: result.ok ? 'version-command-completed' : 'version-unavailable' });
  }
  let serviceStatus = 'unknown';
  if (platform === 'linux' && components.some(component => component.component === 'server')) {
    const systemctl = await locate('systemctl');
    if (systemctl) { const status = await runner(systemctl, ['is-active', 'nxserver.service']); const value = status.stdout.trim(); if (['active', 'inactive', 'failed', 'activating', 'deactivating'].includes(value)) serviceStatus = value; }
  }
  return {
    schema: 'omi.node-roster/1', observedAt: new Date().toISOString(), host: { name: options.hostname || os.hostname(), platform, nodeVersion: process.versions.node },
    tools,
    localModels: options.ollamaURL ? await ollamaModels(options.ollamaURL, dependencies.fetch || fetch) : { status: 'not-requested', models: [], inferenceVerified: false },
    noMachine: { installed: components.length > 0, components, serviceStatus, role: 'operator-access', licenseVerification: 'not-performed', ...(options.reportedNoMachineLicenses !== undefined ? { ownerReportedLicenseCount: options.reportedNoMachineLicenses } : {}), ...(options.reportedNoMachineEdition ? { ownerReportedEdition: String(options.reportedNoMachineEdition).slice(0, 100) } : {}), ...(options.reportedNoMachineValidThrough ? { ownerReportedValidThrough: String(options.reportedNoMachineValidThrough).slice(0, 40) } : {}) },
    resourceSharing: 'off', connectionVerification: 'not-performed',
  };
}

async function privateDirectory(path) {
  await fs.mkdir(path, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const stat = await fs.lstat(path);
  if (!stat.isDirectory() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || (process.getuid && (stat.uid !== process.getuid() || (stat.mode & 0o077) !== 0))) fail('Use an owner-only real directory for the node prefix and state.');
  return fs.realpath(path);
}
async function regularJSON(path, optional = false) {
  let stat; try { stat = await fs.lstat(path); } catch (error) { if (optional && error.code === 'ENOENT') return undefined; throw error; }
  if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || stat.size > 256 * 1024 || (process.getuid && (stat.uid !== process.getuid() || (stat.mode & 0o077) !== 0))) fail('Node metadata must use bounded owner-private regular files.');
  return JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(path, 'utf8'));
}
async function syncDirectory(path) { const handle = await fs.open(path, 'r').catch(error => { if (process.platform === 'win32') return null; throw error; }); if (handle) { try { await handle.sync().catch(error => { if (process.platform !== 'win32') throw error; }); } finally { await handle.close(); } } }
async function writeJSON(path, value, exclusive = false) {
  if (!exclusive) await regularJSON(path, true);
  const temporary = path + '.' + randomUUID() + '.tmp';
  const handle = await fs.open(temporary, 'wx', 0o600);
  try { await handle.writeFile(json(value)); await handle.sync(); } finally { await handle.close(); }
  try { if (exclusive) { await fs.link(temporary, path); await fs.unlink(temporary); } else await fs.rename(temporary, path); await syncDirectory(dirname(path)); }
  finally { await fs.unlink(temporary).catch(error => { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; }); }
}
function prefixPath(value, root) {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !isAbsolute(value)) fail('Supply --prefix with an absolute private path.');
  const path = resolve(value);
  if (inside(path, root) || inside(root, path)) fail('The node prefix must be outside the source checkout, not its ancestor.');
  return path;
}
function seatIds(roster, explicit) {
  const nodeName = roster.host.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g, '-').replace(/^[^a-z0-9]+/, '').slice(0, 45) || 'node';
  const ids = explicit ?? roster.tools.filter(tool => tool.installed && !PROVIDERS.has(tool.id)).map(tool => `${tool.id}-${nodeName}`.slice(0, 100));
  if (!Array.isArray(ids) || ids.length > 32 || ids.some(id => !SLUG.test(id) || RESERVED.test(id)) || new Set(ids.map(id => id.toLowerCase())).size !== ids.length) fail('Supply at most 32 unique portable agent IDs.');
  return ids.length ? ids : [`unconfigured-${nodeName}`];
}
async function runtimeFiles(root) {
  const files = ['node/install.sh', 'node/nodectl.sh', 'node/discover.mjs', 'node/diagnose.sh', 'node/diagnose.mjs', 'node/README.md'];
  const names = await fs.readdir(join(root, 'relay'));
  for (const name of names.sort()) if ((name.endsWith('.mjs') && !name.endsWith('.test.mjs')) || name.endsWith('.md')) files.push('relay/' + name);
  const result = [];
  for (const name of files.sort()) {
    const path = join(root, name); const stat = await fs.lstat(path);
    if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || stat.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) fail('Runtime source must contain bounded regular files.');
    const bytes = await fs.readFile(path); result.push({ path: name, bytes, sha256: digest(bytes) });
  }
  for (const required of ['relay/station.mjs', 'relay/git-worker.mjs', 'relay/advisory.mjs', 'relay/mailroom.mjs']) if (!result.some(file => file.path === required)) fail('The bundle lacks a required relay module.');
  return result;
}

/** Installs code into immutable version directories; existing station config and queues are preserved. */
export async function installNode(options, dependencies = {}) {
  const root = await fs.realpath(options.sourceRoot || SOURCE_ROOT);
  const prefix = prefixPath(options.prefix, root);
  const temporaryPrefix = inside(prefix, '/tmp') || inside(prefix, '/var/tmp') || inside(prefix, '/run') || inside(prefix, '/dev/shm');
  if (temporaryPrefix && !options.allowTemporaryPrefix && !options.check && !options.dryRun) fail('Use a persistent home or data directory, not temporary storage. Simulations may explicitly pass --allow-temporary-prefix.');
  const nodeMajor = Number((options.nodeVersion || process.versions.node).split('.')[0]);
  if (!Number.isInteger(nodeMajor) || nodeMajor < 20) fail('Node 20 or newer is required for the agent kit; Node 24 is recommended and required for the SQLite forum.');
  const git = await (dependencies.findExecutable || findExecutable)('git');
  if (!git) fail('Git is required. On Ubuntu, install Git using the operating system package manager, then rerun.');
  const roster = await (dependencies.discover || discover)(options);
  const ids = seatIds(roster, options.agents);
  const source = await runtimeFiles(root);
  const runtimeHash = digest(JSON.stringify(source.map(file => [file.path, file.sha256])));
  if (options.check || options.dryRun) return { status: options.check ? 'check-complete' : 'dry-run', prefix, requiredNode: '20+', recommendedNode: '24+', gitAvailable: true, runtimeHash, candidateSeats: ids, storageWarning: temporaryPrefix ? 'temporary-prefix-not-persistent' : null, writesPerformed: false, roster };
  const canonical = await privateDirectory(prefix);
  if (inside(canonical, root) || inside(root, canonical)) fail('The resolved prefix overlaps the source checkout.');
  for (const name of ['runtime', 'state', 'rosters', 'logs']) await privateDirectory(join(canonical, name));
  const oldInstallation = await regularJSON(join(canonical, 'installation.json'), true);
  const oldProcess = await regularJSON(join(canonical, 'station-process.json'), true);
  if (oldInstallation && oldInstallation.runtimeHash !== runtimeHash && Number.isSafeInteger(oldProcess?.pid) && oldProcess.pid > 1 && processExists(oldProcess.pid)) fail('Stop the existing station with its current controller before installing a newer runtime. Existing state was preserved.');
  const versionDir = join(canonical, 'runtime', runtimeHash);
  let present = false;
  try { const stat = await fs.lstat(versionDir); if (!stat.isDirectory() || stat.isSymbolicLink()) fail('Installed runtime path is unsafe.'); present = true; } catch (error) { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; }
  const manifest = { schema: 'omi.node-runtime/1', runtimeHash, files: source.map(({ path, sha256 }) => ({ path, sha256 })) };
  if (present) {
    for (const file of source) { const target = join(versionDir, file.path); const stat = await fs.lstat(target); if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || digest(await fs.readFile(target)) !== file.sha256) fail('Existing runtime integrity check failed.'); }
  } else {
    const staging = join(canonical, 'runtime', '.staging-' + randomUUID()); await privateDirectory(staging);
    try {
      for (const file of source) { await fs.mkdir(dirname(join(staging, file.path)), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 }); await fs.writeFile(join(staging, file.path), file.bytes, { mode: file.path.endsWith('.sh') ? 0o700 : 0o600, flag: 'wx' }); }
      await writeJSON(join(staging, 'manifest.json'), manifest, true); await fs.rename(staging, versionDir); await syncDirectory(dirname(versionDir));
    } finally { await fs.rm(staging, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  }
  const stationPath = join(canonical, 'station.json');
  const existing = await regularJSON(stationPath, true);
  if (existing === undefined) {
    await writeJSON(stationPath, { remote: 'https://github.com/nighteyes-neversleep/kaos_labs.git', branch: 'omi-relay', directory: join(canonical, 'state', 'station'), participants: ids.map(id => ({ id, enabled: false })), intervalMs: 15000, concurrency: 2, timeoutMs: 30000 }, true);
  }
  const rosterPath = join(canonical, 'rosters', new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-') + '-' + randomUUID() + '.json');
  await writeJSON(rosterPath, roster, true);
  const installation = { schema: 'omi.node-installation/1', runtimeHash, runtimeRoot: versionDir, stationConfig: stationPath, rosterPath, installedAt: new Date().toISOString(), candidateSeats: ids, stationConfigStatus: existing === undefined ? 'created-disabled' : 'preserved-existing' };
  await writeJSON(join(canonical, 'installation.json'), installation);
  return { status: 'installed', prefix: canonical, runtimeHash, runtimeRoot: versionDir, stationConfig: stationPath, rosterPath, stationConfigStatus: installation.stationConfigStatus, enabledByInstaller: 0, resourceSharing: 'off' };
}

function processExists(pid) { try { process.kill(pid, 0); return true; } catch (error) { if (error.code === 'ESRCH') return false; throw error; } }
export function parseProcessCommand(command) {
  // Parses the quoting used by Node's own spawn on POSIX/Windows ps output.
  const args = []; let current = ''; let quote = null;
  for (let index = 0; index < command.length; index++) {
    const char = command[index];
    if (quote) { if (char === quote) quote = null; else current += char; }
    else if (char === '"' || char === "'") quote = char;
    else if (/\s/.test(char)) { if (current) { args.push(current); current = ''; } }
    else current += char;
  }
  if (current) args.push(current);
  return args;
}
async function processArguments(pid) {
  if (process.platform === 'linux') {
    try { return (await fs.readFile(`/proc/${pid}/cmdline`, 'utf8')).split('\0').filter(Boolean); } catch { return null; }
  }
  const result = process.platform === 'win32'
    ? await runFile('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-NonInteractive', '-Command', `(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter "ProcessId=${pid}").CommandLine`])
    : await runFile('/bin/ps', ['-ww', '-p', String(pid), '-o', 'command=']);
  return result.ok ? parseProcessCommand(result.stdout.trim()) : null;
}
export function matchesOwnedArguments(args, expected) {
  if (!Array.isArray(args) || args.length !== 4 || args[2] !== '--config') return false;
  const norm = value => process.platform === 'win32' ? resolve(value).toLowerCase() : resolve(value);
  return norm(args[0]) === norm(expected.node) && norm(args[1]) === norm(expected.script) && norm(args[3]) === norm(expected.config);
}
async function installed(prefix) {
  if (typeof prefix !== 'string' || !isAbsolute(prefix)) fail('Supply an absolute installed node prefix.');
  const path = resolve(prefix);
  const prefixStat = await fs.lstat(path);
  if (!prefixStat.isDirectory() || prefixStat.isSymbolicLink() || (process.getuid && (prefixStat.uid !== process.getuid() || (prefixStat.mode & 0o077) !== 0))) fail('The installed prefix must be an existing owner-private directory.');
  if (await fs.realpath(path) !== path) fail('Installed prefix parent links are not supported.');
  const data = await regularJSON(join(path, 'installation.json'));
  if (data?.schema !== 'omi.node-installation/1' || !HASH.test(data.runtimeHash || '') || data.runtimeRoot !== join(path, 'runtime', data.runtimeHash) || data.stationConfig !== join(path, 'station.json')) fail('Installed node metadata is invalid.');
  for (const directory of [join(path, 'runtime'), data.runtimeRoot, join(data.runtimeRoot, 'node'), join(data.runtimeRoot, 'relay')]) { const stat = await fs.lstat(directory); if (!stat.isDirectory() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || (process.getuid && (stat.uid !== process.getuid() || (stat.mode & 0o077) !== 0))) fail('Runtime directories must be real and owner-private.'); }
  const manifest = await regularJSON(join(data.runtimeRoot, 'manifest.json'));
  if (manifest?.schema !== 'omi.node-runtime/1' || manifest.runtimeHash !== data.runtimeHash || !Array.isArray(manifest.files) || manifest.files.length > 256 || digest(JSON.stringify(manifest.files.map(file => [file.path, file.sha256]))) !== data.runtimeHash) fail('Runtime manifest is invalid.');
  if (!manifest.files.some(file => file.path === 'relay/station.mjs')) fail('The station runtime is missing.');
  for (const file of manifest.files) {
    if (!/^((?:relay|node)\/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+)$/.test(file.path) || !HASH.test(file.sha256 || '')) fail('Runtime manifest path is invalid.');
    const target = join(data.runtimeRoot, file.path); const stat = await fs.lstat(target);
    if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || digest(await fs.readFile(target)) !== file.sha256) fail('Installed runtime integrity check failed.');
  }
  return { prefix: path, data, expected: { node: process.execPath, script: join(data.runtimeRoot, 'relay', 'station.mjs'), config: data.stationConfig } };
}

function sameExpected(recorded, expected) { return recorded && recorded.node === expected.node && recorded.script === expected.script && recorded.config === expected.config; }

export async function controlNode(action, prefix) {
  if (!['status', 'start', 'stop'].includes(action)) fail('Choose status, start, or stop.');
  const setup = await installed(prefix);
  const pidPath = join(setup.prefix, 'station-process.json');
  let record = await regularJSON(pidPath, true);
  const existing = record && Number.isSafeInteger(record.pid) && record.pid > 1 && processExists(record.pid);
  if (existing && (!sameExpected(record.expected, setup.expected) || !matchesOwnedArguments(await processArguments(record.pid), setup.expected))) fail('The recorded PID is not this installed station; no signal was sent.');
  if (action === 'status') return { status: existing ? 'running' : 'stopped', pid: existing ? record.pid : null, connectionVerification: 'not-performed', stationConfig: setup.data.stationConfig };
  const lockPath = join(setup.prefix, 'node-control.lock');
  let lock;
  try { lock = await fs.open(lockPath, 'wx', 0o600); await lock.writeFile(json({ pid: process.pid })); await lock.sync(); }
  catch { fail('Another node controller may be active. Inspect the private lock before retrying.'); }
  try {
    // Recheck within the mutation lock to avoid duplicate starts.
    record = await regularJSON(pidPath, true);
    const running = record && Number.isSafeInteger(record.pid) && record.pid > 1 && processExists(record.pid);
    if (running && (!sameExpected(record.expected, setup.expected) || !matchesOwnedArguments(await processArguments(record.pid), setup.expected))) fail('The recorded PID is not this installed station; no signal was sent.');
    if (action === 'stop') {
      if (!running) return { status: 'already-stopped', pid: null };
      process.kill(record.pid, 'SIGTERM');
      for (let i = 0; i < 40 && processExists(record.pid); i++) await delay(100);
      if (processExists(record.pid)) return { status: 'stop-requested', pid: record.pid, detail: 'Waiting for a bounded active Git operation; no forced kill was sent.' };
      await fs.unlink(pidPath); return { status: 'stopped', pid: record.pid };
    }
    if (running) return { status: 'already-running', pid: record.pid };
    const stationModule = await import(pathToFileURL(setup.expected.script).href);
    const config = await stationModule.loadStationConfig(setup.data.stationConfig);
    if (!config.participants.some(seat => seat.enabled)) fail('All seats are disabled. Configure actual adapters/identities and enable the chosen seats before starting.');
    const logPath = join(setup.prefix, 'logs', 'station.log');
    try { const logStat = await fs.lstat(logPath); if (!logStat.isFile() || logStat.isSymbolicLink() || (process.getuid && (logStat.uid !== process.getuid() || (logStat.mode & 0o077) !== 0))) fail('The station log must be owner-private.'); } catch (error) { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; }
    const log = await fs.open(logPath, constants.O_WRONLY | constants.O_APPEND | constants.O_CREAT | constants.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600);
    const child = spawn(process.execPath, [setup.expected.script, '--config', setup.expected.config], { detached: true, stdio: ['ignore', log.fd, log.fd], windowsHide: true });
    await log.close();
    const earlyError = new Promise(resolveResult => { child.once('error', () => resolveResult(true)); child.once('exit', () => resolveResult(true)); });
    const died = await Promise.race([earlyError, delay(400).then(() => false)]);
    try {
      if (died || !child.pid || !matchesOwnedArguments(await processArguments(child.pid), setup.expected)) fail('The station did not start successfully; inspect its private log.');
      await writeJSON(pidPath, { schema: 'omi.node-process/1', pid: child.pid, expected: setup.expected, startedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    } catch (error) {
      if (child.pid && matchesOwnedArguments(await processArguments(child.pid), setup.expected)) child.kill('SIGTERM');
      child.unref(); throw error;
    }
    child.unref();
    return { status: 'started', pid: child.pid, logPath, connectionVerification: 'not-performed' };
  } finally { await lock?.close(); await fs.unlink(lockPath).catch(() => {}); }
}

function options(argv) {
  const result = { extraCommands: [] };
  for (let index = 0; index < argv.length; index++) {
    const name = argv[index];
    if (name === '--allow-temporary-prefix') result.allowTemporaryPrefix = true;
    else if (['--check', '--dry-run'].includes(name)) result[name === '--check' ? 'check' : 'dryRun'] = true;
    else if (['--prefix', '--agents', '--ollama-url', '--output', '--extra-command', '--reported-nomachine-licenses', '--reported-nomachine-edition', '--reported-nomachine-valid-through'].includes(name)) {
      const value = argv[++index]; if (!value || value.startsWith('--')) fail(`Missing value for ${name}.`);
      if (name === '--prefix') result.prefix = value;
      if (name === '--agents') result.agents = value.split(',').filter(Boolean);
      if (name === '--ollama-url') result.ollamaURL = value;
      if (name === '--output') result.output = value;
      if (name === '--extra-command') result.extraCommands.push(value);
      if (name === '--reported-nomachine-licenses') result.reportedNoMachineLicenses = Number(value);
      if (name === '--reported-nomachine-edition') result.reportedNoMachineEdition = value;
      if (name === '--reported-nomachine-valid-through') result.reportedNoMachineValidThrough = value;
    } else fail('Unsupported argument. Use --help for the supported node commands.');
  }
  return result;
}
export async function main(argv = process.argv.slice(2)) {
  try {
    if (argv.includes('--help')) {
      console.log('Inventory: node node/discover.mjs [--output /private/roster.json] [--ollama-url http://127.0.0.1:11434]\nInstall: bash node/install.sh --prefix /private/omi-node [--check|--dry-run] [--agents id,...]\nControl: bash node/nodectl.sh status|start|stop --prefix /private/omi-node\nDiscovery never logs in, starts models, reads licenses, or verifies remote seat connectivity.'); return 0;
    }
    if (argv[0] === '--control') { const action = argv[1]; const parsed = options(argv.slice(2)); console.log(json(await controlNode(action, parsed.prefix))); return 0; }
    const installing = argv[0] === '--install'; const parsed = options(installing ? argv.slice(1) : argv);
    const result = installing ? await installNode(parsed) : await discover(parsed);
    if (parsed.output) {
      if (!isAbsolute(parsed.output)) fail('Use an absolute private roster output path.');
      await privateDirectory(dirname(parsed.output)); await writeJSON(parsed.output, result, true);
      console.log(json({ status: 'inventory-written', path: parsed.output }));
    } else console.log(json(result));
    return 0;
  } catch (error) { console.error(JSON.stringify({ event: 'node-operation-failed', message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Node operation failed.' })); return 1; }
}
if (process.argv[1] && resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) process.exitCode = await main();

test('inventory uses bounded version/status calls, never claims authentication or leaks raw command output', async () => {
  const calls = [];
  const roster = await discover({ reportedNoMachineLicenses: 5, reportedNoMachineEdition: 'Enterprise Desktop', reportedNoMachineValidThrough: '2027-10-22' }, {
    findExecutable: async name => ['claude', 'nxserver', 'systemctl'].includes(name) ? '/bin/' + name : null,
    existingExecutable: async path => path === '/bin/nxserver',
    runFile: async (file, args) => { calls.push([file, args]); return { ok: true, stdout: file.endsWith('systemctl') ? 'active\n' : file.endsWith('nxserver') ? 'NoMachine Enterprise Desktop 9.4.2 secret=PRIVATE' : 'Claude 2.1.0 token=PRIVATE' }; },
    fetch: async () => assert.fail('Default discovery must not make HTTP requests'),
  });
  assert.equal(roster.tools.find(tool => tool.id === 'claude').version, '2.1.0');
  assert.equal(roster.tools.find(tool => tool.id === 'claude').authentication, 'not-checked');
  assert.ok(roster.tools.every(tool => tool.connected === false));
  assert.equal(roster.noMachine.components[0].edition, 'Enterprise Desktop');
  assert.equal(roster.noMachine.serviceStatus, 'active');
  assert.equal(roster.noMachine.ownerReportedLicenseCount, 5);
  assert.equal(roster.noMachine.licenseVerification, 'not-performed');
  assert.equal(roster.localModels.status, 'not-requested');
  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(roster).includes('PRIVATE'), false);
  assert.deepEqual(calls.map(value => value[1]), [['--version'], ['--version'], ['is-active', 'nxserver.service']]);
});

test('controller performs actual owned child lifecycle, rejects foreign PID, and refuses runtime tampering', async t => {
  const setup = await fixture(t); const installed = await installNode(setup.options, dependencies);

  assert.equal(started.status, 'started'); assert.equal((await controlNode('status', setup.prefix)).status, 'running');
  assert.equal((await controlNode('start', setup.prefix)).status, 'already-running');

  assert.equal((await controlNode('stop', setup.prefix)).status, 'stopped');
  assert.equal((await controlNode('status', setup.prefix)).status, 'stopped');

  await assert.rejects(controlNode('stop', setup.prefix), /no signal was sent/);

  await assert.rejects(controlNode('start', setup.prefix), /integrity check failed/);
});

test('diagnostics default is read-only, optional canary compares boots/home/state without deleting files', async t => {
  const setup = await fixture(t); await fs.mkdir(setup.prefix, { mode: 0o700 });
  const deps = { bootId: 'boot-one', serviceState: async () => 'active' };

  assert.equal(before.persistence.writesPerformed, false); assert.deepEqual(await fs.readdir(setup.prefix), []);
  assert.equal(before.noMachine.licenseVerification, 'not-performed'); assert.equal(JSON.stringify(before).includes(setup.directory), false);
  const baseline = await diagnose({ prefix: setup.prefix, home: setup.directory, record: true }, deps);
  assert.equal(baseline.persistence.status, 'baseline-recorded');
  const again = await diagnose({ prefix: setup.prefix, home: setup.directory, record: true }, deps);
  assert.equal(again.persistence.canaryMatchesPrevious, true); assert.equal(again.persistence.sameBoot, true); assert.equal(again.persistence.sameHomeInode, true);
  const rebooted = await diagnose({ prefix: setup.prefix, home: setup.directory, record: true }, { ...deps, bootId: 'boot-two' });
  assert.equal(rebooted.persistence.sameBoot, false); assert.equal(rebooted.persistence.canaryMatchesPrevious, true);
  await fs.rename(join(setup.prefix, 'state', 'diagnostics', 'canary.json'), join(setup.prefix, 'state', 'diagnostics', 'saved-canary.json'));
  const replaced = await diagnose({ prefix: setup.prefix, home: setup.directory, record: true }, deps);
  assert.equal(replaced.persistence.canaryPresentBeforeRun, false); assert.equal(replaced.persistence.canaryMatchesPrevious, false);
  assert.equal((await fs.stat(join(setup.prefix, 'state', 'diagnostics', 'saved-canary.json'))).isFile(), true);
});

===== BEGIN FILE node/README.md | SHA256 728e5bcc075b6e524ffd259a97921767732a9c319180dfd6a02bd0ee29231a1c | BYTES 5443 =====
# OMI node kit: Ubuntu, Bash, and private state

Use a Bash terminal on each Ubuntu Desktop computer. The kit needs Git and Node.js 20 or newer; Node 24 LTS is recommended. The separate SQLite forum backend needs Node 24. This installer does not replace an existing Node installation or install global AI clients. If a prerequisite is absent, it explains what to install. No browser is needed for discovery, installation, or node control.

```bash
bash node/install.sh --prefix "$HOME/.local/share/omi-node" --check
bash node/install.sh --prefix "$HOME/.local/share/omi-node" --dry-run
bash node/install.sh --prefix "$HOME/.local/share/omi-node" --agents claude-station,codex-station,deepseek-station
bash node/nodectl.sh status --prefix "$HOME/.local/share/omi-node"
```

Run these commands from the extracted kit or the source repository. The installer creates owner-only directories and files under the absolute prefix. Keep this prefix on persistent storage outside the checkout. Temporary paths under `/tmp`, `/var/tmp`, `/run`, or `/dev/shm` are refused for installation unless `--allow-temporary-prefix` is explicitly supplied for testing. Checks and dry runs make no changes. They include a temporary-storage warning when relevant. An overlay filesystem can also be ephemeral; use the diagnostics below to investigate it.

The installation saves code in immutable, hash-verified runtime directories, a new roster snapshot, and an installer-owned `installation.json` pointer. It creates `station.json` only when absent. Every newly created participant is disabled. Reinstallation preserves an existing station configuration, queues, cursors, credentials, and prior roster snapshots. It never copies login files, scans your home directory, enables resource sharing, or starts a model.

Discovery checks only known executable names on `PATH`, standard NoMachine paths, bounded `--version` commands, and the NoMachine service state. Installed clients are candidates for the roster; authentication, model availability, and connection to the team remain unverified. Ollama model names can be inventoried only when you request a loopback endpoint:

```bash
node node/discover.mjs --ollama-url http://127.0.0.1:11434
```

No remote model endpoint is probed. An installed model runner and a downloaded model do not prove successful inference. Additional simple executable names can be checked with repeated `--extra-command NAME` flags, up to eight.

Configure each real participant's identity, addresses, local adapter, and existing Git credential helper using `relay/STATION.md`, `relay/MAILROOM.md`, and the bundle's adapter guide. Never put tokens in the station config or remote URL. Enable the selected seat in the private `station.json` only after that participant is ready. Then:

```bash
bash node/nodectl.sh start --prefix "$HOME/.local/share/omi-node"
bash node/nodectl.sh status --prefix "$HOME/.local/share/omi-node"
bash node/nodectl.sh stop --prefix "$HOME/.local/share/omi-node"
```

These commands control the headless mail station, not the models. Start refuses an all-disabled roster. The controller records its child PID and exact Node/script/config arguments, verifies the runtime hashes, and refuses to signal a process whose identity does not match. It sends a graceful stop without a forced kill. A process marked running has not thereby completed a remote send/receive test. Logs stay owner-private and contain station metadata; message contents and tokens are not logged by this kit. The controller deliberately refuses an ambiguous stale control lock rather than guessing who owns it. Stop a running station before installing a newer runtime.

The Bash launchers also work on macOS and WSL when Git and Node are available. Ubuntu is the tested target. Native Windows `.cmd` client version checks may report unavailable; use Ubuntu/WSL for this release. NoMachine is optional remote operator access, separate from mail transport and model adapters. Discovery does not activate licenses, read license files, install NoMachine, or expose a desktop to the network. Owner-reported entitlements can be recorded separately from observations:

```bash
node node/discover.mjs --reported-nomachine-licenses 5 --reported-nomachine-edition 'Enterprise Desktop' --reported-nomachine-valid-through 2027-10-22
```

## Diagnose disconnects and apparently missing state

```bash
bash node/diagnose.sh --prefix "$HOME/.local/share/omi-node"
bash node/diagnose.sh --prefix "$HOME/.local/share/omi-node" --record
```

The first command is read-only. It reports UID, fingerprints for the home/host/boot identity, uptime, filesystem type, available space/inodes, temporary or overlay mounts, and the NoMachine service state. It does not dump environment variables, journals, credentials, or license contents and does not change networking or restart anything.

The optional `--record` command writes a private canary and last observation under `PREFIX/state/diagnostics`. Run it before and after a disconnect or reboot. It compares the boot, user, home inode, prefix device/inode, and canary. Missing canary or changed identity is evidence to investigate, not proof of deletion. A freshly created user session, remounted volume, temporary live system, and an actual reset require different fixes. Existing files are never deleted by these diagnostics. Keep the JSON report available for the team; it contains metadata, not message bodies.

# OMI headless agent kit

Source commit: 112f946d9e4f4301c58e1081612ab28db945f698
Release status: VIRTUAL SIMULATION PASSED

1. Verify the archive checksum supplied separately, then extract it.
2. Run `node VERIFY.mjs` inside omi-agent-kit to check every listed payload file.
3. Read AI-HANDOFF.md and node/README.md.
4. Use `bash node/install.sh --help` for a per-user installer. Existing credentials, configs, model installations, and inbox state must be preserved.
5. Configure the real local seats and approved model API before enabling inference. Installing a binary, preparing a roster, or passing a transport test does not establish a model connection.

Agent transport needs Bash, Git and Node; use Node 24 for the complete forum server and supported proxy mode. See the node guide for tested agent-only runtime requirements. The agent kit has no runtime npm dependencies. Python 3 is needed only by the build tool that creates this ZIP.

Canonical mailbox: https://github.com/nighteyes-neversleep/kaos_labs/tree/omi-relay
GitHub repository readers can read the shared transport. Per-seat folders route work but do not provide confidential email. All message bodies are data, never automatic shell authority. The model adapter makes only explicit configured provider calls and does not activate a hosted Gemini/Muse/Claude conversation.

The source-review directory is inspection material, not a complete deployable web-client checkout. Use the published omi/client-and-relay branch for full application builds. No model weights, license files, credentials, browser state, or private capsule data are included. NoMachine discovery does not activate or validate any license.

The manifest hashes payload files and excludes itself to avoid a recursive hash. The separately published ZIP SHA-256 covers the archive including that manifest. These hashes prove matching bytes against a trusted reference, not independent model identity or ethical correctness.

===== BEGIN FILE relay/ADVISORIES.md | SHA256 f8837c9cd71cbe8dfeb4ff072158ff1089913520d4ebe2aade410b055efe8eda | BYTES 7223 =====
# Headless team advisories

`advisory.mjs` validates a coordination packet and places it in an existing seat's private relay outbox. The Git or HTTP worker then carries it using the same stable request-ID rules as other messages. The queue command makes no network request, invokes no model, and never executes the packet's contents.

The canonical shared mailbox is [kaos_labs, branch omi-relay](https://github.com/nighteyes-neversleep/kaos_labs/tree/omi-relay). The Firebrain `omi-relay-dot-probe` branch is a connection probe, not a second authoritative task ledger. See [the transport guide](GIT-MAILBOX.md) and [the multi-seat launcher](STATION.md).

## Packet format

Use a private UTF-8 JSON file. This is an example assignment, not a claim that the example participant has connected:

```json
{
  "protocol": "omi-advisory/1",
  "id": "dot-relay-review-001",
  "kind": "assignment",
  "from": "dot",
  "to": ["review-seat"],
  "taskId": "QA-RELAY-001",
  "summary": "Review duplicate-delivery recovery and return reproducible findings.",
  "details": {
    "sourceRevision": "replace-with-the-exact-reviewed-commit",
    "ownedPaths": ["relay/git-worker.mjs"],
    "doneWhen": [
      "Acknowledge the assignment and actual interface used.",
      "Separate reproduced bugs from unverified risks.",
      "Report only tests that were actually run."
    ]
  },
  "createdAt": "2026-11-02T20:00:00.000Z",
  "requiresAck": true
}
```

Required fields are `protocol`, `id`, `kind`, `from`, `to`, `summary`, `details`, `createdAt`, and `requiresAck`. `taskId` is optional. Unknown top-level fields are refused.

- `protocol` is exactly `omi-advisory/1`.
- `kind` is `plan`, `assignment`, `progress`, `blocker`, `result`, or `ack`.
- `id`, `from`, `taskId`, and recipient IDs are 1–100 ASCII letters, digits, `_`, or `-`, beginning with a letter or digit. The `id` is the stable retry key; do not reuse it for changed content.
- `to` contains 1–32 recipient IDs, unique even after case folding.
- `summary` contains 1–600 readable characters. It keeps human-facing context available when the details use compact machine-readable data.
- `details` contains JSON data, including `null` if there are no details. Functions, getters, cycles, sparse arrays, nonfinite numbers, and non-JSON objects are refused. Structural bounds are 24 nested levels and 4,096 visited values.
- `createdAt` is a canonical ISO timestamp, including milliseconds and timezone, as produced by `new Date().toISOString()`.
- `requiresAck` is a boolean. It requests a response; it does not manufacture an acknowledgement.

The complete serialized advisory must fit the existing transport's 12,000-character body limit. Details are copied and object keys sorted before serialization, so an equivalent JSON key order does not create a conflicting retry.

## Queue without a browser

Use the outbox already prepared by the station or one-seat worker. Both the outbox and advisory input below are example paths:

```sh
node relay/advisory.mjs \
  --outbox /private/station/dot/outbox \
  --file /private/advisories/relay-review.json
```

PowerShell:

```powershell
node relay/advisory.mjs --outbox 'C:\Private\OMI\dot\outbox' --file 'C:\Private\OMI\advisories\relay-review.json'
```

The outbox must be an existing real directory outside the checkout. POSIX ownership and private permissions are checked; use `0700` directories. Windows uses the account's private directory ACL instead of POSIX mode bits. Direct symlink outboxes, symlink files, and paths resolving into the checkout are refused. New files use `0600` where supported. Input files must be bounded regular files containing valid UTF-8 JSON.

Successful console output includes only the event, queue status, advisory ID, and queue file path. It does not print the advisory body. `queued` means the local file is durable; it does not mean a remote participant received it. `already-queued` means an identical request is still present locally.

The resulting transport envelope is:

```text
topicId: coordination-advisory
body: canonical JSON advisory
clientMessageId: advisory.id
```

The tool writes a private temporary non-JSON file, flushes it, renames it into the queue, and flushes the directory where supported. Relay readers ignore the temporary file. A producer lock prevents normal simultaneous writes to the same advisory ID; an abandoned lock is reclaimed only when its recorded process no longer exists. Keep the spool local to one host rather than sharing it as a multi-host filesystem lock service.

An identical queued ID is reused, including when another producer used a different filename. Different content under the same ID fails without replacing the existing item. If the worker has already consumed the file, the tool can queue it again; remote deduplication and the worker's sent archive remain the final authority for that retry. Outbox inspection is bounded to 10,000 entries and 32 MiB.

## Ownership, acknowledgements, and evidence

Use a `plan` to state current work and dependencies. Give each `assignment` one owner and explicit paths or outputs so parallel agents do not silently edit the same component. Send `progress` and `blocker` packets with the affected `taskId`. A `result` should name the exact reviewed revision, changes, executed checks, and remaining limits.

Keep a task awaiting acknowledgement until its participant replies. A useful `ack` records the actual host, interface, source revision, and what the participant can read and write. An acknowledgement relayed by the owner must say so in `details`; it is not proof of a direct model connection. In particular, an owner-relayed Muse acknowledgement or read-only observation does not establish Muse's write access. Never infer that Claude, DeepSeek, Gemini, Muse, or any other external model received work merely because a local queue write or transport cycle succeeded.

Logical sender names are routing metadata under repository access controls. They are not independent cryptographic proof of a model's identity. An advisory is coordination data, not authority to bypass OMI permissions, execute arbitrary commands, share private capsule contents, or change resource-sharing authorization.

The owner approved compact machine-readable inter-agent messages. Keep decisions, evidence, results, and user-facing progress in plain English as well, so the coordination remains auditable and usable without visual output.

## Keep credentials out

Common credential-shaped keys such as `token`, `apiKey`, `password`, `PIN`, `secret`, and `privateKey` are rejected recursively, including common normalized variants. This is a bounded key-name heuristic, not a general secret detector. It cannot recognize every credential hidden in prose or arbitrary code. Do not place credentials, private capsule data, or key material anywhere in an advisory.

## Verify

```sh
node --test relay/advisory.test.mjs
```

The suite exercises schema and size limits, canonical snapshots, ID conflicts, concurrent producers, private paths and symlinks, lock recovery, real CLI input handling, and metadata-only output. It uses temporary local files and synthetic data; it makes no model or remote-service call.

===== END FILE relay/ADVISORIES.md =====

===== BEGIN FILE relay/advisory.mjs | SHA256 203f9e837ca6467deea1cc7f65cb871a0b37b8e7a82070d030c8f340b77831d5 | BYTES 15073 =====
#!/usr/bin/env node
/** Queue validated coordination data. This module never contacts or executes an agent. */
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { constants } from 'node:fs';
import * as fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import { dirname, isAbsolute, relative, resolve, sep } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const CHECKOUT = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const SLUG = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,99}$/;
const KINDS = new Set(['plan', 'assignment', 'progress', 'blocker', 'result', 'ack']);
const FIELDS = new Set(['protocol', 'id', 'kind', 'from', 'to', 'taskId', 'summary', 'details', 'createdAt', 'requiresAck']);
const MAX_BODY = 12_000;
const FILE_LIMIT = 256 * 1024;
const BAD_CONTROLS = /[\u0000-\u0008\u000b\u000c\u000e-\u001f\u007f]/;
const isObject = value => value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
const slug = value => typeof value === 'string' && SLUG.test(value);
const inside = (path, root) => {
  const suffix = relative(root, path);
  return suffix === '' || (!isAbsolute(suffix) && suffix !== '..' && !suffix.startsWith(`..${sep}`));
};

export class AdvisoryError extends Error {
  constructor(code, message) { super(message); this.name = 'AdvisoryError'; this.code = code; }
}
const fail = (code, message) => { throw new AdvisoryError(code, message); };

function credentialKey(key) {
  const normalized = key.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
  return ['token', 'apikey', 'password', 'pin', 'secret', 'privatekey', 'authorization', 'credentials'].includes(normalized)
    || normalized.endsWith('token') || normalized.includes('apikey') || normalized.includes('password')
    || normalized.includes('privatekey') || normalized.endsWith('secret');
}

/** Copies JSON values without coercion, getters, prototypes, or caller mutation. */
function detailsSnapshot(value) {
  const active = new Set();
  let nodes = 0;
  function copy(item, depth) {
    if (++nodes > 4096 || depth > 24) fail('DETAIL_LIMIT', 'Advisory details exceed the supported structural limit.');
    if (item === null || typeof item === 'boolean') return item;
    if (typeof item === 'number') {
      if (!Number.isFinite(item)) fail('INVALID_DETAILS', 'Details must contain finite JSON numbers.');
      return item;
    }
    if (typeof item === 'string') {
      if (item.length > MAX_BODY || BAD_CONTROLS.test(item)) fail('INVALID_DETAILS', 'A detail string is invalid or too long.');
      return item;
    }
    if (typeof item !== 'object') fail('INVALID_DETAILS', 'Details must contain only JSON data.');
    if (active.has(item)) fail('INVALID_DETAILS', 'Details cannot contain cycles.');
    active.add(item);
    let result;
    if (Array.isArray(item)) {
      if (Object.keys(item).length !== item.length || Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(item).length) fail('INVALID_DETAILS', 'Detail arrays cannot contain extra fields.');
      result = [];
      for (let index = 0; index < item.length; index++) {
        const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(item, String(index));
        if (!descriptor || !Object.hasOwn(descriptor, 'value')) fail('INVALID_DETAILS', 'Detail arrays cannot contain holes or getters.');
        result.push(copy(descriptor.value, depth + 1));
      }
    } else {
      if (![Object.prototype, null].includes(Object.getPrototypeOf(item)) || Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(item).length) {
        fail('INVALID_DETAILS', 'Details must use plain JSON objects.');
      }
      const pairs = [];
      for (const key of Object.keys(item).sort()) {
        if (key.length > 200 || BAD_CONTROLS.test(key)) fail('INVALID_DETAILS', 'A detail key is invalid or too long.');
        if (credentialKey(key)) fail('CREDENTIAL_FIELD', 'Credential-shaped fields are not allowed in advisories.');
        const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(item, key);
        if (!descriptor || !Object.hasOwn(descriptor, 'value')) fail('INVALID_DETAILS', 'Details cannot use getters or setters.');
        pairs.push([key, copy(descriptor.value, depth + 1)]);
      }
      result = Object.fromEntries(pairs);
    }
    active.delete(item);
    return result;
  }
  return copy(value, 0);
}

/** Returns a canonical, bounded snapshot suitable for the existing relay body. */
export function validateAdvisory(value) {
  if (!isObject(value) || ![Object.prototype, null].includes(Object.getPrototypeOf(value))) fail('INVALID_ADVISORY', 'An advisory must be a plain JSON object.');
  if (Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(value).length) fail('INVALID_ADVISORY', 'Advisories cannot contain symbol keys.');
  for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
    if (credentialKey(key)) fail('CREDENTIAL_FIELD', 'Credential-shaped fields are not allowed in advisories.');
    if (!FIELDS.has(key)) fail('UNKNOWN_FIELD', 'The advisory contains an unsupported field.');
    const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value, key);
    if (!descriptor || !Object.hasOwn(descriptor, 'value')) fail('INVALID_ADVISORY', 'Advisories cannot use getters or setters.');
  }
  if (['protocol', 'id', 'kind', 'from', 'to', 'summary', 'details', 'createdAt', 'requiresAck'].some(key => !Object.hasOwn(value, key))) {
    fail('INVALID_ADVISORY', 'The advisory is missing a required field.');
  }
  if (value.protocol !== 'omi-advisory/1') fail('INVALID_PROTOCOL', 'Use protocol omi-advisory/1.');
  if (!slug(value.id) || !slug(value.from) || !KINDS.has(value.kind)) fail('INVALID_ADVISORY', 'The advisory ID, sender, or kind is invalid.');
  const recipients = [];
  if (Array.isArray(value.to) && value.to.length >= 1 && value.to.length <= 32 && Object.keys(value.to).length === value.to.length && Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(value.to).length === 0) {
    for (let index = 0; index < value.to.length; index++) {
      const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value.to, String(index));
      if (!descriptor || !Object.hasOwn(descriptor, 'value') || !slug(descriptor.value)) fail('INVALID_RECIPIENTS', 'Supply 1–32 distinct recipient IDs.');
      recipients.push(descriptor.value);
    }
  }
  if (recipients.length < 1 || new Set(recipients.map(id => id.toLowerCase())).size !== recipients.length) {
    fail('INVALID_RECIPIENTS', 'Supply 1–32 distinct recipient IDs.');
  }
  if (Object.hasOwn(value, 'taskId') && !slug(value.taskId)) fail('INVALID_TASK', 'The optional taskId must be a 1–100 character slug.');
  if (typeof value.summary !== 'string' || !value.summary.trim() || value.summary.length > 600 || BAD_CONTROLS.test(value.summary)) {
    fail('INVALID_SUMMARY', 'Supply a readable summary of 1–600 characters.');
  }
  if (typeof value.createdAt !== 'string' || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value.createdAt)) || new Date(value.createdAt).toISOString() !== value.createdAt) {
    fail('INVALID_DATE', 'createdAt must be a canonical ISO timestamp.');
  }
  if (typeof value.requiresAck !== 'boolean' || !Object.hasOwn(value, 'details')) fail('INVALID_ADVISORY', 'Supply details and a boolean requiresAck.');
  const snapshot = {
    protocol: 'omi-advisory/1', id: value.id, kind: value.kind, from: value.from, to: recipients,
    ...(Object.hasOwn(value, 'taskId') ? { taskId: value.taskId } : {}),
    summary: value.summary.trim(), details: detailsSnapshot(value.details), createdAt: value.createdAt, requiresAck: value.requiresAck,
  };
  if (JSON.stringify(snapshot).length > MAX_BODY) fail('BODY_TOO_LARGE', 'The serialized advisory must not exceed 12,000 characters.');
  return snapshot;
}

async function privateOutbox(path) {
  if (typeof path !== 'string' || !isAbsolute(path) || inside(resolve(path), CHECKOUT)) fail('INVALID_OUTBOX', 'Use an absolute private outbox outside the checkout.');
  const lexical = resolve(path);
  let stat;
  try { stat = await fs.lstat(lexical); }
  catch { fail('INVALID_OUTBOX', 'The private outbox directory must already exist.'); }
  if (!stat.isDirectory() || stat.isSymbolicLink()) fail('INVALID_OUTBOX', 'The outbox must be a real directory, without a symbolic link.');
  const canonical = await fs.realpath(lexical);
  const root = await fs.realpath(CHECKOUT);
  if (inside(canonical, root) || (process.getuid && (stat.uid !== process.getuid() || (stat.mode & 0o077) !== 0))) {
    fail('INVALID_OUTBOX', 'Use an owner-private outbox outside the checkout.');
  }
  return canonical;
}

async function readJSON(path, { optional = false, limit = FILE_LIMIT } = {}) {
  let stat;
  try { stat = await fs.lstat(path); }
  catch (error) { if (optional && error.code === 'ENOENT') return undefined; throw error; }
  if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || stat.size > limit) fail('INVALID_FILE', 'Advisory files must be bounded regular files without symbolic links.');
  let descriptor;
  try { descriptor = await fs.open(path, constants.O_RDONLY | (constants.O_NOFOLLOW ?? 0)); }
  catch (error) { if (optional && error.code === 'ENOENT') return undefined; throw error; }
  try {
    const opened = await descriptor.stat();
    if (!opened.isFile() || opened.size > limit) fail('INVALID_FILE', 'The advisory file changed or is too large.');
    const bytes = await descriptor.readFile();
    if (bytes.length > limit) fail('INVALID_FILE', 'The advisory file is too large.');
    try { return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(bytes)); }
    catch { fail('INVALID_JSON', 'The advisory file must contain valid UTF-8 JSON.'); }
  } finally { await descriptor.close(); }
}

async function syncDirectory(path) {
  let descriptor;
  try { descriptor = await fs.open(path, 'r'); await descriptor.sync(); }
  catch (error) { if (process.platform !== 'win32' || !['EPERM', 'EACCES', 'EISDIR', 'EINVAL'].includes(error.code)) throw error; }
  finally { await descriptor?.close(); }
}

async function producerLock(path) {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
    try {
      const descriptor = await fs.open(path, 'wx', 0o600);
      try { await descriptor.writeFile(JSON.stringify({ version: 1, pid: process.pid })); await descriptor.sync(); }
      catch (error) { await descriptor.close(); await fs.unlink(path); throw error; }
      await descriptor.close();
      return async () => { await fs.unlink(path); };
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.code !== 'EEXIST') throw error;
      const previous = await readJSON(path, { limit: 1024 });
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(previous?.pid) || previous.pid < 1) fail('QUEUE_BUSY', 'An invalid producer lock requires local review.');
      try { process.kill(previous.pid, 0); }
      catch (probe) {
        if (probe.code === 'ESRCH') { await fs.unlink(path); continue; }
      }
      fail('QUEUE_BUSY', 'Another producer owns this advisory ID; retry after it finishes.');
    }
  }
  fail('QUEUE_BUSY', 'The advisory producer lock could not be acquired.');
}

/** Queueing is local; only the existing relay can acknowledge remote delivery. */
export async function enqueueAdvisory(outbox, value) {
  const advisory = validateAdvisory(value);
  const body = JSON.stringify(advisory);
  const envelope = { topicId: 'coordination-advisory', body, clientMessageId: advisory.id };
  const directory = await privateOutbox(outbox);
  const hash = createHash('sha256').update(advisory.id, 'utf8').digest('hex');
  const path = resolve(directory, `advisory-${hash}.json`);
  const unlock = await producerLock(resolve(directory, `.advisory-${hash}.lock`));
  let temporary;
  try {
    const files = await fs.readdir(directory);
    if (files.length > 10_000) fail('QUEUE_LIMIT', 'The outbox exceeds the advisory queue inspection limit.');
    const candidates = [path, ...files.filter(name => name.endsWith('.json') && resolve(directory, name) !== path).sort().map(name => resolve(directory, name))];
    let inspectedBytes = 0;
    for (const candidate of candidates) {
      let existing;
      try {
        const stat = await fs.lstat(candidate).catch(error => { if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return null; throw error; });
        if (!stat) continue;
        inspectedBytes += stat.size;
        if (inspectedBytes > 32 * 1024 * 1024) fail('QUEUE_LIMIT', 'The outbox exceeds the advisory byte inspection limit.');
        existing = await readJSON(candidate, { optional: true });
      } catch (error) {
        // Other producers may leave bad items for the relay to quarantine. Do
        // not execute, follow, replace, or let those items block an unrelated ID.
        if (candidate !== path && error instanceof AdvisoryError && ['INVALID_FILE', 'INVALID_JSON'].includes(error.code)) continue;
        throw error;
      }
      if (existing === undefined || (candidate !== path && existing?.clientMessageId !== advisory.id)) continue;
      if (!isObject(existing) || Object.keys(existing).sort().join(',') !== 'body,clientMessageId,topicId'
          || existing.topicId !== envelope.topicId || existing.clientMessageId !== envelope.clientMessageId || existing.body !== envelope.body) {
        fail('ID_CONFLICT', 'This advisory ID is already queued with different content.');
      }
      return { status: 'already-queued', id: advisory.id, path: candidate };
    }
    temporary = resolve(directory, `.advisory-${hash}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`);
    const descriptor = await fs.open(temporary, 'wx', 0o600);
    try { await descriptor.writeFile(JSON.stringify(envelope, null, 2) + '\n'); await descriptor.sync(); }
    finally { await descriptor.close(); }
    await fs.rename(temporary, path);
    temporary = undefined;
    await syncDirectory(directory);
    return { status: 'queued', id: advisory.id, path };
  } finally {
    if (temporary) await fs.unlink(temporary).catch(error => { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; });
    await unlock();
  }
}

export async function main(argv = process.argv.slice(2)) {
  try {
    if (argv.length === 1 && argv[0] === '--help') {
      console.log('Usage: node relay/advisory.mjs --outbox /private/seat/outbox --file /private/advisory.json');
      return 0;
    }
    const options = new Map();
    for (let index = 0; index < argv.length; index += 2) {
      const name = argv[index]; const value = argv[index + 1];
      if (!['--outbox', '--file'].includes(name) || options.has(name) || !value || value.startsWith('--')) fail('INVALID_ARGUMENTS', 'Supply --outbox and --file once each.');
      options.set(name, value);
    }
    if (options.size !== 2) fail('INVALID_ARGUMENTS', 'Supply --outbox and --file.');
    const advisory = await readJSON(resolve(options.get('--file')));
    const result = await enqueueAdvisory(options.get('--outbox'), advisory);
    console.log(JSON.stringify({ event: 'advisory_queued', ...result }));
    return 0;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(JSON.stringify({ event: 'advisory_error', code: error instanceof AdvisoryError ? error.code : 'LOCAL_IO_ERROR', message: error instanceof AdvisoryError ? error.message : 'The advisory could not be queued.' }));
    return 1;
  }
}

if (process.argv[1] && resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) process.exitCode = await main();

===== END FILE relay/advisory.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE relay/advisory.test.mjs | SHA256 007753b468c00d16c5227b0981ec9e9dfb4454c9b87cc48562ba2a4bb76e963c | BYTES 12926 =====
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import * as fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { dirname, join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import test from 'node:test';
import { AdvisoryError, enqueueAdvisory, validateAdvisory } from './advisory.mjs';

const ROOT = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const CLI = resolve(ROOT, 'relay/advisory.mjs');
const packet = changes => ({
  protocol: 'omi-advisory/1', id: 'dot-plan-001', kind: 'assignment', from: 'dot', to: ['gemini', 'claude'],
  taskId: 'QA-001', summary: 'Review relay recovery and report reproducible findings.',
  details: { source: { revision: 'example-revision', paths: ['relay/git-worker.mjs'] }, checks: ['crash recovery', 'duplicate delivery'] },
  createdAt: '2026-11-02T20:00:00.000Z', requiresAck: true, ...changes,
});
async function fixture(t) {
  const directory = await fs.mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'omi-advisory-'));
  const outbox = join(directory, 'outbox');
  await fs.mkdir(outbox, { mode: 0o700 });
  t.after(() => fs.rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  return { directory, outbox };
}
const hash = id => createHash('sha256').update(id).digest('hex');
const queuePath = (outbox, id) => join(outbox, `advisory-${hash(id)}.json`);
const errorCode = code => error => error instanceof AdvisoryError && error.code === code;

test('validation produces a canonical independent JSON snapshot', () => {
  const value = packet({ details: { z: [1, false, null], a: { second: 2, first: 1 } }, summary: '  Readable status.  ' });
  const actual = validateAdvisory(value);
  assert.equal(actual.summary, 'Readable status.');
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(actual.details), ['a', 'z']);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(actual.details.a), ['first', 'second']);
  value.details.a.first = 999;
  value.to.push('muse');
  assert.equal(actual.details.a.first, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(actual.to, ['gemini', 'claude']);
  assert.equal(validateAdvisory(packet({ kind: 'ack', details: null })).kind, 'ack');
});

test('schema refuses invalid roles, identities, recipients, dates, unknown fields and missing fields', () => {
  const invalid = [
    null, [], packet({ protocol: 'unverified-protocol' }), packet({ kind: 'execute' }), packet({ id: 123 }),
    packet({ from: '../operator' }), packet({ to: [] }), packet({ to: ['muse', 'MUSE'] }), packet({ to: [12] }),
    packet({ to: new Array(1) }), packet({ to: Array.from({ length: 33 }, (_, i) => `seat-${i}`) }),
    packet({ taskId: 'x'.repeat(101) }), packet({ summary: ' ' }), packet({ summary: 'x'.repeat(601) }),
    packet({ createdAt: '2026-11-02' }), packet({ createdAt: 'not-a-date' }), packet({ requiresAck: 'yes' }), packet({ extra: true }),
  ];
  for (const value of invalid) assert.throws(() => validateAdvisory(value), AdvisoryError);
  const absent = packet(); delete absent.details;
  assert.throws(() => validateAdvisory(absent), AdvisoryError);
  let read = false;
  const getter = packet(); Object.defineProperty(getter, 'summary', { enumerable: true, get() { read = true; return 'No'; } });
  assert.throws(() => validateAdvisory(getter), AdvisoryError);
  assert.equal(read, false);
});

test('common credential-shaped keys are refused at every nesting level without inspecting values', () => {
  for (const field of ['token', 'apiKey', 'password', 'PIN', 'secret', 'privateKey', 'access_token', 'refreshToken', 'client-secret', 'Authorization']) {
    assert.throws(() => validateAdvisory(packet({ details: { nested: [{ [field]: 'synthetic-value-never-log' }] } })), errorCode('CREDENTIAL_FIELD'));
  }
  assert.throws(() => validateAdvisory(packet({ token: 'synthetic-value-never-log' })), errorCode('CREDENTIAL_FIELD'));
  // A key heuristic cannot discover a credential hidden in free text; do not
  // claim that it can. Legitimate descriptions about token handling remain text.
  assert.equal(validateAdvisory(packet({ details: { note: 'Review how credentials are handled.' } })).details.note, 'Review how credentials are handled.');
});

test('details reject lossy/non-JSON data, getters, cycles and excessive structure', () => {
  const cycle = {}; cycle.self = cycle;
  for (const value of [undefined, NaN, Infinity, 1n, new Date(), new Map(), cycle, [undefined], new Array(2)]) {
    assert.throws(() => validateAdvisory(packet({ details: value })), AdvisoryError);
  }
  let read = false;
  const getter = []; Object.defineProperty(getter, '0', { enumerable: true, get() { read = true; return 1; } });
  assert.throws(() => validateAdvisory(packet({ details: getter })), AdvisoryError);
  assert.equal(read, false);
  let deep = null; for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) deep = { next: deep };
  assert.throws(() => validateAdvisory(packet({ details: deep })), errorCode('DETAIL_LIMIT'));
  assert.throws(() => validateAdvisory(packet({ details: Array(4096).fill(0) })), errorCode('DETAIL_LIMIT'));
});

test('the serialized transport body is bounded even when individual strings fit', () => {
  assert.throws(() => validateAdvisory(packet({ details: { first: 'x'.repeat(6000), second: 'y'.repeat(6000) } })), errorCode('BODY_TOO_LARGE'));
  assert.throws(() => validateAdvisory(packet({ details: { code: 'x'.repeat(12_001) } })), AdvisoryError);
});

test('queue creates the existing transport envelope with private durable files and no temporary residue', async t => {
  const { outbox } = await fixture(t);
  const value = packet();
  const result = await enqueueAdvisory(outbox, value);
  assert.equal(result.status, 'queued');
  assert.equal(result.id, value.id);
  const envelope = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(result.path, 'utf8'));
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(envelope), ['topicId', 'body', 'clientMessageId']);
  assert.equal(envelope.topicId, 'coordination-advisory');
  assert.equal(envelope.clientMessageId, value.id);
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(envelope.body), validateAdvisory(value));
  assert.equal(envelope.body.length <= 12_000, true);
  if (process.platform !== 'win32') assert.equal((await fs.stat(result.path)).mode & 0o777, 0o600);
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.readdir(outbox), [result.path.split(/[\\/]/).at(-1)]);
});

test('equivalent requeue is idempotent; changed content under the same ID cannot overwrite it', async t => {
  const { outbox } = await fixture(t);
  const original = packet({ details: { z: 1, a: 2 } });
  const first = await enqueueAdvisory(outbox, original);
  const bytes = await fs.readFile(first.path, 'utf8');
  const again = await enqueueAdvisory(outbox, packet({ details: { a: 2, z: 1 } }));
  assert.equal(again.status, 'already-queued');
  await assert.rejects(enqueueAdvisory(outbox, packet({ summary: 'Different work.' })), errorCode('ID_CONFLICT'));
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(first.path, 'utf8'), bytes);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(outbox)).length, 1);
});

test('snapshot is captured before awaits and transport requeue retains the stable request ID', async t => {
  const { outbox } = await fixture(t);
  const value = packet({ details: { state: 'before' } });
  const pending = enqueueAdvisory(outbox, value);
  value.details.state = 'after';
  const result = await pending;
  const first = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(result.path, 'utf8'));
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(first.body).details.state, 'before');
  await fs.unlink(result.path); // Represents a relay that acknowledged/archived it.
  const requeued = await enqueueAdvisory(outbox, packet({ details: { state: 'before' } }));
  assert.equal(requeued.status, 'queued');
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(requeued.path, 'utf8')).clientMessageId, value.id);
});

test('existing manual-producer filenames share stable request-ID protection', async t => {
  const { outbox } = await fixture(t);
  const value = packet();
  const manual = join(outbox, 'manually-queued.json');
  await fs.writeFile(manual, JSON.stringify({ topicId: 'coordination-advisory', body: JSON.stringify(validateAdvisory(value)), clientMessageId: value.id }), { mode: 0o600 });
  assert.deepEqual(await enqueueAdvisory(outbox, value), { status: 'already-queued', id: value.id, path: manual });
  await assert.rejects(enqueueAdvisory(outbox, packet({ summary: 'Changed request.' })), errorCode('ID_CONFLICT'));
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.readdir(outbox), ['manually-queued.json']);
});

test('concurrent conflicting producers never replace the winning advisory', async t => {
  const { outbox } = await fixture(t);
  const values = [packet({ summary: 'First candidate.' }), packet({ summary: 'Second candidate.' })];
  const results = await Promise.allSettled(values.map(value => enqueueAdvisory(outbox, value)));
  const winning = results.map((result, i) => ({ result, i })).filter(item => item.result.status === 'fulfilled');
  assert.equal(winning.length, 1);
  const failure = results.find(result => result.status === 'rejected');
  assert(['QUEUE_BUSY', 'ID_CONFLICT'].includes(failure.reason.code));
  const envelope = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(winning[0].result.value.path, 'utf8'));
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(envelope.body).summary, values[winning[0].i].summary);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(outbox)).length, 1);
});

test('unsafe/private paths and symlink destinations are refused without modifying targets', async t => {
  const { directory, outbox } = await fixture(t);
  await assert.rejects(enqueueAdvisory('relative-path', packet()), errorCode('INVALID_OUTBOX'));
  await assert.rejects(enqueueAdvisory(ROOT, packet()), errorCode('INVALID_OUTBOX'));
  await assert.rejects(enqueueAdvisory(join(directory, 'absent'), packet()), errorCode('INVALID_OUTBOX'));
  const linked = join(directory, 'linked'); await fs.symlink(outbox, linked, 'dir');
  await assert.rejects(enqueueAdvisory(linked, packet()), errorCode('INVALID_OUTBOX'));
  if (process.platform !== 'win32') {
    await fs.chmod(outbox, 0o755);
    await assert.rejects(enqueueAdvisory(outbox, packet()), errorCode('INVALID_OUTBOX'));
    await fs.chmod(outbox, 0o700);
  }
  const target = join(directory, 'preserve.txt'); await fs.writeFile(target, 'preserve this fixture', { mode: 0o600 });
  await fs.symlink(target, queuePath(outbox, packet().id));
  await assert.rejects(enqueueAdvisory(outbox, packet()), errorCode('INVALID_FILE'));
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(target, 'utf8'), 'preserve this fixture');
});

test('an active producer lock is preserved and a terminated producer lock can recover', async t => {
  const { outbox } = await fixture(t);
  const lock = join(outbox, `.advisory-${hash(packet().id)}.lock`);
  await fs.writeFile(lock, JSON.stringify({ version: 1, pid: process.pid }), { mode: 0o600 });
  await assert.rejects(enqueueAdvisory(outbox, packet()), errorCode('QUEUE_BUSY'));
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(lock, 'utf8')).pid, process.pid);
  await fs.writeFile(lock, JSON.stringify({ version: 1, pid: 2_147_483_647 }), { mode: 0o600 });
  assert.equal((await enqueueAdvisory(outbox, packet())).status, 'queued');
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(outbox)).length, 1);
});

test('CLI queues a real input file and prints metadata only, without contents', async t => {
  const { directory, outbox } = await fixture(t);
  const input = join(directory, 'advisory.json');
  const value = packet({ summary: 'BODY-MUST-NOT-BE-LOGGED', details: { marker: 'PRIVATE-NOTE-MUST-NOT-BE-LOGGED' } });
  await fs.writeFile(input, JSON.stringify(value), { mode: 0o600 });
  const stdout = execFileSync(process.execPath, [CLI, '--outbox', outbox, '--file', input], { encoding: 'utf8' });
  const result = JSON.parse(stdout);
  assert.equal(result.event, 'advisory_queued');
  assert.equal(result.status, 'queued');
  assert.equal(result.id, value.id);
  assert(!stdout.includes(value.summary));
  assert(!stdout.includes(value.details.marker));
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(result.path, 'utf8')).body).summary, value.summary);
});

test('CLI malformed JSON/UTF-8 and credential field errors fail without echoing input', async t => {
  const { directory, outbox } = await fixture(t);
  for (const [name, contents] of [
    ['broken', '{"summary":"PRIVATE-INPUT-DO-NOT-ECHO"'],
    ['utf8', Buffer.from([0xff])],
    ['credential', JSON.stringify(packet({ details: { apiKey: 'PRIVATE-INPUT-DO-NOT-ECHO' } }))],
  ]) {
    const input = join(directory, `${name}.json`);
    await fs.writeFile(input, contents, { mode: 0o600 });
    let failure;
    try { execFileSync(process.execPath, [CLI, '--outbox', outbox, '--file', input], { encoding: 'utf8', stdio: 'pipe' }); }
    catch (error) { failure = error; }
    assert.equal(failure?.status, 1);
    assert.equal(JSON.parse(failure.stderr).event, 'advisory_error');
    assert(!String(failure.stderr).includes('PRIVATE-INPUT-DO-NOT-ECHO'));
  }
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.readdir(outbox), []);
});
