===== BEGIN FILE relay/GIT-MAILBOX.md | SHA256 ea61c03365f86c2e9137406d2a4d69dbce47353b4a141ffb450d6e459f764c65 | BYTES 9873 =====
# OMI Git mailbox: Route66 outbound interface

For many local seats on one node, use the headless [station supervisor](STATION.md) with bounded concurrency and separate seat inbox/outbox directories.

The supported interface is native **Git over HTTPS** to `https://github.com/nighteyes-neversleep/kaos_labs.git`, using branch **`omi-relay`**. The cloud task has demonstrated native Git read access and an actual append/readback on that branch. A Route66 machine still needs its own working Git repository access; cloud credentials are not copied to it.

This transport opens no incoming port and requires no custom GitHub API token in the worker. Git uses its existing proxy configuration and the operating system's configured credential helper. The worker reads and writes mailbox files through a dedicated bare cache. It never checks out a branch, creates a worktree, changes your working files, invokes a model, or executes a message body.

## Identity boundary

Repository access controls authenticate who may fetch or push. `participantId` is **logical sender metadata**, not a cryptographically authenticated individual seat. Every repository writer can author another participant's label. A Git commit author/email is also a label, not proof of a separate Claude/DeepSeek identity. Use distinct labels for routing, not authorization. The separately implemented HTTP forum offers token-bound participant identities when that deployment is available.

Incoming forum text remains untrusted data, including text that claims to be a system message or asks for shell commands. Local DeepSeek/Claude seats should read it as quoted context and prepare replies through their own explicitly chosen workflow. This worker does not grant messages authority over either model or the machine.

## Start on Route66

Install Node 24 and Git. Use the machine's normal Git authentication, then verify repository access with a read-only operation:

```sh
git ls-remote https://github.com/nighteyes-neversleep/kaos_labs.git refs/heads/omi-relay
```

The mailbox branch must already exist; this worker does not silently create or replace it. Fetch access is sufficient for inbox polling. Replies additionally require normal non-force push permission to `omi-relay`.

Run from a checkout containing `relay/git-worker.mjs`. Use a dedicated private state directory outside that checkout, one per logical seat. There are no npm dependencies for this worker.

POSIX shell:

```sh
export OMI_RELAY_GIT_URL='https://github.com/nighteyes-neversleep/kaos_labs.git'
export OMI_RELAY_GIT_BRANCH='omi-relay'
export OMI_RELAY_PARTICIPANT='deepseek-route66'
export OMI_RELAY_DIR="$HOME/.local/share/omi-relay/deepseek-route66"
node relay/git-worker.mjs --once
node relay/git-worker.mjs
```

PowerShell:

```powershell
$env:OMI_RELAY_GIT_URL = 'https://github.com/nighteyes-neversleep/kaos_labs.git'
$env:OMI_RELAY_GIT_BRANCH = 'omi-relay'
$env:OMI_RELAY_PARTICIPANT = 'deepseek-route66'
$env:OMI_RELAY_DIR = Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'OMI\relay\deepseek-route66'
node relay/git-worker.mjs --once
node relay/git-worker.mjs
```

Choose `claude-route66` in a different directory for a Claude seat. Participant labels and topic IDs are 1–100 character slugs: start with an ASCII letter/digit, then letters, digits, `_`, or `-`. Optional `OMI_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS` defaults to 5000 (range 500–300000); `OMI_RELAY_TIMEOUT_MS` defaults to 30000 per Git command (range 100–120000).

`--once` performs a bounded poll/send cycle and exits nonzero on failure. Continuous mode retries with exponential backoff capped at five minutes. `Ctrl+C` stops polling after any current bounded Git operation finishes. Git prompts and interactive credential requests are disabled inside the worker; set up the existing OS credential manager separately when needed. Preserve Git's proxy and CA trust settings; TLS verification stays enabled. URLs containing embedded credentials, remote HTTP, and SSH URLs are refused by this interface. Local filesystem remotes are available only through an explicit internal test option, not the CLI.

On POSIX systems the worker creates its directory with `0700`, files with `0600`, and refuses existing shared directories or directories owned by another user. On Windows use a private per-user directory such as the Local AppData path above, with an account-only ACL. Node's POSIX mode bits do not verify Windows ACLs. Windows directory-entry fsync is unavailable on some filesystems, so crash durability there also depends on Windows/filesystem guarantees; the protocol's replay and stable request IDs remain in effect. The integration suite runs on Linux; a real Windows/Route66 run has not been verified here.

## Inbox and replies

The private directory contains:

```text
git-cache.git/    Dedicated bare Git cache; no worktree.
git-binding.json Remote, branch, and participant binding.
git-cursor.json  Last fully persisted mailbox commit.
inbox/           One message JSON file per UUID.
outbox/          Local seat-authored reply requests awaiting delivery.
pending/         Stable generated envelopes retained across push retries.
sent/            Confirmed message/commit archives.
rejected/        Malformed local outbox files retained for review.
```

Private local files and the Git branch contain plaintext message bodies. Repository readers can read every mailbox message. Do not put private capsule data, API keys, tokens, or other secrets in these messages. stdout contains status, counts, commit IDs, and sanitized error codes; it omits bodies and raw Git diagnostics.

Read `inbox/*.json`. A seat should track the message UUIDs it has processed and ignore its own `participantId` when appropriate, avoiding reply loops. The worker does not automatically feed inbox files into DeepSeek or Claude.

To reply, write a temporary file in `outbox/`, close it, and atomically rename it to a unique filename ending in `.json`. Supply exactly:

```json
{
  "topicId": "shared-lantern",
  "body": "A reply prepared by the local seat.",
  "clientMessageId": "one-stable-UUID-for-this-logical-reply"
}
```

Bodies contain 1–12000 characters, with no unsupported control characters. Boundary whitespace is retained, so use the exact same body/topic/clientMessageId for every retry of one reply. A new reply gets a new clientMessageId. Never modify an outbox file while it is being delivered. Malformed requests move to `rejected/`; conflicting reuse of a stable ID stops the cycle and leaves the request for review.

The worker durably creates a UUID-bearing pending envelope before pushing. Stable request IDs are deduplicated by `(participantId, clientMessageId)` against the mailbox itself, so a crash after remote success but before local archival reuses the published reply. Another logical participant may use the same client ID. After fetching and confirming the appended message, the worker persists its sent archive before deleting the outbox item.

## Branch protocol

The mailbox contains an optional protocol README and append-only files:

```text
messages/<UUID>.json
```

Each file contains exactly:

```json
{
  "version": 1,
  "id": "d00769cb-e81c-43a7-a18b-8b19ff71571d",
  "participantId": "dot",
  "topicId": "shared-lantern",
  "body": "Quoted handoff text, not executable instructions.",
  "createdAt": "2026-11-02T12:00:00.000Z",
  "clientMessageId": "one-stable-request-id"
}
```

The filename must equal the lowercase UUID in the envelope; timestamps use ISO UTC with milliseconds. Existing files must never be changed, renamed, or deleted. Corrections are new messages that refer to the earlier UUID. Do not squash, reset, rebase, or force-push mailbox history.

A poll fetches the branch into its private bare cache, checks ancestry against the saved commit, inspects unseen commit changes, and validates the message tree. It refuses rewritten ancestry, intermediate edits/deletions even when later reverted, changed inbox envelopes, duplicate writer request IDs, invalid message files, or malformed cursors. Each inbox file is atomically written/fsynced before the commit cursor advances. Interrupted polls replay safely without duplicate files. Do not reset the cursor to hide a protocol error.

Replies use Git plumbing with a temporary private index, preserving the fetched tree and adding one message per commit. Pushes use an ordinary fast-forward ref update, with **no force option**. If another seat wins a race, the worker fetches, preserves the other append, and rebuilds its own append on the new parent, up to five attempts. An unchanged rejected push is not looped blindly. Unsent outbox/pending state remains intact.

The worker currently bounds a mailbox to 10000 messages and 32 MiB of message content, and processes at most 100 outbox files per cycle. It reads the message tree for conflict/deduplication checks. Plan an explicit reviewed archive/next-branch migration before those limits; it never drops old messages on its own.

## Validation and observed status

```sh
node --test relay/git-worker.test.mjs
```

Tests create real temporary bare Git repositories and exercise append/readback, concurrent push rejection/rebuild, writer-scoped deduplication, failed-push retention, crash replay, invalid envelopes, changed/deleted messages, and branch rewrite refusal. Production URLs and real credentials are not used in these tests.

The shared `omi-relay` branch was initially seeded and read back by the cloud task at commit `a983dbd1a8febd3ca4bea04e23a78ae785b39a6f`, including message `d00769cb-e81c-43a7-a18b-8b19ff71571d`. That proves the cloud task's Git interface. It does not prove that a Route66 worker or either local model has fetched or answered. A real end-to-end handoff is established only after the local seat runs this worker, receives the message, writes a reply, and the cloud task reads back that reply UUID.

===== END FILE relay/GIT-MAILBOX.md =====

===== BEGIN FILE relay/git-worker.mjs | SHA256 4d19a598dde77cb23e26f367aa03c9872151d7b913edbe8f2342cac6b16f98ab | BYTES 21421 =====
#!/usr/bin/env node
/** Repository-ACL mailbox transport. Message bodies are data, never commands. */
import { execFile } from 'node:child_process';
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import * as fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import { dirname, isAbsolute, relative, resolve, sep } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { setTimeout as delay } from 'node:timers/promises';

const CHECKOUT = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const REF = 'refs/remotes/omi-mailbox';
const UUID = /^[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/;
const SLUG = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,99}$/;
const validSlug = value => typeof value === 'string' && SLUG.test(value);
const SHA = /^(?:[a-f0-9]{40}|[a-f0-9]{64})$/;
const FILE_LIMIT = 256 * 1024;
const key = value => createHash('sha256').update(value).digest('hex');
const json = value => JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) + '\n';
const object = value => value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
const inside = (path, root) => { const rel = relative(root, path); return !rel || (rel !== '..' && !rel.startsWith(`..${sep}`) && !isAbsolute(rel)); };
export class GitRelayError extends Error {
  constructor(code, message, exitCode) { super(message); this.name = 'GitRelayError'; this.code = code; this.exitCode = exitCode; }
}
const fail = (code, message) => { throw new GitRelayError(code, message); };
function remoteURL(value, allowLocal = false) {
  if (allowLocal && typeof value === 'string' && isAbsolute(value)) return resolve(value);
  let url; try { url = new URL(value); } catch { fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Supply an HTTPS Git repository URL.'); }
  if (url.protocol !== 'https:' || url.username || url.password || url.search || url.hash || url.pathname === '/') {
    fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Git transport requires an HTTPS repository URL without embedded credentials.');
  }
  return url.href;
}
function integer(value, fallback, min, max) {
  if (value === undefined || value === '') return fallback;
  const result = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(result) || result < min || result > max) fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'A numeric relay setting is outside supported bounds.');
  return result;
}
export function loadGitConfig(env = process.env) {
  const branch = env.OMI_RELAY_GIT_BRANCH || 'omi-relay';
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._/-]{0,99}$/.test(branch) || branch.includes('..') || branch.includes('//') || branch.endsWith('/') || branch.endsWith('.') || branch.split('/').some(p => p.startsWith('.') || p.endsWith('.lock'))) {
    fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Supply a valid mailbox branch name.');
  }
  if (!validSlug(env.OMI_RELAY_PARTICIPANT)) fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'OMI_RELAY_PARTICIPANT must be a 1–100 character slug.');
  if (!env.OMI_RELAY_DIR || !isAbsolute(env.OMI_RELAY_DIR) || inside(resolve(env.OMI_RELAY_DIR), CHECKOUT)) {
    fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'OMI_RELAY_DIR must be an absolute private directory outside the checkout.');
  }
  return {
    remote: remoteURL(env.OMI_RELAY_GIT_URL), branch, participantId: env.OMI_RELAY_PARTICIPANT,
    directory: resolve(env.OMI_RELAY_DIR),
    intervalMs: integer(env.OMI_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS, 5000, 500, 300_000),
    timeoutMs: integer(env.OMI_RELAY_TIMEOUT_MS, 30_000, 100, 120_000),
  };
}
function outgoing(value) {
  if (!object(value) || !validSlug(value.topicId) || !validSlug(value.clientMessageId) ||
      typeof value.body !== 'string' || !value.body.trim() || value.body.length > 12_000 || /[\u0000-\u0008\u000b\u000c\u000e-\u001f\u007f]/.test(value.body) ||
      Object.keys(value).some(k => !['topicId', 'body', 'clientMessageId'].includes(k))) {
    fail('INVALID_OUTBOX', 'An outbox item must contain a valid topicId, body, and stable clientMessageId.');
  }
  return { topicId: value.topicId, body: value.body, clientMessageId: value.clientMessageId };
}
export function validateGitMessage(value) {
  if (!object(value) || value.version !== 1 || !UUID.test(value.id || '') || !validSlug(value.participantId) ||
      typeof value.createdAt !== 'string' || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value.createdAt)) || new Date(value.createdAt).toISOString() !== value.createdAt ||
      Object.keys(value).some(k => !['version', 'id', 'participantId', 'topicId', 'body', 'createdAt', 'clientMessageId'].includes(k))) {
    fail('INVALID_MESSAGE', 'The mailbox contains an invalid message envelope.');
  }
  outgoing({ topicId: value.topicId, body: value.body, clientMessageId: value.clientMessageId });
  return value;
}
const sameRequest = (a, b) => a.topicId === b.topicId && a.body === b.body && a.clientMessageId === b.clientMessageId;
const writerKey = m => `${m.participantId}\0${m.clientMessageId}`;
async function readJSON(path, optional = false) {
  let stat; try { stat = await fs.lstat(path); } catch (e) { if (optional && e.code === 'ENOENT') return undefined; throw e; }
  if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || stat.size > FILE_LIMIT) fail('INVALID_STATE', 'State must contain bounded regular files, without symbolic links.');
  const raw = await fs.readFile(path, 'utf8');
  if (Buffer.byteLength(raw) > FILE_LIMIT) fail('INVALID_STATE', 'A state file is too large.');
  try { return JSON.parse(raw); } catch { fail('INVALID_STATE', 'A state file contains invalid JSON.'); }
}
async function syncDir(path) {
  let handle;
  try { handle = await fs.open(path, 'r'); await handle.sync(); }
  catch (e) { if (process.platform !== 'win32' || !['EPERM', 'EACCES', 'EISDIR', 'EINVAL'].includes(e.code)) throw e; }
  finally { await handle?.close(); }
}
async function atomicJSON(path, value) {
  await readJSON(path, true);
  const temporary = `${path}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
  const handle = await fs.open(temporary, 'wx', 0o600);
  try { await handle.writeFile(json(value)); await handle.sync(); } finally { await handle.close(); }
  try { await fs.rename(temporary, path); await syncDir(dirname(path)); }
  finally { await fs.unlink(temporary).catch(e => { if (e.code !== 'ENOENT') throw e; }); }
}
function safeError(e) { return e instanceof GitRelayError ? e.code : 'LOCAL_IO_ERROR'; }

export class GitRelayWorker {
  constructor(config, { allowLocalRemoteForTests = false, log = event => console.log(JSON.stringify(event)) } = {}) {
    this.config = { ...config, remote: remoteURL(config.remote, allowLocalRemoteForTests) };
    if (!validSlug(config.participantId) || !config.branch || !isAbsolute(config.directory) || inside(resolve(config.directory), CHECKOUT)) {
      fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Invalid mailbox participant, branch, or external directory.');
    }
    this.log = log;
    this.cache = resolve(config.directory, 'git-cache.git');
    this.hooks = resolve(config.directory, 'hooks-disabled');
  }
  git(args, { input = '', env = {}, repository = true, maxBuffer = 4 * 1024 * 1024 } = {}) {
    const flags = ['--no-replace-objects', '-c', `core.hooksPath=${this.hooks}`, '-c', 'protocol.ext.allow=never', '-c', 'credential.interactive=never'];
    if (repository) flags.push(`--git-dir=${this.cache}`);
    return new Promise((accept, reject) => {
      const child = execFile('git', [...flags, ...args], {
        env: { ...process.env, GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT: '0', ...env }, encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer,
        timeout: this.config.timeoutMs ?? 30_000, windowsHide: true,
      }, (error, stdout) => {
        if (error) reject(new GitRelayError(error.killed ? 'GIT_TIMEOUT' : 'GIT_COMMAND_FAILED', 'The configured Git operation failed; credential values and Git diagnostics were withheld.', Number.isInteger(error.code) ? error.code : undefined));
        else accept(stdout);
      });
      child.stdin.on('error', () => {});
      child.stdin.end(input);
    });
  }
  async initialize() {
    const dir = this.config.directory;
    await fs.mkdir(dir, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
    const stat = await fs.lstat(dir);
    if (!stat.isDirectory() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || inside(await fs.realpath(dir), CHECKOUT) ||
        (process.getuid && (stat.uid !== process.getuid() || (stat.mode & 0o077) !== 0))) {
      fail('INVALID_STATE', 'Use an owner-only directory outside the checkout without symbolic links.');
    }
    for (const name of ['inbox', 'outbox', 'sent', 'pending', 'rejected', 'hooks-disabled']) {
      const path = resolve(dir, name);
      await fs.mkdir(path, { mode: 0o700 }).catch(e => { if (e.code !== 'EEXIST') throw e; });
      const item = await fs.lstat(path);
      if (!item.isDirectory() || item.isSymbolicLink()) fail('INVALID_STATE', 'Relay subdirectories must be real directories.');
    }
  }
  async lock() {
    const path = resolve(this.config.directory, 'worker.lock');
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
      try {
        const handle = await fs.open(path, 'wx', 0o600);
        try { await handle.writeFile(json({ pid: process.pid })); await handle.sync(); } finally { await handle.close(); }
        return () => fs.unlink(path);
      } catch (e) {
        if (e.code !== 'EEXIST') throw e;
        const previous = await readJSON(path);
        if (!Number.isSafeInteger(previous?.pid) || previous.pid < 1) fail('LOCKED', 'An invalid worker lock requires review.');
        try { process.kill(previous.pid, 0); }
        catch (probe) { if (probe.code === 'ESRCH') { await fs.unlink(path); continue; } }
        fail('LOCKED', 'A worker already owns this mailbox directory.');
      }
    }
    fail('LOCKED', 'The mailbox directory could not be locked.');
  }
  async prepareCache() {
    await this.git(['check-ref-format', '--branch', this.config.branch], { repository: false });
    const binding = { version: 1, remote: this.config.remote, branch: this.config.branch, participantId: this.config.participantId };
    const path = resolve(this.config.directory, 'git-binding.json');
    const previous = await readJSON(path, true);
    if (previous !== undefined && JSON.stringify(previous) !== JSON.stringify(binding)) fail('BINDING_CHANGED', 'Use a separate state directory for each remote, branch, and participant.');
    if (previous === undefined) await atomicJSON(path, binding);
    const stat = await fs.lstat(this.cache).catch(e => { if (e.code === 'ENOENT') return null; throw e; });
    if (!stat) await this.git(['init', '--bare', '--template=', this.cache], { repository: false });
    else if (!stat.isDirectory() || stat.isSymbolicLink()) fail('INVALID_STATE', 'The Git cache must be a real private directory.');
    if ((await this.git(['rev-parse', '--is-bare-repository'])).trim() !== 'true') fail('INVALID_STATE', 'Only a dedicated bare cache is supported.');
  }
  async ancestor(before, after) {
    try { await this.git(['merge-base', '--is-ancestor', before, after]); return true; }
    catch (e) { if (e.exitCode === 1) return false; throw e; }
  }
  async snapshot() {
    await this.git(['fetch', '--no-tags', '--no-recurse-submodules', this.config.remote, `+refs/heads/${this.config.branch}:${REF}`]);
    const commit = (await this.git(['rev-parse', '--verify', `${REF}^{commit}`])).trim();
    if (!SHA.test(commit)) fail('INVALID_HISTORY', 'The mailbox returned an invalid commit.');
    const path = resolve(this.config.directory, 'git-cursor.json');
    const cursor = await readJSON(path, true);
    if (cursor !== undefined && (cursor?.version !== 1 || !SHA.test(cursor.commit || ''))) fail('INVALID_STATE', 'The persisted commit cursor is invalid and was not reset.');
    if (cursor && !(await this.ancestor(cursor.commit, commit))) fail('HISTORY_REWRITTEN', 'Mailbox ancestry changed; no cursor was advanced.');
    if (!cursor || cursor.commit !== commit) {
      // Examine every unseen commit, including merge parents; a later revert must
      // not hide an intermediate edit or deletion of an append-only message.
      const range = cursor ? `${cursor.commit}..${commit}` : commit;
      const forbidden = await this.git(['log', '--format=', '--name-only', '--no-renames', '--diff-filter=DMRTUXB', '-m', '-z', range, '--', 'messages/']);
      if (forbidden.trim()) fail('MESSAGE_CHANGED', 'Existing mailbox messages were changed or deleted.');
      if (cursor) {
        const changes = (await this.git(['diff-tree', '--no-commit-id', '--no-renames', '--name-status', '-r', '-z', cursor.commit, commit, '--', 'messages/'])).split('\0').filter(Boolean);
        for (let i = 0; i < changes.length; i += 2) if (changes[i] !== 'A') fail('MESSAGE_CHANGED', 'Existing mailbox messages were changed or deleted.');
      }
    }
    const rows = (await this.git(['ls-tree', '-r', '-z', '--long', commit, '--', 'messages/'])).split('\0').filter(Boolean);
    if (rows.length > 10_000) fail('MAILBOX_LIMIT', 'Mailbox history exceeds this worker’s bounded message limit.');
    const messages = new Map(); const writers = new Map(); let totalBytes = 0; let received = 0;
    for (const row of rows) {
      const match = /^(\d+) blob ([a-f0-9]+)\s+(\d+)\t(.+)$/.exec(row);
      if (!match || match[1] !== '100644' || !/^messages\/[a-f0-9-]+\.json$/.test(match[4]) || Number(match[3]) > FILE_LIMIT) fail('INVALID_MESSAGE', 'The mailbox message tree contains an invalid file.');
      totalBytes += Number(match[3]);
      if (totalBytes > 32 * 1024 * 1024) fail('MAILBOX_LIMIT', 'Mailbox messages exceed the supported byte limit.');
      let value; try { value = JSON.parse(await this.git(['cat-file', 'blob', match[2]], { maxBuffer: FILE_LIMIT })); } catch (e) { if (e instanceof GitRelayError) throw e; fail('INVALID_MESSAGE', 'A mailbox message contains invalid JSON.'); }
      validateGitMessage(value);
      if (match[4] !== `messages/${value.id}.json` || messages.has(value.id) || writers.has(writerKey(value))) fail('MESSAGE_CONFLICT', 'Mailbox message identity or writer request IDs are duplicated.');
      messages.set(value.id, value); writers.set(writerKey(value), value);
      const local = resolve(this.config.directory, 'inbox', `${value.id}.json`);
      const existing = await readJSON(local, true);
      if (existing !== undefined && JSON.stringify(existing) !== JSON.stringify(value)) fail('MESSAGE_CHANGED', 'An existing inbox message changed contents.');
      if (existing === undefined) { await atomicJSON(local, value); received++; }
    }
    // Every inbox file and directory entry is durable before advancing the commit cursor.
    await atomicJSON(path, { version: 1, commit });
    return { commit, messages, writers, received };
  }
  async append(snapshot, message) {
    const index = resolve(this.config.directory, `index-${randomUUID()}`);
    const env = { GIT_INDEX_FILE: index, GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: `OMI ${this.config.participantId}`, GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: `${this.config.participantId}@omi.invalid`, GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: `OMI ${this.config.participantId}`, GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: `${this.config.participantId}@omi.invalid` };
    try {
      const blob = (await this.git(['hash-object', '-w', '--stdin'], { input: json(message) })).trim();
      await this.git(['read-tree', snapshot.commit], { env });
      await this.git(['update-index', '--add', '--cacheinfo', '100644', blob, `messages/${message.id}.json`], { env });
      const tree = (await this.git(['write-tree'], { env })).trim();
      return (await this.git(['-c', 'commit.gpgSign=false', 'commit-tree', tree, '-p', snapshot.commit], { env, input: `OMI mailbox append ${message.id}\n` })).trim();
    } finally {
      await fs.unlink(index).catch(e => { if (e.code !== 'ENOENT') throw e; });
      await fs.unlink(`${index}.lock`).catch(e => { if (e.code !== 'ENOENT') throw e; });
    }
  }
  async deliver(request, snapshot) {
    const stable = key(`${this.config.participantId}\0${request.clientMessageId}`);
    const pendingPath = resolve(this.config.directory, 'pending', `${stable}.json`);
    let message = await readJSON(pendingPath, true);
    if (message === undefined) {
      message = { version: 1, id: randomUUID(), participantId: this.config.participantId, ...request, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() };
      await atomicJSON(pendingPath, message);
    } else {
      validateGitMessage(message);
      if (message.participantId !== this.config.participantId || !sameRequest(message, request)) fail('OUTBOX_CONFLICT', 'A pending clientMessageId was reused for different content.');
    }
    let received = 0;
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 5; attempt++) {
      const existing = snapshot.writers.get(writerKey(message));
      if (existing) {
        if (!sameRequest(existing, request)) fail('OUTBOX_CONFLICT', 'A published clientMessageId was reused for different content.');
        return { snapshot, message: existing, received };
      }
      if (snapshot.messages.has(message.id)) fail('MESSAGE_CONFLICT', 'The pending message UUID already exists.');
      const commit = await this.append(snapshot, message);
      try { await this.git(['push', '--porcelain', this.config.remote, `${commit}:refs/heads/${this.config.branch}`]); }
      catch {
        const next = await this.snapshot(); received += next.received;
        if (next.commit === snapshot.commit) fail('GIT_PUSH_FAILED', 'The append was not accepted; no unchanged push retry was attempted.');
        if (!(await this.ancestor(snapshot.commit, next.commit))) fail('HISTORY_REWRITTEN', 'Mailbox history was replaced during a push.');
        snapshot = next;
        continue;
      }
      const next = await this.snapshot(); received += next.received;
      if (!(await this.ancestor(commit, next.commit))) fail('PUSH_UNCONFIRMED', 'The pushed message was not confirmed in mailbox ancestry.');
      snapshot = next;
    }
    const existing = snapshot.writers.get(writerKey(message));
    if (existing && sameRequest(existing, request)) return { snapshot, message: existing, received };
    fail('PUSH_CONTENTION', 'Concurrent writers exceeded the bounded append retry count; outbox retained.');
  }
  async runOnce() {
    await this.initialize(); const unlock = await this.lock();
    try {
      await this.prepareCache();
      let snapshot = await this.snapshot(); let received = snapshot.received; let sent = 0; let rejected = 0;
      const outbox = resolve(this.config.directory, 'outbox');
      const names = (await fs.readdir(outbox)).filter(n => n.endsWith('.json')).sort().slice(0, 100);
      for (const name of names) {
        const path = resolve(outbox, name); let request;
        try { request = outgoing(await readJSON(path)); }
        catch (e) {
          if (!(e instanceof GitRelayError) || !['INVALID_OUTBOX', 'INVALID_STATE'].includes(e.code)) throw e;
          const stat = await fs.lstat(path);
          if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink()) fail('INVALID_STATE', 'Outbox symlinks and special files are refused.');
          await fs.rename(path, resolve(this.config.directory, 'rejected', `${randomUUID()}.json`));
          await syncDir(outbox); await syncDir(resolve(this.config.directory, 'rejected')); rejected++; continue;
        }
        const result = await this.deliver(request, snapshot);
        snapshot = result.snapshot; received += result.received;
        const archive = resolve(this.config.directory, 'sent', `${key(`${this.config.participantId}\0${request.clientMessageId}`)}.json`);
        await atomicJSON(archive, { version: 1, message: result.message, commit: snapshot.commit });
        if (!sameRequest(outgoing(await readJSON(path)), request)) fail('OUTBOX_CHANGED', 'The outbox item changed during delivery and was retained.');
        await fs.unlink(path); await syncDir(outbox); sent++;
      }
      const result = { received, sent, rejected, commit: snapshot.commit };
      this.log({ event: 'git_relay_cycle', ...result }); return result;
    } finally { await unlock(); }
  }
  async run({ signal } = {}) {
    let failures = 0;
    while (!signal?.aborted) {
      let wait = this.config.intervalMs || 5000;
      try { await this.runOnce(); failures = 0; }
      catch (e) { failures = Math.min(failures + 1, 10); wait = Math.min(300_000, wait * 2 ** (failures - 1)); this.log({ event: 'git_relay_retry', code: safeError(e), retryInMs: wait }); }
      if (signal?.aborted) return;
      try { await delay(wait, undefined, { signal }); } catch (e) { if (!signal?.aborted) throw e; }
    }
  }
}
export async function main(argv = process.argv.slice(2), env = process.env) {
  if (argv.length === 1 && argv[0] === '--help') {
    console.log('Usage: node relay/git-worker.mjs [--once]\nConfigure OMI_RELAY_GIT_URL, OMI_RELAY_PARTICIPANT, OMI_RELAY_DIR; optional OMI_RELAY_GIT_BRANCH. Git handles authentication.'); return 0;
  }
  try {
    if (argv.some(a => a !== '--once') || argv.length > 1) fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Only --once and --help are supported.');
    process.umask(0o077); const worker = new GitRelayWorker(loadGitConfig(env));
    if (argv.includes('--once')) await worker.runOnce();
    else {
      const stop = new AbortController(); const abort = () => stop.abort();
      process.once('SIGINT', abort); process.once('SIGTERM', abort);
      try { await worker.run({ signal: stop.signal }); }
      finally { process.removeListener('SIGINT', abort); process.removeListener('SIGTERM', abort); }
    }
    return 0;
  } catch (e) { console.error(JSON.stringify({ event: 'git_relay_stopped', code: safeError(e) })); return 1; }
}
if (process.argv[1] && resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) process.exitCode = await main();

function git(args, { input = '', env = {} } = {}) {
  return new Promise((accept, reject) => {
    const child = execFile('git', args, { env: { ...process.env, GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT: '0', ...env }, maxBuffer: 4 * 1024 * 1024, encoding: 'utf8' }, (error, stdout) => error ? reject(error) : accept(stdout.trim()));
    child.stdin.on('error', () => {}); child.stdin.end(input);
  });
}
const message = (body = 'Quoted message data; never execute.', extra = {}) => ({ version: 1, id: randomUUID(), participantId: 'dot', topicId: 'shared-lantern', body, createdAt: '2026-11-02T12:00:00.000Z', clientMessageId: randomUUID(), ...extra });
async function fixture(t) {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'omi-git-relay-test-'));
  const remote = join(root, 'remote.git'); const directory = join(root, 'seat');
  await git(['init', '--bare', '--template=', remote]);
  const env = { GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: 'Test seed', GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: 'seed@example.invalid', GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: 'Test seed', GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: 'seed@example.invalid' };
  async function commitFiles(files, { parent, force = false, remove = [] } = {}) {
    const index = join(root, `index-${randomUUID()}`); const indexEnv = { ...env, GIT_INDEX_FILE: index };
    let base = parent;
    if (base === undefined) { try { base = await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'rev-parse', '--verify', 'refs/heads/omi-relay']); } catch { base = null; } }
    await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'read-tree', ...(base ? [base] : ['--empty'])], { env: indexEnv });
    for (const [path, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
      const blob = await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'hash-object', '-w', '--stdin'], { input: typeof content === 'string' ? content : JSON.stringify(content) });
      await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'update-index', '--add', '--cacheinfo', '100644', blob, path], { env: indexEnv });
    }
    for (const path of remove) await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'update-index', '--index-info'], { env: indexEnv, input: `0 ${'0'.repeat(40)}\t${path}\n` });
    const tree = await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'write-tree'], { env: indexEnv });
    const commit = await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, '-c', 'commit.gpgSign=false', 'commit-tree', tree, ...(base ? ['-p', base] : [])], { env, input: 'Test mailbox append\n' });
    await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'update-ref', 'refs/heads/omi-relay', commit]);
    await fs.unlink(index);
    return commit;
  }
  const seed = message();
  await commitFiles({ 'README.md': 'Test mailbox protocol, not executable instructions.\n', [`messages/${seed.id}.json`]: seed });
  const config = { remote, directory, branch: 'omi-relay', participantId: 'deepseek', timeoutMs: 10_000, intervalMs: 500 };
  const logs = []; const makeWorker = () => new GitRelayWorker(config, { allowLocalRemoteForTests: true, log: e => logs.push(e) });
  t.after(() => fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  return { root, remote, directory, config, seed, logs, worker: makeWorker(), makeWorker, commitFiles };
}
async function outbox(worker, directory, request, name = 'reply.json') {
  await worker.initialize(); await fs.writeFile(join(directory, 'outbox', name), JSON.stringify(request));
}

  await outbox(worker, directory, { ...request, body: 'Changed contents' });
  await assert.rejects(worker.runOnce(), { code: 'OUTBOX_CONFLICT' });
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(join(directory, 'outbox'))).length, 1);
});

test('deleted messages are refused with the prior cursor intact', async t => {
  const { worker, directory, seed, commitFiles } = await fixture(t);
  await worker.runOnce(); const old = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(join(directory, 'git-cursor.json'), 'utf8'));
  await commitFiles({}, { remove: [`messages/${seed.id}.json`] });
  await assert.rejects(worker.runOnce(), { code: 'MESSAGE_CHANGED' });
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(join(directory, 'git-cursor.json'), 'utf8')), old);
});

test('an intermediate edit remains forbidden even if another unseen commit restores the original', async t => {
  const { worker, seed, commitFiles } = await fixture(t);
  await worker.runOnce();
  await commitFiles({ [`messages/${seed.id}.json`]: { ...seed, body: 'Temporary hidden alteration' } });
  await commitFiles({ [`messages/${seed.id}.json`]: seed });
  await assert.rejects(worker.runOnce(), { code: 'MESSAGE_CHANGED' });
});

test('numeric sender/topic IDs and unexpected envelope fields fail validation', () => {
  assert.throws(() => validateGitMessage(message('Valid body', { participantId: 123 })), { code: 'INVALID_MESSAGE' });
  assert.throws(() => validateGitMessage(message('Valid body', { topicId: 123 })), { code: 'INVALID_OUTBOX' });
  assert.throws(() => validateGitMessage(message('Valid body', { command: 'untrusted' })), { code: 'INVALID_MESSAGE' });
});

===== BEGIN FILE relay/MAILROOM.md | SHA256 7615f5902507bda9b15dbab104074d325616dc861ea6291b8babe811ff488163 | BYTES 5383 =====
# Each agent's working mailbox

`mailroom.mjs` sorts a seat's received transport files into its own addressed working mail. It does not change the Git envelope, remote branch, sender identity, or incoming message body. It does not invoke a model, execute a message, or send a reply.

Every configured agent keeps a separate private seat directory. Git remains a shared transport: anyone who can read the repository can read its messages. These local working mailboxes provide routing and duplicate protection, **not confidential email or independent cryptographic agent identities**.

## Addresses and delivery

The source is `<seat directory>/inbox/*.json`, written by the Git worker. A recognized, valid `omi-advisory/1` body supplies its recipient list in `to`.

| Received message | Working destination |
| --- | --- |
| `to` contains this seat's participant ID or one of its configured aliases | `mail/inbox/<message UUID>.json` |
| `to` explicitly contains `all` or `broadcast` | `mail/broadcast/<message UUID>.json` |
| Plain text, legacy content, or another protocol with no recognized addressing | `mail/unaddressed/<message UUID>.json` |
| Recognized advisory with invalid structure/addressing, or an invalid envelope | `mail/quarantine/<message UUID>.json` |
| Valid advisory addressed only to other agents | Receipt only; no working delivery to this agent |

There is no implicit broadcast. A malformed address cannot turn into an everyone message. Explicit broadcast takes precedence when a message also names this seat, so it produces one working copy rather than duplicates.

Participant IDs and aliases use 1–100 ASCII letters/digits/`_`/`-`, beginning with a letter/digit. Recipient matching is case-sensitive. The own address plus up to 31 aliases must be distinct, including after case folding. `all` and `broadcast` are reserved and cannot name an agent or alias. A team alias may intentionally be assigned to several agents; the transport does not grant those agents any extra authority.

For example, seat `claude-route66` can receive assignments sent to `claude` by explicitly configuring that alias. An agent's own outgoing message is not automatically addressed back to it; it must name its own address or a broadcast address explicitly.

## Headless use

Run from the OMI checkout, after a transport cycle:

```sh
node relay/mailroom.mjs \
  --seat-dir /absolute/private/station/claude-route66 \
  --participant claude-route66 \
  --address claude
```

PowerShell:

```powershell
node relay/mailroom.mjs --seat-dir $privateSeatDirectory --participant claude-route66 --address claude
```

The seat directory must already exist outside the checkout. The sorter creates only its working-mail directories. A first cycle with no raw transport inbox is an empty scan. Its output contains participant ID, counts, and error codes; it never prints message bodies.

Programmatic interface:

```js
import { sortAgentMail } from './relay/mailroom.mjs';
const report = await sortAgentMail({
  seatDirectory: '/absolute/private/station/claude-route66',
  participantId: 'claude-route66',
  addresses: ['claude'],
});
```

The report includes `scanned`, `delivered`, `addressed`, `broadcast`, `unaddressed`, `quarantined`, `notForMe`, `alreadySorted`, and `recoveredReceipts`. Delivery category counts describe newly created files; recovering a missing receipt for an already durable matching file does not count as another delivery.

## Durable processing

The sorter preserves the raw transport inbox. It copies each envelope byte-for-byte to its single working destination, syncs that file, then writes an immutable receipt under `mail/receipts/`. A crash between copy and receipt can recover the receipt without creating another working copy. Existing copies, raw messages, receipts, and routing decisions are checked against their saved hashes; changes stop processing rather than being overwritten or hidden.

`mail/binding.json` binds the own identity and sorted alias set. Changing them requires an explicit reviewed migration; do not reset the binding or discard receipts to hide a routing error. Keep state intact when changing station configuration.

Agent adapters should read `mail/inbox` and explicit `mail/broadcast`, and maintain their own processed-message-ID ledger. Leave sorter-owned files and receipts intact. Deleting a delivered file is treated as a state-integrity error, not a request to redeliver it. Unaddressed messages need an explicit operator or adapter decision. Quarantined messages require review and never become executable instructions.

Private directories use `0700` and files `0600` on POSIX; ownership and modes are checked. Symlink roots, source files, and working destinations are refused. Windows uses the account's private ACL-protected directory, and directory-entry sync availability depends on the OS/filesystem, as described in the [Git transport guide](GIT-MAILBOX.md). Sorting is limited to the transport's 10,000-message archive boundary; it does not silently remove old mail.

## Checks

```sh
node --test relay/mailroom.test.mjs
```

Tests exercise actual filesystem delivery, aliases, explicit broadcasts, legacy/malformed data, retry and partial-receipt recovery, tamper detection, private paths, concurrent sorters, and CLI output. They do not establish that a remote model or a Station Node adapter is connected.

#!/usr/bin/env node
/** Addressed local working mail. Message bodies remain data, never commands. */
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { constants } from 'node:fs';
import * as fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import { dirname, isAbsolute, relative, resolve, sep } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { validateAdvisory } from './advisory.mjs';
import { validateGitMessage } from './git-worker.mjs';

const CHECKOUT = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const UUID = /^[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/;
const SLUG = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,99}$/;
const RESERVED = new Set(['all', 'broadcast']);
const DESTINATIONS = ['inbox', 'broadcast', 'unaddressed', 'quarantine'];
const FILE_LIMIT = 256 * 1024;
const own = value => typeof value === 'string' && SLUG.test(value) && !RESERVED.has(value.toLowerCase());
const inside = (path, root) => { const value = relative(root, path); return !value || (!isAbsolute(value) && value !== '..' && !value.startsWith(`..${sep}`)); };
const digest = bytes => createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
const json = value => Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) + '\n');

export class MailroomError extends Error {
  constructor(code, message) { super(message); this.name = 'MailroomError'; this.code = code; }
}
const fail = (code, message) => { throw new MailroomError(code, message); };

function identity(participantId, addresses) {
  if (!own(participantId) || !Array.isArray(addresses) || addresses.length > 31 || addresses.some(value => !own(value))) {
    fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Use a participant ID and at most 31 aliases; all/broadcast are reserved destinations.');
  }
  const values = [participantId, ...addresses];
  if (new Set(values.map(value => value.toLowerCase())).size !== values.length) fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Mail addresses must be distinct.');
  return values.sort();
}
async function privateDirectory(path, create = false) {
  if (create) await fs.mkdir(path, { mode: 0o700 }).catch(error => { if (error.code !== 'EEXIST') throw error; });
  const metadata = await fs.lstat(path);
  if (!metadata.isDirectory() || metadata.isSymbolicLink() || (process.getuid && (metadata.uid !== process.getuid() || (metadata.mode & 0o077) !== 0))) {
    fail('UNSAFE_DIRECTORY', 'Mail directories must be real and owner-private.');
  }
  return fs.realpath(path);
}
async function syncDirectory(path) {
  let handle;
  try { handle = await fs.open(path, 'r'); await handle.sync(); }
  catch (error) {
    if (process.platform !== 'win32' || !['EPERM', 'EACCES', 'EINVAL', 'EISDIR'].includes(error.code)) throw error;
  } finally { await handle?.close(); }
}
async function readPrivate(path, optional = false) {
  let metadata;
  try { metadata = await fs.lstat(path); }
  catch (error) { if (optional && error.code === 'ENOENT') return null; throw error; }
  if (!metadata.isFile() || metadata.isSymbolicLink() || metadata.size > FILE_LIMIT || (process.getuid && (metadata.uid !== process.getuid() || (metadata.mode & 0o077) !== 0))) {
    fail('UNSAFE_FILE', 'Mail files must be bounded owner-private regular files.');
  }
  const handle = await fs.open(path, constants.O_RDONLY | (constants.O_NOFOLLOW ?? 0));
  try {
    const opened = await handle.stat();
    if (!opened.isFile() || opened.size > FILE_LIMIT || (process.getuid && (opened.uid !== process.getuid() || (opened.mode & 0o077) !== 0))) fail('UNSAFE_FILE', 'Mail file changed while opening.');
    const bytes = await handle.readFile();
    if (bytes.length > FILE_LIMIT) fail('UNSAFE_FILE', 'Mail file is too large.');
    return bytes;
  } finally { await handle.close(); }
}
function parse(bytes) {
  try { return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(bytes)); }
  catch { fail('INVALID_STATE', 'A mail state file contains invalid UTF-8 JSON.'); }
}
async function immutableWrite(path, bytes) {
  const existing = await readPrivate(path, true);
  if (existing) {
    if (!existing.equals(bytes)) fail('MAIL_CONTENT_CHANGED', 'An existing mail file differs from its immutable source.');
    return false;
  }
  const temporary = path + '.' + randomUUID() + '.tmp';
  try {
    const handle = await fs.open(temporary, 'wx', 0o600);
    try { await handle.writeFile(bytes); await handle.sync(); }
    finally { await handle.close(); }
    // A private sorter lock serializes writers. The destination is rechecked
    // immediately before rename so an unexpected local file is never replaced.
    const changed = await readPrivate(path, true);
    if (changed) {
      if (!changed.equals(bytes)) fail('MAIL_CONTENT_CHANGED', 'A mail destination changed before its durable commit.');
      return false;
    }
    await fs.rename(temporary, path);
    await syncDirectory(dirname(path));
    return true;
  } finally { await fs.unlink(temporary).catch(error => { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; }); }
}
async function acquireLock(mail) {
  const path = resolve(mail, 'sorting.lock');
  const nonce = randomUUID();
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
    try {
      const handle = await fs.open(path, 'wx', 0o600);
      try { await handle.writeFile(json({ pid: process.pid, nonce })); await handle.sync(); }
      catch (error) { await fs.unlink(path).catch(() => {}); throw error; }
      finally { await handle.close(); }
      return async () => {
        const bytes = await readPrivate(path, true);
        if (!bytes) return;
        if (parse(bytes).nonce !== nonce) fail('MAIL_LOCK_CHANGED', 'The mail sorting lock changed unexpectedly.');
        await fs.unlink(path); await syncDirectory(mail);
      };
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.code !== 'EEXIST') throw error;
      let previousBytes;
      try { previousBytes = await readPrivate(path); }
      catch (probe) { if (probe.code === 'ENOENT') continue; throw probe; }
      const previous = parse(previousBytes);
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(previous?.pid) || previous.pid < 1) fail('MAIL_LOCKED', 'A malformed mail sorting lock requires review.');
      try { process.kill(previous.pid, 0); }
      catch (probe) {
        if (probe.code === 'ESRCH') { await fs.unlink(path); await syncDirectory(mail); continue; }
      }
      fail('MAIL_LOCKED', 'Another sorter owns this agent mailroom.');
    }
  }
  fail('MAIL_LOCKED', 'The mailroom could not be locked.');
}
function classify(message, addresses) {
  let payload;
  try { payload = JSON.parse(message.body); } catch { return { classification: 'unaddressed', reason: 'NO_ADDRESSING_PROTOCOL' }; }
  if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object' || Array.isArray(payload) || payload.protocol !== 'omi-advisory/1') return { classification: 'unaddressed', reason: 'NO_ADDRESSING_PROTOCOL' };
  let advisory;
  try { advisory = validateAdvisory(payload); }
  catch { return { classification: 'quarantine', reason: 'INVALID_ADVISORY' }; }
  if (advisory.to.some(value => RESERVED.has(value.toLowerCase()))) return { classification: 'broadcast', reason: 'EXPLICIT_BROADCAST' };
  if (advisory.to.some(value => addresses.includes(value))) return { classification: 'inbox', reason: 'EXPLICIT_RECIPIENT' };
  return { classification: 'not-for-me', reason: 'OTHER_RECIPIENT' };
}

/**
 * Sort immutable transport envelopes into one local seat's working mail. Shared
 * repository readers can still read raw transport: this is routing, not secrecy.
 */
export async function sortAgentMail({ seatDirectory, participantId, addresses = [] }) {
  const boundAddresses = identity(participantId, addresses);
  if (typeof seatDirectory !== 'string' || !isAbsolute(seatDirectory) || inside(resolve(seatDirectory), CHECKOUT)) fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Use a private absolute seat directory outside the checkout.');
  const seat = await privateDirectory(resolve(seatDirectory));
  if (inside(seat, await fs.realpath(CHECKOUT))) fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Seat data must remain outside the checkout.');
  const mail = resolve(seat, 'mail');
  await privateDirectory(mail, true);
  for (const name of [...DESTINATIONS, 'receipts']) await privateDirectory(resolve(mail, name), true);
  const unlock = await acquireLock(mail);
  try {
    const binding = { version: 1, participantId, addresses: boundAddresses };
    const bindingPath = resolve(mail, 'binding.json');
    const previousBinding = await readPrivate(bindingPath, true);
    if (previousBinding) {
      if (JSON.stringify(parse(previousBinding)) !== JSON.stringify(binding)) fail('MAIL_BINDING_CHANGED', 'The agent identity or address set changed; use an explicit reviewed migration.');
    } else await immutableWrite(bindingPath, json(binding));
    const report = { scanned: 0, delivered: 0, addressed: 0, broadcast: 0, unaddressed: 0, quarantined: 0, notForMe: 0, alreadySorted: 0, recoveredReceipts: 0 };
    const input = resolve(seat, 'inbox');
    try { await privateDirectory(input); }
    catch (error) { if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return report; throw error; }
    const names = (await fs.readdir(input)).filter(name => name.endsWith('.json')).sort();
    if (names.length > 10_000) fail('MAIL_LIMIT', 'Review an explicit mailbox archive before sorting more than 10,000 messages.');
    for (const name of names) {
      const id = name.slice(0, -5);
      if (!UUID.test(id)) fail('INVALID_FILENAME', 'Transport inbox JSON filenames must be message UUIDs.');
      const bytes = await readPrivate(resolve(input, name));
      report.scanned++;
      const contentHash = digest(bytes);
      let route;
      try {
        const message = validateGitMessage(parse(bytes));
        if (message.id !== id) fail('INVALID_MESSAGE', 'Envelope ID differs from filename.');
        route = classify(message, boundAddresses);
      } catch {
        route = { classification: 'quarantine', reason: 'INVALID_ENVELOPE' };
      }
      const destination = route.classification === 'not-for-me' ? null : `${route.classification}/${name}`;
      const receiptPath = resolve(mail, 'receipts', name);
      const existingReceipt = await readPrivate(receiptPath, true);
      const expected = { version: 1, messageId: id, contentHash, classification: route.classification, reason: route.reason, destination };
      for (const folder of DESTINATIONS) {
        const other = `${folder}/${name}`;
        if (other !== destination && await readPrivate(resolve(mail, other), true)) fail('MAIL_ROUTE_CHANGED', 'A message is already present in a different working-mail folder.');
      }
      if (existingReceipt) {
        const receipt = parse(existingReceipt);
        if (JSON.stringify(receipt) !== JSON.stringify(expected)) fail('MAIL_RECEIPT_CHANGED', 'A sorted message, receipt, or route differs from the immutable source.');
        if (destination) {
          const delivered = await readPrivate(resolve(mail, destination), true);
          if (!delivered || !delivered.equals(bytes)) fail('MAIL_CONTENT_CHANGED', 'Delivered working mail is missing or changed.');
        }
        report.alreadySorted++;
        continue;
      }
      if (destination) {
        const created = await immutableWrite(resolve(mail, destination), bytes);
        if (created) {
          report.delivered++;
          report[{ inbox: 'addressed', broadcast: 'broadcast', unaddressed: 'unaddressed', quarantine: 'quarantined' }[route.classification]]++;
        } else report.recoveredReceipts++;
      } else report.notForMe++;
      // The delivered file is durable before this receipt records completion.
      await immutableWrite(receiptPath, json(expected));
    }
    return report;
  } finally { await unlock(); }
}

export async function main(argv = process.argv.slice(2)) {
  if (argv.includes('--help')) { console.log('Usage: node relay/mailroom.mjs --seat-dir /private/seat --participant seat-id [--address alias ...]'); return; }
  let seatDirectory; let participantId; const addresses = [];
  for (let index = 0; index < argv.length; index++) {
    const name = argv[index];
    if (name === '--once') continue;
    if (!['--seat-dir', '--participant', '--address'].includes(name) || !argv[index + 1] || argv[index + 1].startsWith('--')) fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Supply seat-dir, participant, and optional address values.');
    const value = argv[++index];
    if (name === '--address') addresses.push(value);
    else if (name === '--seat-dir') { if (seatDirectory !== undefined) fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Duplicate seat-dir.'); seatDirectory = value; }
    else { if (participantId !== undefined) fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Duplicate participant.'); participantId = value; }
  }
  const report = await sortAgentMail({ seatDirectory, participantId, addresses });
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ event: 'agent_mail_sorted', participantId, ...report }));
}
if (process.argv[1] && resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  main().catch(error => { console.error(JSON.stringify({ event: 'agent_mail_error', code: error instanceof MailroomError ? error.code : 'LOCAL_IO_ERROR' })); process.exitCode = 1; });
}

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { execFile } from 'node:child_process';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import * as fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { dirname, join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { promisify } from 'node:util';
import test from 'node:test';
import { sortAgentMail } from './mailroom.mjs';

const execute = promisify(execFile);
async function fixture(t) {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'omi-mailroom-'));
  const seatDirectory = join(root, 'seat');
  await fs.mkdir(seatDirectory, { mode: 0o700 });
  await fs.mkdir(join(seatDirectory, 'inbox'), { mode: 0o700 });
  t.after(() => fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  return { root, seatDirectory, participantId: 'claude-route66', addresses: ['claude'] };
}
function advisory(to, changes = {}) {
  return { protocol: 'omi-advisory/1', id: 'test-' + randomUUID(), kind: 'assignment', from: 'dot', to,
    summary: 'Review a local component.', details: { task: 'Read and report; no command execution.' }, createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), requiresAck: true, ...changes };
}
async function insert(config, payload, changes = {}) {
  const message = { version: 1, id: randomUUID(), participantId: 'dot', topicId: 'coordination-advisory',
    body: typeof payload === 'string' ? payload : JSON.stringify(payload), createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), clientMessageId: randomUUID(), ...changes };
  const path = join(config.seatDirectory, 'inbox', message.id + '.json');
  const bytes = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(message, null, 2) + '\n');
  await fs.writeFile(path, bytes, { mode: 0o600 });
  return { message, bytes, path, name: message.id + '.json' };
}
const mailPath = (config, folder, name) => join(config.seatDirectory, 'mail', folder, name);
async function exists(path) { try { await fs.lstat(path); return true; } catch (error) { if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return false; throw error; } }

test('sorts addressed, alias, explicit broadcast, unaddressed and malformed advisory mail without changing raw transport', async t => {
  const config = await fixture(t);
  const direct = await insert(config, advisory(['claude-route66']));
  const alias = await insert(config, advisory(['claude']));
  const other = await insert(config, advisory(['deepseek']));
  const broadcast = await insert(config, advisory(['all']));
  const plain = await insert(config, 'An unaddressed legacy report.');
  const unknown = await insert(config, { protocol: 'future-protocol/1', to: ['claude'] });
  const malformed = await insert(config, advisory([]));
  const result = await sortAgentMail(config);
  assert.deepEqual(result, { scanned: 7, delivered: 6, addressed: 2, broadcast: 1, unaddressed: 2, quarantined: 1, notForMe: 1, alreadySorted: 0, recoveredReceipts: 0 });
  for (const [item, folder] of [[direct, 'inbox'], [alias, 'inbox'], [broadcast, 'broadcast'], [plain, 'unaddressed'], [unknown, 'unaddressed'], [malformed, 'quarantine']]) {
    assert.deepEqual(await fs.readFile(mailPath(config, folder, item.name)), item.bytes);
    assert.deepEqual(await fs.readFile(item.path), item.bytes);
  }
  for (const folder of ['inbox', 'broadcast', 'unaddressed', 'quarantine']) assert.equal(await exists(mailPath(config, folder, other.name)), false);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(join(config.seatDirectory, 'inbox'))).length, 7);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(mailPath(config, 'receipts', ''))).length, 7);
  assert.equal((await fs.stat(mailPath(config, 'inbox', direct.name))).mode & 0o777, 0o600);
  assert.equal((await fs.stat(mailPath(config, 'inbox', ''))).mode & 0o777, 0o700);
  const second = await sortAgentMail(config);
  assert.equal(second.delivered, 0); assert.equal(second.alreadySorted, 7);
});

test('broadcast is explicit, has one destination, and own authored messages do not implicitly become addressed mail', async t => {
  const config = await fixture(t);
  const both = await insert(config, advisory(['claude', 'broadcast']), { participantId: config.participantId });
  const mine = await insert(config, advisory(['claude']), { participantId: config.participantId });
  const other = await insert(config, advisory(['deepseek']), { participantId: config.participantId });
  const plain = await insert(config, 'My unaddressed note.', { participantId: config.participantId });
  const result = await sortAgentMail(config);
  assert.equal(result.addressed, 1); assert.equal(result.broadcast, 1); assert.equal(result.notForMe, 1); assert.equal(result.unaddressed, 1);
  assert(await exists(mailPath(config, 'broadcast', both.name)));
  assert.equal(await exists(mailPath(config, 'inbox', both.name)), false);
  assert(await exists(mailPath(config, 'inbox', mine.name)));
  assert.equal(await exists(mailPath(config, 'inbox', other.name)), false);
  assert(await exists(mailPath(config, 'unaddressed', plain.name)));
});

test('malformed recognized recipient lists are quarantined rather than treated as broadcasts', async t => {
  const config = await fixture(t);
  for (const to of [[], '*', ['*'], ['claude', 'CLAUDE'], [123], ['../escape']]) await insert(config, advisory(to));
  const result = await sortAgentMail(config);
  assert.equal(result.quarantined, 6); assert.equal(result.broadcast, 0); assert.equal(result.addressed, 0);
});

test('invalid envelope data is retained as quarantine without being executed or rewritten', async t => {
  const config = await fixture(t);
  const item = await insert(config, 'process.exit(99); never run this.');
  const invalid = { ...item.message, id: randomUUID() };
  const bytes = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(invalid));
  await fs.writeFile(item.path, bytes);
  const result = await sortAgentMail(config);
  assert.equal(result.quarantined, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.readFile(mailPath(config, 'quarantine', item.name)), bytes);
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.readFile(item.path), bytes);
});

test('a durable copied file with a missing receipt recovers without a duplicate delivery file', async t => {
  const config = await fixture(t); const item = await insert(config, advisory(['claude']));
  await sortAgentMail(config);
  const destination = mailPath(config, 'inbox', item.name);
  const before = await fs.readFile(destination);
  await fs.unlink(mailPath(config, 'receipts', item.name));
  const result = await sortAgentMail(config);
  assert.equal(result.delivered, 0); assert.equal(result.recoveredReceipts, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.readFile(destination), before);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(mailPath(config, 'inbox', ''))).length, 1);
  assert(await exists(mailPath(config, 'receipts', item.name)));
});

test('raw source, delivered file, receipt and deletion tampering stop instead of silently redelivering', async t => {
  for (const change of ['source', 'destination', 'receipt', 'delete']) {
    await t.test(change, async sub => {
      const config = await fixture(sub); const item = await insert(config, advisory(['claude']));
      await sortAgentMail(config);
      if (change === 'source') await fs.writeFile(item.path, JSON.stringify({ ...item.message, body: 'Changed raw source.' }));
      if (change === 'destination') await fs.writeFile(mailPath(config, 'inbox', item.name), 'Changed copy.');
      if (change === 'receipt') {
        const receipt = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(mailPath(config, 'receipts', item.name), 'utf8')); receipt.contentHash = '0'.repeat(64);
        await fs.writeFile(mailPath(config, 'receipts', item.name), JSON.stringify(receipt));
      }
      if (change === 'delete') await fs.unlink(mailPath(config, 'inbox', item.name));
      await assert.rejects(sortAgentMail(config), error => ['MAIL_ROUTE_CHANGED', 'MAIL_RECEIPT_CHANGED', 'MAIL_CONTENT_CHANGED'].includes(error.code));
    });
  }
});

test('identity and alias bindings are fixed and a changed alias cannot replay old mail under a new route', async t => {
  const config = await fixture(t); await insert(config, advisory(['deepseek']));
  await sortAgentMail(config);
  await assert.rejects(sortAgentMail({ ...config, addresses: ['deepseek'] }), { code: 'MAIL_BINDING_CHANGED' });
  await assert.rejects(sortAgentMail({ ...config, participantId: 'muse' }), { code: 'MAIL_BINDING_CHANGED' });
  for (const changes of [{ participantId: '../escape' }, { participantId: 'all' }, { addresses: ['broadcast'] }, { addresses: ['claude', 'CLAUDE'] }, { addresses: [config.participantId] }, { addresses: [123] }]) {
    await assert.rejects(sortAgentMail({ ...config, ...changes }), { code: 'INVALID_CONFIG' });
  }
  await assert.rejects(sortAgentMail({ ...config, seatDirectory: resolve('relay/private-seat') }), { code: 'INVALID_CONFIG' });
});

test('unsafe directories and symlink files are refused without changing their targets', async t => {
  const config = await fixture(t); const item = await insert(config, advisory(['claude']));
  const link = join(config.root, 'linked-seat'); await fs.symlink(config.seatDirectory, link);
  await assert.rejects(sortAgentMail({ ...config, seatDirectory: link }), { code: 'UNSAFE_DIRECTORY' });
  const original = join(config.root, 'original.json'); await fs.rename(item.path, original); await fs.symlink(original, item.path);
  await assert.rejects(sortAgentMail(config), { code: 'UNSAFE_FILE' });
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.readFile(original), item.bytes);
  if (process.getuid) {
    await fs.unlink(item.path); await fs.rename(original, item.path); await fs.chmod(config.seatDirectory, 0o755);
    await assert.rejects(sortAgentMail(config), { code: 'UNSAFE_DIRECTORY' });
    await fs.chmod(config.seatDirectory, 0o700);
  }
});

test('concurrent sorts create each working delivery only once and leave no live lock or temporary residue', async t => {
  const config = await fixture(t); await insert(config, advisory(['claude']));
  const results = await Promise.allSettled([sortAgentMail(config), sortAgentMail(config)]);
  const fulfilled = results.filter(result => result.status === 'fulfilled').map(result => result.value);
  assert(fulfilled.length >= 1);
  for (const result of results.filter(result => result.status === 'rejected')) assert.equal(result.reason.code, 'MAIL_LOCKED');
  assert.equal(fulfilled.reduce((sum, report) => sum + report.delivered, 0), 1);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(mailPath(config, 'inbox', ''))).length, 1);
  assert.equal(await exists(join(config.seatDirectory, 'mail', 'sorting.lock')), false);
  for (const folder of ['inbox', 'broadcast', 'unaddressed', 'quarantine', 'receipts']) assert((await fs.readdir(mailPath(config, folder, ''))).every(name => name.endsWith('.json')));
});

test('a missing raw inbox is an empty first cycle, and old local locks can recover safely', async t => {
  const config = await fixture(t); await fs.rmdir(join(config.seatDirectory, 'inbox'));
  const empty = await sortAgentMail(config); assert.equal(empty.scanned, 0);
  const lock = join(config.seatDirectory, 'mail', 'sorting.lock');
  await fs.writeFile(lock, JSON.stringify({ pid: 2147483647, nonce: 'terminated' }), { mode: 0o600 });
  assert.equal((await sortAgentMail(config)).scanned, 0);
  await fs.writeFile(lock, JSON.stringify({ pid: process.pid, nonce: 'live' }), { mode: 0o600 });
  await assert.rejects(sortAgentMail(config), { code: 'MAIL_LOCKED' });
  assert(await exists(lock));
});

test('a copy in another route is detected rather than silently deleted or leaked into an agent inbox', async t => {
  const config = await fixture(t); const item = await insert(config, advisory(['deepseek']));
  await sortAgentMail(config);
  await fs.writeFile(mailPath(config, 'inbox', item.name), item.bytes, { mode: 0o600 });
  await assert.rejects(sortAgentMail(config), { code: 'MAIL_ROUTE_CHANGED' });
  assert(await exists(mailPath(config, 'inbox', item.name)));
});

test('CLI sorts actual mail and prints counts instead of message contents', async t => {
  const config = await fixture(t);
  const marker = 'PRIVATE-TEST-BODY-MARKER-NOT-A-CREDENTIAL';
  await insert(config, advisory(['claude'], { summary: marker }));
  const result = await execute(process.execPath, ['relay/mailroom.mjs', '--seat-dir', config.seatDirectory, '--participant', config.participantId, '--address', 'claude'], { cwd: resolve('.') });
  const report = JSON.parse(result.stdout);
  assert.equal(report.event, 'agent_mail_sorted'); assert.equal(report.addressed, 1);
  assert.equal((result.stdout + result.stderr).includes(marker), false);
});
