test('one failed seat does not stop others, backs off across cycles, and recovers', async t => {
  const { config } = await fixture(t); let now = 1_000_000; const calls = { deepseek: 0, claude: 0 }; const logs = [];
  const supervisor = new StationSupervisor(config, { mailSorter: async () => ({}),
    now: () => now, log: event => logs.push(event),
    workerFactory: seat => ({ runOnce: async () => {
      calls[seat.participantId]++;
      if (seat.participantId === 'deepseek' && calls.deepseek <= 2) throw new Error('secret token and private body must never be printed');
      return { received: 0, sent: 1, rejected: 0 };
    } }),
  });
  let result = await supervisor.runOnce(); assert.equal(result.failed, 1); assert.equal(result.completed, 1);
  result = await supervisor.runOnce(); assert.equal(result.backoff, 1); assert.equal(calls.deepseek, 1); assert.equal(calls.claude, 2);
  now += 15000; result = await supervisor.runOnce(); assert.equal(result.failed, 1);
  assert.equal(result.participants.find(p => p.participantId === 'deepseek').retryInMs, 30000);
  now += 15000; result = await supervisor.runOnce(); assert.equal(result.backoff, 1);
  now += 15000; result = await supervisor.runOnce(); assert.equal(result.completed, 2);
  assert.equal(calls.deepseek, 3); assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(logs).includes('secret token'));
});

test('the production default sorter delivers addressed advisories to working mail and leaves raw input unchanged', async t => {
  const { config } = await fixture(t, { participants: [{ id: 'claude-route66', addresses: ['claude'] }] });
  const id = randomUUID();
  const advisory = {
    protocol: 'omi-advisory/1', id: 'station-integration-test', kind: 'assignment', from: 'dot', to: ['claude'],
    taskId: 'QA-CLAUDE-001', summary: 'Review a bounded assigned task.', details: { target: 'test fixture' },
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), requiresAck: true,
  };
  const envelope = { version: 1, id, participantId: 'dot', topicId: 'coordination-advisory', body: JSON.stringify(advisory), createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), clientMessageId: 'default-sorter-fixture' };
  const raw = JSON.stringify(envelope, null, 2) + '\n';
  const supervisor = new StationSupervisor(config, {
    workerFactory: worker => ({ runOnce: async () => {
      await fs.mkdir(join(worker.directory, 'inbox'), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
      await fs.writeFile(join(worker.directory, 'inbox', `${id}.json`), raw, { mode: 0o600 }); return { received: 1 };
    } }), log: () => {},
  });
  const first = await supervisor.runOnce();
  assert.equal(first.completed, 1); assert.equal(first.participants[0].mail.addressed, 1);
  const directory = join(config.directory, 'claude-route66');
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(join(directory, 'mail', 'inbox', `${id}.json`), 'utf8'), raw);
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(join(directory, 'inbox', `${id}.json`), 'utf8'), raw);
  assert.equal((await supervisor.runOnce()).participants[0].mail.alreadySorted, 1);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(join(directory, 'mail', 'inbox'))).length, 1);
});

===== BEGIN FILE relay/worker.mjs | SHA256 5275cadd4bcc6add9e9eb40ae975d4086f848849e3b809092c25317a708c9e90 | BYTES 22267 =====
#!/usr/bin/env node
/** Outbound forum transport only. Forum text is data, never executable instructions. */
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { closeSync, constants, fstatSync, lstatSync, openSync, readFileSync, realpathSync } from 'node:fs';
import * as fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import { dirname, isAbsolute, relative, resolve, sep } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { setTimeout as delay } from 'node:timers/promises';

const CHECKOUT = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const MAX_FILE_BYTES = 256 * 1024;
const MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_BACKOFF_MS = 300_000;
const MAX_PAGES = 10;
const VALID_ID = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,99}$/;
const INVALID_BODY_CONTROLS = /[\u0000-\u0008\u000b\u000c\u000e-\u001f\u007f]/;

export class RelayError extends Error {
  constructor(code, message, { status, retryAfterMs } = {}) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'RelayError';
    this.code = code;
    this.status = status;
    this.retryAfterMs = retryAfterMs;
  }
}

const fail = (code, message) => { throw new RelayError(code, message); };
const inside = (path, root) => {
  const suffix = relative(root, path);
  return suffix === '' || (!suffix.startsWith(`..${sep}`) && suffix !== '..' && !isAbsolute(suffix));
};
const isObject = value => value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
const text = (value, max = 256) => typeof value === 'string' && value.length > 0 && value.length <= max;
const validId = value => typeof value === 'string' && VALID_ID.test(value);
const keyFor = value => createHash('sha256').update(value).digest('hex');
const asJSON = value => `${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`;
const sameRequest = (a, b) => a.topicId === b.topicId && a.body === b.body && a.clientMessageId === b.clientMessageId;

function boundedInteger(value, fallback, min, max, name) {
  if (value === undefined || value === '') return fallback;
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(number) || number < min || number > max) {
    fail('INVALID_CONFIG', `${name} is outside its supported bounds.`);
  }
  return number;
}

function loadCredentialFile(path) {
  if (!text(path, 4096) || !isAbsolute(path) || inside(resolve(path), CHECKOUT)) {
    fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'OMI_RELAY_TOKEN_FILE must be an absolute private file outside the checkout.');
  }
  let descriptor;
  try {
    const link = lstatSync(path);
    if (!link.isFile() || link.isSymbolicLink() || inside(realpathSync(path), CHECKOUT)) {
      fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'The participant credential must be a regular file without a symbolic link.');
    }
    descriptor = openSync(path, constants.O_RDONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW);
    const stat = fstatSync(descriptor);
    if (!stat.isFile() || stat.size > MAX_FILE_BYTES || (stat.mode & 0o077) !== 0 ||
        (process.getuid && stat.uid !== process.getuid())) {
      fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'The participant credential file must be bounded and accessible only to its owner.');
    }
    let credential;
    try { credential = JSON.parse(readFileSync(descriptor, 'utf8')); }
    catch { fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'The participant credential file must contain valid JSON.'); }
    if (!isObject(credential) || !text(credential.token, 8192) || !isObject(credential.participant) || !validId(credential.participant.id)) {
      fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'The participant credential file must contain participant.id and token.');
    }
    return { token: credential.token, expectedParticipantId: credential.participant.id };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof RelayError) throw error;
    fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'The participant credential file could not be read securely.');
  } finally { if (descriptor !== undefined) closeSync(descriptor); }
}

/** Read secret values only to use them; callers must never log the returned object. */
export function loadConfig(env = process.env) {
  let url;
  try { url = new URL(env.OMI_RELAY_URL); } catch { fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'OMI_RELAY_URL must be a valid HTTPS origin.'); }
  const localhost = ['localhost', '127.0.0.1', '[::1]'].includes(url.hostname);
  if (url.protocol !== 'https:' && !(url.protocol === 'http:' && localhost && env.OMI_RELAY_ALLOW_HTTP_LOCALHOST === 'true')) {
    fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'HTTPS is required; HTTP localhost requires explicit local-test permission.');
  }
  if (url.username || url.password || url.search || url.hash || url.pathname !== '/') {
    fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'OMI_RELAY_URL must contain only an origin, without credentials, path, query, or fragment.');
  }
  if (env.OMI_RELAY_TOKEN && env.OMI_RELAY_TOKEN_FILE) {
    fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Choose OMI_RELAY_TOKEN or OMI_RELAY_TOKEN_FILE, not both.');
  }
  const credential = env.OMI_RELAY_TOKEN_FILE
    ? loadCredentialFile(env.OMI_RELAY_TOKEN_FILE)
    : { token: env.OMI_RELAY_TOKEN };
  if (!text(credential.token, 8192) || /[\s\x00-\x1f\x7f]/.test(credential.token)) {
    fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Supply a participant token through the environment or a private credential file.');
  }
  if (!text(env.OMI_RELAY_DIR, 4096) || !isAbsolute(env.OMI_RELAY_DIR)) {
    fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'OMI_RELAY_DIR must be an absolute private directory outside the checkout.');
  }
  const directory = resolve(env.OMI_RELAY_DIR);
  if (inside(directory, CHECKOUT)) fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Relay state must be kept outside the source checkout.');
  return {
    origin: url.origin,
    ...credential,
    directory,
    intervalMs: boundedInteger(env.OMI_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS, 5000, 500, MAX_BACKOFF_MS, 'OMI_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS'),
    timeoutMs: boundedInteger(env.OMI_RELAY_TIMEOUT_MS, 15_000, 100, 120_000, 'OMI_RELAY_TIMEOUT_MS'),
  };
}

async function regularFile(path, { optional = false } = {}) {
  let stat;
  try { stat = await fs.lstat(path); } catch (error) {
    if (optional && error.code === 'ENOENT') return null;
    throw error;
  }
  if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || stat.size > MAX_FILE_BYTES) {
    fail('INVALID_STATE', 'Relay data must be bounded regular files; symbolic links are refused.');
  }
  return stat;
}

async function readJSON(path, { optional = false } = {}) {
  if (!(await regularFile(path, { optional }))) return undefined;
  const handle = await fs.open(path, constants.O_RDONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW);
  try {
    const raw = await handle.readFile('utf8');
    if (Buffer.byteLength(raw) > MAX_FILE_BYTES) fail('INVALID_STATE', 'Relay data file is too large.');
    try { return JSON.parse(raw); } catch { fail('INVALID_STATE', 'Relay data contains invalid JSON.'); }
  } finally { await handle.close(); }
}

async function syncDirectory(path) {
  const handle = await fs.open(path, constants.O_RDONLY);
  try { await handle.sync(); } finally { await handle.close(); }
}

async function atomicJSON(path, value) {
  await regularFile(path, { optional: true });
  const temporary = `${path}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
  const handle = await fs.open(temporary, 'wx', 0o600);
  try {
    await handle.writeFile(asJSON(value));
    await handle.sync();
  } finally { await handle.close(); }
  try {
    await fs.rename(temporary, path);
    await syncDirectory(dirname(path));
  } finally { await fs.unlink(temporary).catch(error => { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; }); }
}

function validateMessage(value) {
  if (!isObject(value) || !validId(value.id) || !Number.isSafeInteger(value.sequence) || value.sequence < 1 ||
      !validId(value.topicId) || !validId(value.participantId) || !text(value.body, 12_000) || INVALID_BODY_CONTROLS.test(value.body) ||
      !text(value.createdAt, 128) || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value.createdAt)) || !validId(value.clientMessageId)) {
    fail('INVALID_RESPONSE', 'The forum returned an invalid message envelope.');
  }
  // Select only documented fields rather than persisting arbitrary server properties.
  return Object.fromEntries(['id', 'sequence', 'topicId', 'participantId', 'body', 'createdAt', 'clientMessageId'].map(key => [key, value[key]]));
}

function validateOutgoing(value) {
  if (!isObject(value) || !validId(value.topicId) || typeof value.body !== 'string' ||
      !text(value.body.trim(), 12_000) || INVALID_BODY_CONTROLS.test(value.body.trim()) || !validId(value.clientMessageId) ||
      Object.keys(value).some(key => !['topicId', 'body', 'clientMessageId'].includes(key))) {
    fail('INVALID_OUTBOX', 'An outbox item must contain only topicId, body, and a stable clientMessageId.');
  }
  // The forum normalizes boundary whitespace before hashing/storing requests.
  return { topicId: value.topicId, body: value.body.trim(), clientMessageId: value.clientMessageId };
}

async function readResponse(response) {
  const reader = response.body?.getReader();
  if (!reader) fail('INVALID_RESPONSE', 'The forum returned no response body.');
  const chunks = [];
  let bytes = 0;
  try {
    while (true) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      bytes += value.byteLength;
      if (bytes > MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES) fail('INVALID_RESPONSE', 'The forum response exceeds its size limit.');
      chunks.push(Buffer.from(value));
    }
  } finally { await reader.cancel().catch(() => {}); }
  try { return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8')); }
  catch { fail('INVALID_RESPONSE', 'The forum returned invalid JSON.'); }
}

function safeError(error) {
  // Never expose arbitrary exception messages, URLs, response bodies, or headers.
  return {
    code: error instanceof RelayError ? error.code : 'LOCAL_IO_ERROR',
    ...(error instanceof RelayError && error.status ? { httpStatus: error.status } : {}),
  };
}

export class RelayWorker {
  constructor(config, { fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch, log = event => console.log(JSON.stringify(event)) } = {}) {
    this.config = config;
    this.fetch = fetchImpl;
    this.log = log;
  }

  async initialize() {
    const root = this.config.directory;
    await fs.mkdir(root, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
    const stat = await fs.lstat(root);
    const actual = await fs.realpath(root);
    if (!stat.isDirectory() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || inside(actual, CHECKOUT) || (stat.mode & 0o077) !== 0 ||
        (process.getuid && stat.uid !== process.getuid())) {
      fail('INVALID_STATE', 'Use an owner-only relay directory outside the checkout, without symbolic links.');
    }
    for (const name of ['inbox', 'outbox', 'sent', 'rejected']) {
      const path = resolve(root, name);
      await fs.mkdir(path, { mode: 0o700 }).catch(error => { if (error.code !== 'EEXIST') throw error; });
      const child = await fs.lstat(path);
      if (!child.isDirectory() || child.isSymbolicLink()) fail('INVALID_STATE', 'Relay subdirectories must be real directories.');
    }
  }

  async acquireLock() {
    const path = resolve(this.config.directory, 'worker.lock');
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
      try {
        const handle = await fs.open(path, 'wx', 0o600);
        await handle.writeFile(asJSON({ pid: process.pid }));
        await handle.sync();
        await handle.close();
        return async () => { await fs.unlink(path); };
      } catch (error) {
        if (error.code !== 'EEXIST') throw error;
        const lock = await readJSON(path);
        if (!isObject(lock) || !Number.isSafeInteger(lock.pid) || lock.pid < 1) {
          fail('LOCKED', 'An invalid worker lock requires local operator review.');
        }
        try { process.kill(lock.pid, 0); }
        catch (probe) {
          if (probe.code === 'ESRCH') { await fs.unlink(path); continue; }
        }
        fail('LOCKED', 'A relay worker already owns this state directory.');
      }
    }
    fail('LOCKED', 'The relay state directory could not be locked.');
  }

  async request(path, { method = 'GET', body, signal } = {}) {
    const url = new URL(path, this.config.origin);
    if (url.origin !== this.config.origin) fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Cross-origin relay requests are forbidden.');
    const timeout = AbortSignal.timeout(this.config.timeoutMs);
    let response;
    try {
      response = await this.fetch(url, {
        method,
        redirect: 'error',
        signal: signal ? AbortSignal.any([signal, timeout]) : timeout,
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${this.config.token}`,
          Accept: 'application/json',
          ...(body === undefined ? {} : { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }),
        },
        ...(body === undefined ? {} : { body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
      });
    } catch {
      fail(timeout.aborted ? 'REQUEST_TIMEOUT' : 'NETWORK_ERROR', 'The outbound forum request did not complete.');
    }
    if (response.url && new URL(response.url).origin !== this.config.origin) {
      await response.body?.cancel().catch(() => {});
      fail('INVALID_RESPONSE', 'A cross-origin forum response was refused.');
    }
    if (!response.ok) {
      const retry = response.headers.get('retry-after');
      const seconds = retry && /^\d+$/.test(retry) ? Number(retry) : 0;
      await response.body?.cancel().catch(() => {});
      throw new RelayError('HTTP_ERROR', 'The forum rejected the request.', {
        status: response.status,
        retryAfterMs: Number.isFinite(seconds) ? Math.min(seconds * 1000, MAX_BACKOFF_MS) : 0,
      });
    }
    try { return await readResponse(response); }
    catch (error) {
      if (timeout.aborted) fail('REQUEST_TIMEOUT', 'The outbound forum response timed out.');
      if (error instanceof RelayError) throw error;
      fail('NETWORK_ERROR', 'The outbound forum response did not complete.');
    }
  }

  async bindIdentity(signal) {
    const identity = await this.request('/api/forum/v1/identity', { signal });
    if (!isObject(identity) || !isObject(identity.participant) || !validId(identity.participant.id)) {
      fail('INVALID_RESPONSE', 'The forum returned an invalid participant identity.');
    }
    if (this.config.expectedParticipantId && identity.participant.id !== this.config.expectedParticipantId) {
      fail('IDENTITY_CHANGED', 'The forum identity does not match the participant credential file.');
    }
    const binding = { version: 1, origin: this.config.origin, participantId: identity.participant.id };
    const path = resolve(this.config.directory, 'binding.json');
    const previous = await readJSON(path, { optional: true });
    if (previous !== undefined && (!isObject(previous) || previous.version !== binding.version || previous.origin !== binding.origin || previous.participantId !== binding.participantId)) {
      fail('IDENTITY_CHANGED', 'Use a separate relay directory for each forum origin and participant.');
    }
    if (previous === undefined) await atomicJSON(path, binding);
    return binding.participantId;
  }

  async receive(signal) {
    const cursorFile = resolve(this.config.directory, 'cursor.json');
    const saved = await readJSON(cursorFile, { optional: true });
    if (saved !== undefined && (!isObject(saved) || saved.version !== 1 || !Number.isSafeInteger(saved.after) || saved.after < 0)) {
      fail('INVALID_STATE', 'The persisted inbox cursor is invalid; it was not reset.');
    }
    let after = saved?.after ?? 0;
    let received = 0;
    let hasMore = false;
    for (let page = 0; page < MAX_PAGES; page++) {
      const result = await this.request(`/api/forum/v1/messages?after=${after}&limit=100`, { signal });
      if (!isObject(result) || !Array.isArray(result.messages) || result.messages.length > 100 ||
          typeof result.hasMore !== 'boolean' || !Number.isSafeInteger(result.nextCursor)) {
        fail('INVALID_RESPONSE', 'The forum returned an invalid inbox page.');
      }
      const messages = result.messages.map(validateMessage);
      let expectedCursor = after;
      for (const message of messages) {
        if (message.sequence <= expectedCursor) fail('INVALID_RESPONSE', 'Forum messages must be in strictly increasing cursor order.');
        expectedCursor = message.sequence;
      }
      if (result.nextCursor !== expectedCursor || (result.hasMore && expectedCursor === after)) {
        fail('INVALID_RESPONSE', 'The forum returned a cursor that could skip or repeat messages.');
      }
      for (const message of messages) {
        const path = resolve(this.config.directory, 'inbox', `${keyFor(message.id)}.json`);
        const previous = await readJSON(path, { optional: true });
        if (previous !== undefined) {
          if (JSON.stringify(validateMessage(previous)) !== JSON.stringify(message)) {
            fail('MESSAGE_CONFLICT', 'A previously stored message ID changed contents.');
          }
        } else {
          // fsync file + containing directory before any cursor advancement.
          await atomicJSON(path, message);
          received++;
        }
      }
      after = result.nextCursor;
      await atomicJSON(cursorFile, { version: 1, after });
      hasMore = result.hasMore;
      if (!hasMore) break;
    }
    return { received, cursor: after, hasMore };
  }

  async send(participantId, signal) {
    const outbox = resolve(this.config.directory, 'outbox');
    const names = (await fs.readdir(outbox)).filter(name => name.endsWith('.json')).sort().slice(0, 100);
    let sent = 0;
    let rejected = 0;
    for (const name of names) {
      const path = resolve(outbox, name);
      let request;
      try { request = validateOutgoing(await readJSON(path)); }
      catch (error) {
        if (!(error instanceof RelayError) || !['INVALID_STATE', 'INVALID_OUTBOX'].includes(error.code)) throw error;
        const stat = await fs.lstat(path);
        if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink()) fail('INVALID_STATE', 'Outbox symbolic links and special files are refused.');
        await fs.rename(path, resolve(this.config.directory, 'rejected', `${randomUUID()}.json`));
        await syncDirectory(outbox);
        await syncDirectory(resolve(this.config.directory, 'rejected'));
        rejected++;
        continue;
      }
      const archivePath = resolve(this.config.directory, 'sent', `${keyFor(request.clientMessageId)}.json`);
      const archive = await readJSON(archivePath, { optional: true });
      if (archive !== undefined) {
        if (!isObject(archive) || archive.version !== 1 || !isObject(archive.request) || !sameRequest(archive.request, request)) {
          fail('OUTBOX_CONFLICT', 'A sent clientMessageId was reused for a different request.');
        }
        const message = validateMessage(archive.message);
        if (message.topicId !== request.topicId || message.clientMessageId !== request.clientMessageId ||
            message.body !== request.body || message.participantId !== participantId) {
          fail('INVALID_STATE', 'A sent archive acknowledgement does not match its saved request.');
        }
      } else {
        const result = await this.request(`/api/forum/v1/topics/${encodeURIComponent(request.topicId)}/messages`, {
          method: 'POST', body: { body: request.body, clientMessageId: request.clientMessageId }, signal,
        });
        const message = validateMessage(result?.message);
        if (message.topicId !== request.topicId || message.clientMessageId !== request.clientMessageId ||
            message.body !== request.body || message.participantId !== participantId) {
          fail('INVALID_RESPONSE', 'The forum acknowledgement did not match the outgoing message.');
        }
        // Retain the stable request ID before removing the producer's outbox item.
        await atomicJSON(archivePath, { version: 1, request, message });
        sent++;
      }
      const current = validateOutgoing(await readJSON(path));
      if (!sameRequest(current, request)) fail('OUTBOX_CHANGED', 'An outbox item changed during delivery and was retained for review.');
      await fs.unlink(path);
      await syncDirectory(outbox);
    }
    return { sent, rejected };
  }

  async runOnce({ signal } = {}) {
    await this.initialize();
    const unlock = await this.acquireLock();
    try {
      const participantId = await this.bindIdentity(signal);
      const inbox = await this.receive(signal);
      const outbox = await this.send(participantId, signal);
      const result = { ...inbox, ...outbox };
      this.log({ event: 'relay_cycle', ...result });
      return result;
    } finally { await unlock(); }
  }

  async run({ signal } = {}) {
    let failures = 0;
    while (!signal?.aborted) {
      let wait = this.config.intervalMs;
      try { await this.runOnce({ signal }); failures = 0; }
      catch (error) {
        if (signal?.aborted) return;
        failures = Math.min(failures + 1, 10);
        wait = Math.min(MAX_BACKOFF_MS, Math.max(this.config.intervalMs * 2 ** (failures - 1), error.retryAfterMs || 0));
        this.log({ event: 'relay_retry', ...safeError(error), retryInMs: wait });
      }
      try { await delay(wait, undefined, { signal }); }
      catch (error) { if (signal?.aborted) return; throw error; }
    }
  }
}

export async function main(argv = process.argv.slice(2), env = process.env) {
  if (argv.length === 1 && argv[0] === '--help') {
    console.log('Usage: node --use-env-proxy relay/worker.mjs [--once]\nConfigure OMI_RELAY_URL, OMI_RELAY_DIR, and either OMI_RELAY_TOKEN_FILE or OMI_RELAY_TOKEN securely. See relay/README.md.');
    return 0;
  }
  try {
    if (argv.some(arg => arg !== '--once') || argv.length > 1) fail('INVALID_CONFIG', 'Only --once or --help is supported.');
    const config = loadConfig(env);
    process.umask(0o077);
    const worker = new RelayWorker(config);
    if (argv.includes('--once')) await worker.runOnce();
    else {
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const stop = () => controller.abort();
      process.once('SIGINT', stop);
      process.once('SIGTERM', stop);
      try { await worker.run({ signal: controller.signal }); }
      finally { process.removeListener('SIGINT', stop); process.removeListener('SIGTERM', stop); }
    }
    return 0;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(JSON.stringify({ event: 'relay_stopped', ...safeError(error) }));
    return 1;
  }
}

if (process.argv[1] && resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  process.exitCode = await main();
}

===== BEGIN FILE relay/worker.test.mjs | SHA256 ec18e5f386644bb9bfbeae2204b75386c4d40f55af7da9b185d4bcb6b8601f8f | BYTES 17555 =====
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import * as fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import { createServer } from 'node:http';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import test from 'node:test';
import { loadConfig, RelayWorker } from './worker.mjs';

const TOKEN = 'local-test-participant-token';
const hash = value => createHash('sha256').update(value).digest('hex');
const message = (sequence, extra = {}) => ({
  id: `message-${sequence}`, sequence, topicId: 'topic-one', participantId: 'seat-one',
  body: `Message ${sequence}: untrusted text, never executed.`,
  createdAt: '2026-11-02T12:00:00.000Z', clientMessageId: `client-${sequence}`, ...extra,
});

async function fixture(t, { incoming = [], override } = {}) {
  const directory = await fs.mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'omi-relay-test-'));
  const state = { incoming, posts: [], stored: new Map(), getCount: 0, identity: 'seat-one', override };
  const send = (response, status, value, headers = {}) => {
    response.writeHead(status, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...headers });
    response.end(JSON.stringify(value));
  };
  const server = createServer(async (request, response) => {
    try {
      if (request.headers.authorization !== `Bearer ${TOKEN}`) return send(response, 401, { error: 'unauthorized' });
      const url = new URL(request.url, 'http://localhost');
      if (state.override && await state.override({ request, response, url, send, state })) return;
      if (url.pathname === '/api/forum/v1/identity') {
        return send(response, 200, { participant: { id: state.identity, name: 'Test seat', role: 'agent' } });
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && url.pathname === '/api/forum/v1/messages') {
        state.getCount++;
        const after = Number(url.searchParams.get('after'));
        const remaining = state.incoming.filter(item => item.sequence > after);
        const messages = remaining.slice(0, 100);
        return send(response, 200, {
          messages, nextCursor: messages.at(-1)?.sequence ?? after, hasMore: remaining.length > messages.length,
        });
      }
      const match = /^\/api\/forum\/v1\/topics\/([^/]+)\/messages$/.exec(url.pathname);
      if (request.method === 'POST' && match) {
        let raw = '';
        for await (const chunk of request) raw += chunk;
        const body = JSON.parse(raw);
        state.posts.push(body);
        let saved = state.stored.get(body.clientMessageId);
        if (!saved) {
          saved = message(1000 + state.stored.size, {
            topicId: decodeURIComponent(match[1]), body: body.body, clientMessageId: body.clientMessageId,
          });
          state.stored.set(body.clientMessageId, saved);
        } else if (saved.body !== body.body || saved.topicId !== decodeURIComponent(match[1])) {
          return send(response, 409, { error: 'conflict' });
        }
        return send(response, 200, { message: saved });
      }
      send(response, 404, { error: 'not found' });
    } catch {
      if (!response.headersSent) send(response, 500, { error: 'mock error' });
      else response.end();
    }
  });
  await new Promise(resolveListen => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolveListen));
  const config = loadConfig({
    OMI_RELAY_URL: `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`,
    OMI_RELAY_ALLOW_HTTP_LOCALHOST: 'true', OMI_RELAY_TOKEN: TOKEN, OMI_RELAY_DIR: directory,
    OMI_RELAY_TIMEOUT_MS: '100', OMI_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS: '500',
  });
  const events = [];
  const worker = new RelayWorker(config, { log: event => events.push(event) });
  t.after(async () => {
    server.closeAllConnections();
    await new Promise(resolveClose => server.close(resolveClose));
    await fs.rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });
  return { directory, config, events, state, worker, server };
}

test('configuration requires HTTPS or explicit loopback HTTP permission and private external state', () => {
  const base = { OMI_RELAY_URL: 'https://forum.example', OMI_RELAY_TOKEN: TOKEN, OMI_RELAY_DIR: '/tmp/private-relay' };
  assert.equal(loadConfig(base).origin, 'https://forum.example');
  for (const url of ['http://forum.example', 'http://127.0.0.1', 'https://user:password@forum.example', 'https://forum.example/path', 'https://forum.example/?token=x']) {
    assert.throws(() => loadConfig({ ...base, OMI_RELAY_URL: url }), { code: 'INVALID_CONFIG' });
  }
  assert.throws(() => loadConfig({ ...base, OMI_RELAY_URL: 'http://forum.example', OMI_RELAY_ALLOW_HTTP_LOCALHOST: 'true' }), { code: 'INVALID_CONFIG' });
  assert.throws(() => loadConfig({ ...base, OMI_RELAY_TOKEN: 'bad\r\ntoken' }), { code: 'INVALID_CONFIG' });
  assert.throws(() => loadConfig({ ...base, OMI_RELAY_DIR: resolve('relay/state') }), { code: 'INVALID_CONFIG' });
  assert.throws(() => loadConfig({ ...base, OMI_RELAY_DIR: 'relative/path' }), { code: 'INVALID_CONFIG' });
  for (const interval of ['0', '-1', '499', '300001', 'NaN', '1.5']) {
    assert.throws(() => loadConfig({ ...base, OMI_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS: interval }), { code: 'INVALID_CONFIG' });
  }
});

test('private participant credential files are supported without logging or requiring an environment token', async t => {
  const { config, directory, state } = await fixture(t);
  const credentialPath = join(directory, 'participant-credential.json');

  const env = {
    OMI_RELAY_URL: config.origin, OMI_RELAY_ALLOW_HTTP_LOCALHOST: 'true',

  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(events).includes(TOKEN));
  assert.throws(() => loadConfig({ ...env, OMI_RELAY_TOKEN: 'other-token' }), { code: 'INVALID_CONFIG' });
  state.identity = 'seat-two';
  await assert.rejects(new RelayWorker(loaded, { log: () => {} }).runOnce(), { code: 'IDENTITY_CHANGED' });
  await fs.chmod(credentialPath, 0o644);
  assert.throws(() => loadConfig(env), { code: 'INVALID_CONFIG' });
  await fs.chmod(credentialPath, 0o600);
  const link = join(directory, 'credential-link.json');
  await fs.symlink(credentialPath, link);
  assert.throws(() => loadConfig({ ...env, OMI_RELAY_TOKEN_FILE: link }), { code: 'INVALID_CONFIG' });
});

test('multiple polls persist a durable cursor and one file per message without duplicate files', async t => {
  const { worker, directory, state, events } = await fixture(t, { incoming: [message(1), message(2)] });
  assert.deepEqual(await worker.runOnce(), { received: 2, cursor: 2, hasMore: false, sent: 0, rejected: 0 });
  assert.deepEqual(await worker.runOnce(), { received: 0, cursor: 2, hasMore: false, sent: 0, rejected: 0 });
  state.incoming.push(message(3));
  assert.equal((await worker.runOnce()).received, 1);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(join(directory, 'inbox'))).length, 3);
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(join(directory, 'cursor.json'), 'utf8')), { version: 1, after: 3 });
  const raw = await fs.readFile(join(directory, 'inbox', `${hash('message-1')}.json`), 'utf8');
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(raw), message(1));
  assert.equal((await fs.stat(directory)).mode & 0o077, 0);
  assert.equal((await fs.stat(join(directory, 'cursor.json'))).mode & 0o077, 0);
  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(events).includes(TOKEN));
  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(events).includes('untrusted text'));
});

test('outbox acknowledgement is archived and a duplicate stable request never posts twice', async t => {
  const { worker, directory, state } = await fixture(t);
  await worker.initialize();
  const request = { topicId: 'topic-one', body: 'A local model reply, not an execution command.', clientMessageId: 'stable-id-one' };

  assert.equal((await worker.runOnce()).sent, 1);
  assert.equal(state.posts.length, 1);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(join(directory, 'outbox'))).length, 0);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(join(directory, 'sent'))).length, 1);
  await fs.writeFile(join(directory, 'outbox', 'duplicate.json'), JSON.stringify(request));
  assert.equal((await worker.runOnce()).sent, 0);
  assert.equal(state.posts.length, 1);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(join(directory, 'outbox'))).length, 0);
});

test('retry after lost archive uses the same clientMessageId and server deduplication', async t => {
  const { worker, config, directory, state } = await fixture(t);
  await worker.initialize();
  const request = { topicId: 'topic-one', body: 'Durable retry', clientMessageId: 'stable-retry' };
  await fs.writeFile(join(directory, 'outbox', 'reply.json'), JSON.stringify(request));
  await worker.runOnce();
  // Simulate acknowledgement receipt followed by a crash before its local archive became durable.
  await fs.unlink(join(directory, 'sent', `${hash(request.clientMessageId)}.json`));
  await fs.writeFile(join(directory, 'outbox', 'reply.json'), JSON.stringify(request));
  await new RelayWorker(config, { log: () => {} }).runOnce();
  assert.equal(state.posts.length, 2);
  assert.equal(state.stored.size, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(state.posts[0], state.posts[1]);
});

test('a failed POST retains its exact outbox request for a later successful retry', async t => {
  let failPost = true;
  const { worker, directory, state } = await fixture(t, {
    override: ({ request, response, send }) => {
      if (request.method === 'POST' && failPost) { send(response, 503, { error: TOKEN }); return true; }
      return false;
    },
  });
  await worker.initialize();
  const request = { topicId: 'topic-one', body: 'Retry exactly', clientMessageId: 'stable-unavailable' };
  await fs.writeFile(join(directory, 'outbox', 'reply.json'), JSON.stringify(request));
  await assert.rejects(worker.runOnce(), { code: 'HTTP_ERROR', status: 503 });
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(join(directory, 'outbox', 'reply.json'), 'utf8')), request);
  failPost = false;
  await worker.runOnce();
  assert.equal(state.posts.length, 1);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(join(directory, 'sent'))).length, 1);
});

test('outgoing validation matches forum IDs/content limits and whitespace normalization', async t => {
  const { worker, directory, state } = await fixture(t);
  await worker.initialize();
  for (const [index, request] of [
    { topicId: '../escape', body: 'Valid', clientMessageId: 'invalid-topic' },
    { topicId: 'topic-one', body: 'x'.repeat(12_001), clientMessageId: 'oversized-body' },
    { topicId: 'topic-one', body: '   ', clientMessageId: 'empty-body' },
    { topicId: 'topic-one', body: 'Bad\u0000text', clientMessageId: 'control-body' },
    { topicId: 'topic-one', body: 'Valid', clientMessageId: 'spaces are invalid' },
  ].entries()) {
    await fs.writeFile(join(directory, 'outbox', `bad-${index}.json`), JSON.stringify(request));
  }
  await fs.writeFile(join(directory, 'outbox', 'valid.json'), JSON.stringify({
    topicId: 'topic-one', body: '  Reply from a local seat.\n', clientMessageId: 'normalized-reply',
  }));
  const result = await worker.runOnce();
  assert.equal(result.rejected, 5);
  assert.equal(result.sent, 1);
  assert.equal(state.posts[0].body, 'Reply from a local seat.');
});

test('sent IDs cannot be reused for changed content', async t => {

  await worker.initialize();
  const request = { topicId: 'topic-one', body: 'First', clientMessageId: 'stable-conflict' };
  await fs.writeFile(join(directory, 'outbox', 'reply.json'), JSON.stringify(request));
  await worker.runOnce();

  await assert.rejects(worker.runOnce(), { code: 'OUTBOX_CONFLICT' });
  assert.equal(state.posts.length, 1);
  assert.equal((await fs.readdir(join(directory, 'outbox'))).length, 1);
});

test('a live worker lock prevents concurrent cursor and outbox mutations', async t => {
  const { worker, directory } = await fixture(t);
  await worker.initialize();

});

test('a lock left by a terminated process is reclaimed on restart', async t => {
  const { worker, directory } = await fixture(t, { incoming: [message(1)] });
  await worker.initialize();
  await fs.writeFile(join(directory, 'worker.lock'), JSON.stringify({ pid: 2_147_483_647 }));
  assert.equal((await worker.runOnce()).received, 1);
  await assert.rejects(fs.stat(join(directory, 'worker.lock')), { code: 'ENOENT' });
});

test('rate limits enter bounded exponential backoff and logs contain no server body or token', async t => {
  const { worker, events } = await fixture(t, {
    override: ({ response, send }) => { send(response, 429, { secret: TOKEN }, { 'Retry-After': '999999' }); return true; },
  });
  const stop = new AbortController();
  worker.log = event => { events.push(event); if (event.event === 'relay_retry') stop.abort(); };
  await worker.run({ signal: stop.signal });
  assert.equal(events[0].event, 'relay_retry');
  assert.equal(events[0].retryInMs, 300_000);
  assert.equal(events[0].httpStatus, 429);
  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(events).includes(TOKEN));
});

===== BEGIN FILE scripts/virtual-team-sim.mjs | SHA256 533e69563b6c2cfea77e7c4306c4ca57bee87dfa9f5a9429638a761cd3dc7996 | BYTES 25610 =====
#!/usr/bin/env node
/** Real isolated mail transport; deterministic HTTP fixture, never a live model. */
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { execFile } from 'node:child_process';
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import * as fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import { createServer } from 'node:http';
import { dirname, join, relative, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { setTimeout as delay } from 'node:timers/promises';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { enqueueAdvisory } from '../relay/advisory.mjs';
import { GitRelayWorker, validateGitMessage } from '../relay/git-worker.mjs';
import { sortAgentMail } from '../relay/mailroom.mjs';
import { ModelAdapter, validateAdapterConfig } from '../relay/model-adapter.mjs';
import { StationSupervisor } from '../relay/station.mjs';

const CHECKOUT = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const OUTPUT = join(CHECKOUT, 'test-results');
const SEATS = ['dot', 'deepseek', 'claude', 'gemini', 'muse'];
const MEMBERS = SEATS.slice(1);
const sha256 = value => createHash('sha256').update(value).digest('hex');
const json = value => JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) + '\n';

function git(args, { input = '', env = {}, cwd = CHECKOUT } = {}) {
  return new Promise((accept, reject) => {
    const child = execFile('git', args, {
      cwd, env: { ...process.env, GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT: '0', ...env },
      encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 4 * 1024 * 1024, timeout: 20_000,
    }, (error, stdout) => error ? reject(error) : accept(stdout.trim()));
    child.stdin.on('error', () => {});
    child.stdin.end(input);
  });
}

/** This exact public-source fingerprint is also checked by the bundle builder. */
async function sourceSnapshot() {
  const paths = [];
  async function walk(directory) {
    const entries = await fs.readdir(join(CHECKOUT, directory), { withFileTypes: true });
    for (const entry of entries) {
      const path = `${directory}/${entry.name}`;
      if (entry.isDirectory()) await walk(path);
      else if (entry.isFile() && /\.(?:mjs|sh)$/.test(entry.name)) paths.push(path);
    }
  }
  await walk('relay');
  await walk('node');
  paths.push('scripts/virtual-team-sim.mjs');
  paths.sort();
  const files = [];
  for (const path of paths) {
    const bytes = await fs.readFile(join(CHECKOUT, path));
    files.push({ path, sha256: sha256(bytes), bytes: bytes.length });
  }
  return {
    commit: await git(['rev-parse', 'HEAD']),
    dirty: Boolean(await git(['status', '--porcelain', '--untracked-files=normal'])),
    fingerprint: sha256(files.map(file => `${file.path}\0${file.sha256}\0${file.bytes}\n`).join('')),
    files,
  };
}

async function jsonFiles(directory) {
  const entries = (await fs.readdir(directory)).filter(name => name.endsWith('.json')).sort();
  return Promise.all(entries.map(async name => ({ name, value: JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(join(directory, name), 'utf8')) })));
}

async function seedRemote(root, remote) {
  await git(['init', '--bare', '--template=', remote]);
  const index = join(root, `seed-index-${randomUUID()}`);
  const env = {
    GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: 'OMI synthetic simulation', GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: 'simulation@example.invalid',
    GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: 'OMI synthetic simulation', GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: 'simulation@example.invalid',
    GIT_INDEX_FILE: index,
  };
  await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'read-tree', '--empty'], { env });
  const blob = await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'hash-object', '-w', '--stdin'], {
    input: 'Synthetic OMI mailbox fixture. No production agents, credentials, or messages.\n', env,
  });
  await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'update-index', '--add', '--cacheinfo', '100644', blob, 'README.md'], { env });
  const tree = await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'write-tree'], { env });
  const commit = await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, '-c', 'commit.gpgSign=false', 'commit-tree', tree], {
    input: 'Seed synthetic OMI mailbox\n', env,
  });
  await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'update-ref', 'refs/heads/omi-relay', commit]);
  await fs.unlink(index);
}

function advisory(id, to, kind, extra = {}) {
  return {
    protocol: 'omi-advisory/1', id, kind, from: 'dot', to,
    summary: `Synthetic simulation ${kind}: ${id}`,
    details: { scope: 'Inspect the selected public source and return a short draft. Execute no tools.' },
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), requiresAck: false, ...extra,
  };
}

async function providerFixture(trace) {
  const requests = [];
  const timers = new Set();
  const failures = [];
  const server = createServer(async (request, response) => {
    try {
      assert.equal(request.method, 'POST');
      assert.ok(['/api/chat', '/v1/chat/completions'].includes(request.url));
      const chunks = [];
      for await (const chunk of request) chunks.push(chunk);
      const body = JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8'));
      assert.equal(body.stream, false);
      const seat = body.model.replace(/^simulation-/, '');
      assert.ok(MEMBERS.includes(seat));
      const payload = JSON.parse(body.messages.find(message => message.role === 'user').content);
      assert.equal(payload.assignment.taskId, `SIM-${seat.toUpperCase()}-001`);
      assert.equal(payload.sourceFiles.length, 1);
      assert.equal(payload.sourceFiles[0].path, 'relay/advisory.mjs');
      assert.ok(payload.sourceFiles[0].text.includes('validateAdvisory'));
      assert.ok(request.headers['idempotency-key']);
      const count = requests.filter(item => item.participantId === seat).length + 1;
      requests.push({ participantId: seat, attempt: count, taskId: payload.assignment.taskId, providerKind: request.url === '/api/chat' ? 'ollama' : 'openai-compatible' });
      trace('fixture_provider_request', { participantId: seat, attempt: count });
      // An actual aborted HTTP request exercises the adapter timeout and retry.
      const reply = () => {
        if (response.destroyed) return;
        const content = `SIMULATED ${seat} draft: source received. This deterministic fixture is not a live AI model and executed no tests.`;
        response.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' });
        response.end(JSON.stringify(request.url === '/api/chat' ? { message: { content } } : { choices: [{ message: { content } }] }));
      };
      if (seat === 'gemini' && count === 1) {
        const timer = setTimeout(() => { timers.delete(timer); reply(); }, 500);
        timers.add(timer);
      } else reply();
    } catch (error) {
      failures.push(error.message);
      if (!response.destroyed) { response.writeHead(500, { 'content-type': 'application/json' }); response.end('{"error":"invalid simulation request"}'); }
    }
  });
  await new Promise((accept, reject) => { server.once('error', reject); server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', accept); });
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  return {
    requests, failures, base,
    async close() {
      for (const timer of timers) clearTimeout(timer);
      server.closeAllConnections();
      await new Promise((accept, reject) => server.close(error => error ? reject(error) : accept()));
    },
  };
}

async function main() {
  if (process.argv.length !== 2) { console.error('Usage: node scripts/virtual-team-sim.mjs'); return 1; }
  const report = {
    protocol: 'omi-virtual-team-sim/1', status: 'failed', startedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    simulatedProvider: true, liveModelsTested: false, productionMailboxUsed: false,
    scope: 'Five synthetic seats using production Git transport, station, advisory, mailroom, and model-adapter code; local bare Git and a deterministic loopback HTTP provider.',
    limitations: ['No live Claude, DeepSeek, Gemini, Muse, or Codex inference was tested.', 'No remote Station Node or Route66 installation was verified.', 'Per-seat sorting is routing for cooperating agents, not secrecy from shared repository readers.', 'Crash injection uses the adapter durable-result fault hook; no operating-system process was killed.'],
    checks: [], timeline: [], seats: [],
  };
  const trace = (event, fields = {}) => report.timeline.push({ sequence: report.timeline.length + 1, event, ...fields });
  let stage = 'initialization';
  async function check(name, verify) {
    stage = name;
    try { const details = await verify(); report.checks.push({ name, status: 'passed', ...(details ? { details } : {}) }); }
    catch (error) { report.checks.push({ name, status: 'failed', error: error.message }); throw error; }
  }
  let root;
  let fixture;
  let initialSource;
  try {
    initialSource = await sourceSnapshot();
    // State lives outside the checkout, is owner-private, and is deleted below.
    root = await fs.mkdtemp('/workspace/omi-virtual-sim-');
    await fs.chmod(root, 0o700);
    const remote = join(root, 'synthetic.git');
    await seedRemote(root, remote);
    const stationDirectory = join(root, 'station');
    const seatDirectory = id => join(stationDirectory, id);
    const alias = id => id === 'muse' ? ['qa-tracker'] : [];
    const makeStation = (directory = stationDirectory, unavailableSeat = null) => new StationSupervisor({
      // Station validates production HTTPS syntax; the test factory replaces it
      // with a local bare repo before any Git request is made.
      remote: 'https://simulation.invalid/omi/fixture.git', branch: 'omi-relay', directory,
      participants: SEATS.map(id => ({ id, enabled: true, addresses: alias(id) })),
      intervalMs: 1000, concurrency: 2, timeoutMs: 10_000,
    }, {
      workerFactory: (config, options) => new GitRelayWorker({ ...config, remote: config.participantId === unavailableSeat ? join(root, 'unavailable.git') : remote }, { ...options, allowLocalRemoteForTests: true }),
      mailSorter: sortAgentMail,
      // A deterministic scheduler clock makes the explicit backoff assertion
      // independent of host disk speed. Adapter timeouts still use real time.
      now: () => 1_700_000_000_000,
      log: event => trace(event.event, Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(event).filter(([key]) => key !== 'event'))),
    });
    const station = makeStation();
    const cycle = async () => {
      const result = await station.runOnce();
      assert.equal(result.failed, 0, `Station failure: ${JSON.stringify(result.participants.filter(seat => seat.status !== 'complete'))}`);
      assert.equal(result.completed, 5);
      return result;
    };
    await check('five independent private seats initialize over real local Git', async () => {
      await cycle();
      for (const id of SEATS) assert.equal((await fs.stat(seatDirectory(id))).mode & 0o077, 0);
    });
    const originalDotCursor = await fs.readFile(join(seatDirectory('dot'), 'git-cursor.json'));
    await check('Dot queues four addressed assignments and one explicit broadcast', async () => {
      for (const id of MEMBERS) await enqueueAdvisory(join(seatDirectory('dot'), 'outbox'), advisory(`assignment-${id}`, [id === 'muse' ? 'qa-tracker' : id], 'assignment', { taskId: `SIM-${id.toUpperCase()}-001` }));
      await enqueueAdvisory(join(seatDirectory('dot'), 'outbox'), advisory('simulation-broadcast', ['all'], 'progress'));
      await enqueueAdvisory(join(seatDirectory('dot'), 'outbox'), advisory('dot-self-assignment', ['dot'], 'assignment', { taskId: 'SIM-DOT-SELF' }));
      for (const [name, body] of [
        ['legacy', 'Synthetic operator note without a routing protocol.'],
        ['malformed', JSON.stringify({ protocol: 'omi-advisory/1', kind: 'assignment', to: ['gemini'] })],
      ]) await fs.writeFile(join(seatDirectory('dot'), 'outbox', `${name}.json`), json({ topicId: 'simulation-fixture', clientMessageId: `simulation-${name}`, body }), { mode: 0o600 });
      await cycle(); await cycle();
    });
    const assignmentInputs = new Map();
    await check('mailrooms isolate directed work, deliver broadcast once, and quarantine malformed advisory', async () => {
      for (const id of SEATS) {
        const raw = await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory(id), 'inbox'));
        assert.equal(raw.length, 8);
        for (const { value } of raw) validateGitMessage(value);
        const inbox = await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory(id), 'mail/inbox'));
        assert.equal(inbox.length, 1);
        const input = inbox[0].value;
        const task = JSON.parse(input.body);
        assert.equal(task.id, id === 'dot' ? 'dot-self-assignment' : `assignment-${id}`);
        assignmentInputs.set(id, input);
        assert.equal((await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory(id), 'mail/broadcast'))).length, 1);
        assert.equal((await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory(id), 'mail/unaddressed'))).length, 1);
        assert.equal((await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory(id), 'mail/quarantine'))).length, 1);
        const receipts = await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory(id), 'mail/receipts'));
        assert.equal(receipts.filter(item => item.value.classification === 'not-for-me').length, 4);
      }
      return { sharedRawTransportCopiesPerSeat: 8, directedCopiesPerSeat: 1, routingIsNotAnAccessControlBoundary: true };
    });
    fixture = await providerFixture(trace);
    const makeAdapter = (id, hooks = {}) => new ModelAdapter(validateAdapterConfig({
      participantId: id, seatDirectory: seatDirectory(id), addresses: alias(id),
      provider: { kind: id === 'muse' ? 'openai-compatible' : 'ollama', url: fixture.base + (id === 'muse' ? '/v1/chat/completions' : '/api/chat'), model: `simulation-${id}` },
      sourceRoot: CHECKOUT, sourceFiles: ['relay/advisory.mjs'], timeoutMs: 150,
      maxResponseBytes: 1024 * 1024, maxAttempts: 3, intervalMs: 500,
    }), { ...hooks, log: event => trace(event.event, Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(event).filter(([key]) => key !== 'event'))) });
    const initialAdapterResults = new Map();
    await check('actual HTTP provider calls carry only each assigned task and selected source', async () => {
      const runs = await Promise.all(MEMBERS.map(async id => {
        const hooks = id === 'claude' ? { afterPendingResult: () => { throw new Error('Synthetic crash after durable result, before outbox enqueue.'); } } : {};
        return [id, await makeAdapter(id, hooks).runOnce()];
      }));
      for (const [id, result] of runs) initialAdapterResults.set(id, result);
      assert.equal(fixture.requests.length, 4);
      assert.deepEqual(fixture.failures, []);
      assert.equal(initialAdapterResults.get('deepseek').queued, 1);
      assert.equal(initialAdapterResults.get('muse').queued, 1);
      assert.equal(initialAdapterResults.get('gemini').failed, 1);
      assert.equal(initialAdapterResults.get('gemini').queued, 0);
      assert.equal(initialAdapterResults.get('claude').failed, 1);
      assert.equal(initialAdapterResults.get('claude').queued, 0);
      assert.equal((await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory('gemini'), 'outbox'))).length, 0);
    });
    await check('restart recovers durable Claude result without repeating provider inference', async () => {
      const requestsBefore = fixture.requests.length;
      const result = await makeAdapter('claude').runOnce();
      assert.equal(result.providerCalls, 0);
      assert.equal(result.queued, 1);
      assert.equal(fixture.requests.length, requestsBefore);
      assert.equal((await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory('claude'), 'outbox'))).length, 1);
    });
    await check('lost adapter receipt reuses the stable pending result without duplicate outbox work', async () => {
      const requestsBefore = fixture.requests.length;
      await fs.unlink(join(seatDirectory('claude'), 'adapter/receipts', `${assignmentInputs.get('claude').id}.json`));
      const result = await makeAdapter('claude').runOnce();
      assert.equal(result.providerCalls, 0);
      assert.equal(result.queued, 1);
      assert.equal((await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory('claude'), 'outbox'))).length, 1);
      assert.equal(fixture.requests.length, requestsBefore);
    });
    await check('concurrent Git writers preserve three successful replies while Gemini remains pending', async () => {
      const rounds = [await cycle(), await cycle()];
      assert.equal(rounds.reduce((sum, round) => sum + round.participants.reduce((n, seat) => n + seat.sent, 0), 0), 3);
      const replies = (await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory('dot'), 'mail/inbox'))).filter(item => JSON.parse(item.value.body).kind === 'result');
      assert.equal(replies.length, 3);
      assert.deepEqual(replies.map(item => item.value.participantId).sort(), ['claude', 'deepseek', 'muse']);
      assert.equal((await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory('gemini'), 'adapter/receipts'))).length, 0);
      const attempt = (await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory('gemini'), 'adapter/attempts')))[0].value;
      assert.equal(attempt.count, 1);
      assert.equal(attempt.lastCode, 'PROVIDER_PENDING');
      return { actualConcurrentWriterLimit: 2, successfulRepliesBeforeFailedSeatRecovery: 3 };
    });
    await check('timed out provider task retries with bounded attempts and returns its fourth result', async () => {
      const adapter = makeAdapter('gemini');
      let result;
      for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 20; attempt++) {
        result = await adapter.runOnce();
        if (result.queued) break;
        assert.equal(result.blocked, 0); assert.equal(result.failed, 0);
        await delay(100);
      }
      assert.equal(result.queued, 1);
      assert.equal(fixture.requests.filter(request => request.participantId === 'gemini').length, 2);
      await cycle(); await cycle();
    });
    await check('Dot receives four correlated draft results with truthful unverified status', async () => {
      const replies = (await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory('dot'), 'mail/inbox'))).filter(item => JSON.parse(item.value.body).kind === 'result');
      assert.equal(replies.length, 4);
      for (const { value: envelope } of replies) {
        const reply = JSON.parse(envelope.body);
        assert.equal(reply.from, envelope.participantId);
        assert.deepEqual(reply.to, ['dot']);
        assert.equal(reply.taskId, `SIM-${reply.from.toUpperCase()}-001`);
        assert.equal(reply.details.inReplyTo, assignmentInputs.get(reply.from).id);
        assert.equal(reply.details.status, 'model-output-unverified');
        assert.deepEqual(reply.details.checksExecutedByAdapter, []);
        assert.equal(reply.details.sourceFiles.length, 1);
        assert.ok(reply.details.output.startsWith('SIMULATED '));
        assert.equal(reply.requiresAck, false);
      }
      assert.deepEqual(fixture.failures, []);
    });
    await check('lost transport sent receipt replays the same identity without a new Git commit', async () => {
      const directory = seatDirectory('muse');
      const [{ name, value: archive }] = await jsonFiles(join(directory, 'sent'));
      const head = await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'rev-parse', 'omi-relay']);
      await fs.unlink(join(directory, 'sent', name));
      const { topicId, body, clientMessageId } = archive.message;
      await fs.writeFile(join(directory, 'outbox', 'replayed-result.json'), json({ topicId, body, clientMessageId }), { mode: 0o600 });
      await cycle();
      assert.equal(await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'rev-parse', 'omi-relay']), head);
      assert.equal((await jsonFiles(join(directory, 'sent'))).length, 1);
    });
    await check('cursor replay and completed adapter restarts create no extra provider calls or messages', async () => {
      const requestsBefore = fixture.requests.length;
      const head = await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'rev-parse', 'omi-relay']);
      await fs.writeFile(join(seatDirectory('dot'), 'git-cursor.json'), originalDotCursor, { mode: 0o600 });
      const restartedStation = makeStation();
      const cycleResult = await restartedStation.runOnce();
      assert.equal(cycleResult.failed, 0);
      const results = await Promise.all(SEATS.map(id => makeAdapter(id).runOnce()));
      for (const result of results) { assert.equal(result.providerCalls, 0); assert.equal(result.queued, 0); assert.equal(result.failed, 0); assert.equal(result.blocked, 0); }
      assert.equal(fixture.requests.length, requestsBefore);
      assert.equal(await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'rev-parse', 'omi-relay']), head);
      for (const id of SEATS) {
        const raw = await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory(id), 'inbox'));
        assert.equal(raw.length, 12);
        const initial = raw.find(item => item.value.id === assignmentInputs.get(id).id).value;
        assert.deepEqual(initial, assignmentInputs.get(id));
        assert.equal((await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory(id), 'mail/broadcast'))).length, 1);
        const resultsForOthers = (await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory(id), 'mail/inbox'))).filter(item => JSON.parse(item.value.body).kind === 'result');
        assert.equal(resultsForOthers.length, id === 'dot' ? 4 : 0);
      }
      const files = (await git([`--git-dir=${remote}`, 'ls-tree', '-r', '--name-only', 'omi-relay'])).split('\n');
      assert.equal(files.filter(path => path.startsWith('messages/')).length, 12);
      assert.equal(new Set(files).size, files.length);
      assert.equal(fixture.requests.length, 5);
    });
    await check('one unavailable Git seat backs off while four other seats keep polling', async () => {
      const isolated = makeStation(join(root, 'failure-isolation-station'), 'gemini');
      const failedCycle = await isolated.runOnce();
      assert.equal(failedCycle.failed, 1); assert.equal(failedCycle.completed, 4);
      assert.equal(failedCycle.participants.find(seat => seat.participantId === 'gemini').status, 'failed');
      const nextCycle = await isolated.runOnce();
      assert.equal(nextCycle.backoff, 1); assert.equal(nextCycle.completed, 4);
      assert.equal(nextCycle.participants.find(seat => seat.participantId === 'gemini').status, 'backoff');
    });
    for (const id of SEATS) {
      const mail = {};
      for (const folder of ['inbox', 'broadcast', 'unaddressed', 'quarantine', 'receipts']) mail[folder] = (await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory(id), 'mail', folder))).length;
      report.seats.push({
        participantId: id, synthetic: true, addresses: alias(id),
        rawReceived: (await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory(id), 'inbox'))).length,
        sorted: mail, assignedTasks: id === 'dot' ? 0 : 1,
        providerRequests: fixture.requests.filter(request => request.participantId === id).length,
        successfulResults: id === 'dot' ? 0 : (await jsonFiles(join(seatDirectory(id), 'sent'))).length,
        receivedResults: id === 'dot' ? 4 : 0,
      });
    }
    report.provider = { type: 'deterministic-local-http-fixture', realInference: false, requests: fixture.requests, totalRequests: fixture.requests.length, successfulTaskResults: 4, injectedTimeouts: 1 };
    report.source = await sourceSnapshot();
    await check('public source fingerprint stayed unchanged throughout the simulation', async () => {
      assert.equal(report.source.fingerprint, initialSource.fingerprint, 'Public source changed while simulation ran; rerun after writers finish.');
      assert.equal(report.source.commit, initialSource.commit, 'HEAD changed while simulation ran; rerun after commit.');
    });
    report.status = 'passed';
  } catch (error) {
    if (!report.checks.some(check => check.status === 'failed')) report.checks.push({ name: stage, status: 'failed', error: error.message });
    report.failure = { stage, message: error.message, ...(error.code ? { code: error.code } : {}) };
    try { report.source = await sourceSnapshot(); } catch { /* Keep primary failure. */ }
  } finally {
    if (fixture) await fixture.close().catch(error => { report.status = 'failed'; report.checks.push({ name: 'fixture cleanup', status: 'failed', error: error.message }); });
    if (root) await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(error => { report.status = 'failed'; report.checks.push({ name: 'private state cleanup', status: 'failed', error: error.message }); });
    report.finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    report.counts = { passed: report.checks.filter(check => check.status === 'passed').length, failed: report.checks.filter(check => check.status === 'failed').length };
    await fs.mkdir(OUTPUT, { recursive: true });
    await fs.writeFile(join(OUTPUT, 'simulation-report.json'), json(report));
    const summary = [
      `OMI virtual team simulation: ${report.status.toUpperCase()}`,
      `${report.counts.passed} checks passed; ${report.counts.failed} failed.`,
      'Five synthetic seats; real local Git transport and production mail/adapter code.',
      'Deterministic local HTTP provider: no live AI model or remote node was tested.',
      ...(report.provider ? [`${report.provider.successfulTaskResults} task results returned; ${report.provider.totalRequests} provider requests including one injected timeout.`] : []),
      ...report.checks.map(check => `${check.status === 'passed' ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} ${check.name}${check.error ? `: ${check.error}` : ''}`),
      ...(report.source ? [`Source commit: ${report.source.commit}`, `Source fingerprint: ${report.source.fingerprint}`, `Source working tree dirty: ${report.source.dirty}`] : []),
      'Limits: the report certifies this synthetic relay scenario, not live model availability or the complete OMI application.',
    ].join('\n') + '\n';
    await fs.writeFile(join(OUTPUT, 'simulation-summary.txt'), summary);
    console.log(summary);
    console.log(`Report: ${relative(CHECKOUT, join(OUTPUT, 'simulation-report.json'))}`);
  }
  return report.status === 'passed' ? 0 : 1;
}

process.exitCode = await main();

===== END FILE scripts/virtual-team-sim.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE source-review/CLAUDE.md | SHA256 a996f9eeca4dc1ecd238574eb92a5597b9e36909760cf52b2d64beedd4babf2c | BYTES 2828 =====
# CLAUDE.md — KAOS LABS OMI Client Working Contract

You are working inside the KAOS LABS OMI Client seed repository.

Read these files first, in order:

1. `omi-client/manifest.json`
2. `omi-client/registry/apps.json`
3. `omi-client/capsules/schema.json`
4. `omi-client/mesh/talky-spider.json`
5. `omi-client/shell/shell.json`
6. `omi-client/cartridges/README.md`

## Architecture

- OMI Client is the universal cross-platform seat.
- Personal Capsules hold identity, privacy, permissions, preferences, and personalized services.
- Talky Spider is the shared authenticated communications/mesh fabric.
- Pandora and mini-apps remain isolated modular cartridges.
- HoneycombMesh -> CartridgeArm -> SegmentNode -> Digital Brain Cell is the distributed work structure.
- VGPU/CPU/storage resources may be pooled only from user-authorized shares.
- Load balancing may begin only after Share is explicitly enabled.

## OMI Sharing Law

- Sharing defaults OFF.
- User authorization and an active share window are required.
- OMI may consume at most 15% of CURRENTLY IDLE resources, never 15% of total resources.
- Foreground user activity always wins and OMI yields immediately when demand rises.
- Private user data is never part of the shared resource pool.
- AUTO MANAGE may manage an authorized share window.
- TURBO is the manual user-triggered share mode and remains subject to the same 15%-of-idle ceiling.

## Hard Rules

Preserve the governing rules in `omi-client/manifest.json`:

- foreground user wins
- private data is not shared
- apps remain isolated
- signed modules only

Do not merge applications into a monolith.
Do not grant apps direct write access to OMI Core.
Do not infer runtime validation from registration or manifests.
Do not silently redesign OMI terminology or architecture.
Do not weaken privacy, sharing, or cartridge boundaries.

## Current Registered App Families

The registry currently contains declarations for:

- Eagle's Nest
- CORVID
- Bio-Tank
- Digital Assembly Line
- War Window
- LISA Braid
- Pandora Sat-Track

Registration does not mean runtime-attested or mounted.

## Working Method

When asked to continue work:

1. Inspect the repository first.
2. State the exact current files/structure.
3. Check requested work against `omi-client/manifest.json`.
4. Prefer the smallest reversible change.
5. Preserve modular cartridge boundaries.
6. Validate JSON/schema references after changes.
7. Report exactly what changed and what remains unverified.

If asked to inspect only, do not write.
If asked to write, do not redesign unrelated architecture.

## Immediate Build Direction

The next intended work is to mount and smoke-test actual cartridges through the OMI Client, beginning with family/private apps such as Eagle's Nest, while keeping each app separately deployable and testable.

===== END FILE source-review/CLAUDE.md =====

  <main>
    <header>
      <div class="brand">
        <div class="brand-mark" aria-hidden="true"><svg width="25" height="25" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.35" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="m3 10 9-7 9 7M5.5 9v10h13V9M9.5 19v-6h5v6"/><path d="M8 7.5 12 10l4-2.5"/></svg></div>
        <div><p class="eyebrow">A little closer, every day</p><h1>Eagle’s Nest</h1></div>
      </div>
      <span class="private"><svg width="12" height="12" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="5" y="10" width="14" height="11" rx="3"/><path d="M8 10V7a4 4 0 0 1 8 0v3"/></svg> Private capsule</span>
    </header>
    <div class="intro"><div><h2 id="board-heading">Your family board</h2><p>A place for the little things worth remembering.</p></div><span class="count" id="count">0 items</span></div>
    <div class="layout">
      <section aria-labelledby="board-heading">
        <div class="tabs" role="group" aria-label="Filter board" aria-describedby="filter-help">
          <button class="tab" type="button" data-filter="all" aria-pressed="true" aria-controls="entries">Everything</button>
          <button class="tab" type="button" data-filter="note" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="entries">Notes</button>
          <button class="tab" type="button" data-filter="task" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="entries">To-dos</button>
        </div>
        <p id="filter-help" class="sr-only">Choose which items appear on the board. The selected filter stays pressed.</p>
        <div class="notice" id="undo-notice"><span>Item removed.</span><button type="button" id="undo">Undo</button><button type="button" id="dismiss-undo" aria-label="Dismiss undo">×</button></div>
        <p id="board-status" class="sr-only" role="status" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"></p>
        <div class="entries" id="entries"></div>
        <p class="footnote">This board lives in your local Personal Capsule. Family sync will need a connected, authenticated Talky Spider service.</p>
      </section>
      <aside class="composer" aria-labelledby="composer-title">
        <h2 id="composer-title">Leave a little something</h2>
        <p>A reminder, a plan, or a thought for later.</p>
        <div id="compose" role="form" aria-label="Add a board item">
          <fieldset><legend>Make it a</legend><div class="types"><label><input type="radio" name="kind" value="note" checked> Note</label><label><input type="radio" name="kind" value="task"> To-do</label></div></fieldset>
          <div class="field"><label class="field-label" for="title">Title</label><input id="title" type="text" maxlength="80" placeholder="What’s on your mind?" required autocomplete="off" aria-describedby="title-help validation-status" aria-invalid="false" disabled><p class="field-help" id="title-help">Required. Up to 80 characters. Press Enter to add or save the item.</p></div>
          <div class="field"><label class="field-label" for="body">Details <span class="optional">· optional</span></label><textarea id="body" maxlength="800" placeholder="A little more, if you like…" aria-describedby="body-help" disabled></textarea><p class="field-help" id="body-help">Up to 800 characters. Enter starts a new line. Control or Command + Enter saves.</p></div>
          <button class="submit" type="button" id="submit" disabled>Add to the board</button>
          <button class="cancel" type="button" id="cancel">Cancel editing</button>
          <p class="validation" id="validation-status" role="alert" aria-live="assertive" aria-atomic="true"></p>
          <p class="status" id="status" role="status" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true">Waiting for your Personal Capsule…</p>
