  <script>
    (() => {
      'use strict';
      const APP_ID = 'eagles-nest';
      const MAX_ENTRIES = 50;
      const byId = id => document.getElementById(id);
      let entries = [];
      let filter = 'all';
      let editing = null;
      let removed = null;
      let initialized = false;
      let parentOrigin = '*';
      let saveSequence = 0;
      let latestRequest = 0;
      const status = message => { byId('status').textContent = message; };
      const announce = message => { byId('board-status').textContent = message; };
      const validation = (message, invalidTitle = false) => {
        byId('validation-status').textContent = message;
        byId('title').setAttribute('aria-invalid', String(invalidTitle));
      };
      function focusEntry(entryId, action = 'edit') {
        const card = [...document.querySelectorAll('.entry')].find(item => item.dataset.entryId === entryId);
        const target = card && card.querySelector(`.${action}`);
        (target || document.querySelector('.tab[aria-pressed="true"]')).focus();
      }
      const id = () => typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function' ? crypto.randomUUID() : `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;
      const send = () => {
        if (!initialized || parent === window) return;
        latestRequest = ++saveSequence;
        if (document.activeElement === byId('retry')) byId('title').focus();
        byId('retry').style.display = 'none';
        status('Saving to your capsule…');
        parent.postMessage({ type: 'omi:save', appId: APP_ID, requestId: latestRequest, data: { version: 1, entries } }, parentOrigin);
      };
      function normalize(data) {
        if (!data || typeof data !== 'object' || data.version !== 1 || !Array.isArray(data.entries)) return [];
        const seen = new Set();
        return data.entries.slice(0, MAX_ENTRIES).filter(item => {
          if (!item || typeof item !== 'object' || typeof item.id !== 'string' || !item.id || item.id.length > 100 || seen.has(item.id)) return false;
          if (typeof item.title !== 'string' || !item.title.trim() || item.title.length > 80 || typeof item.body !== 'string' || item.body.length > 800) return false;
          if (!['note', 'task'].includes(item.kind) || typeof item.done !== 'boolean' || typeof item.createdAt !== 'string' || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(item.createdAt))) return false;
          seen.add(item.id);
          return true;
        }).map(item => ({ id: item.id, title: item.title, body: item.body, kind: item.kind, done: item.done, createdAt: item.createdAt }));
      }
      function button(label, onClick, className, accessibleLabel) {
        const element = document.createElement('button');
        element.type = 'button';
        element.textContent = label;
        if (className) element.className = className;
        if (accessibleLabel) element.setAttribute('aria-label', accessibleLabel);
        element.addEventListener('click', onClick);
        return element;
      }
      function clearComposer() {
        editing = null;
        byId('title').value = '';
        byId('body').value = '';
        document.querySelector('input[name="kind"][value="note"]').checked = true;
        byId('composer-title').textContent = 'Leave a little something';
        byId('submit').textContent = 'Add to the board';
        byId('cancel').style.display = 'none';
        validation('');
      }
      function render() {
        const list = byId('entries');
        list.replaceChildren();
        byId('count').textContent = `${entries.length} ${entries.length === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'}`;
        const visible = entries.filter(entry => filter === 'all' || entry.kind === filter);
        if (!visible.length) {
          const empty = document.createElement('div');
          empty.className = 'empty';
          // All markup here is static. User content is rendered with textContent below.
          empty.innerHTML = '<svg width="37" height="37" viewBox="0 0 32 32" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.2" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="6" y="4" width="20" height="25" rx="4"/><path d="M12 4V2m8 2V2M11 12h10m-10 5h10m-10 5h6"/></svg>';
          const title = document.createElement('h3');
          title.textContent = filter === 'task' ? 'A little room to make plans' : filter === 'note' ? 'Your thoughts belong here' : 'Make this space your own';
          const detail = document.createElement('p');
          detail.textContent = filter === 'task' ? 'Add a to-do and check it off when it’s done.' : 'Start with a note or a to-do. The board grows with the things you add.';
          empty.append(title, detail);
          list.append(empty);
        }
        visible.forEach((entry, index) => {
          const card = document.createElement('article');
          card.className = `entry${entry.kind === 'task' && entry.done ? ' done' : ''}`;
          card.dataset.entryId = entry.id;
          card.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', `entry-heading-${index}`);
          const top = document.createElement('div');
          top.className = 'entry-top';
          const kind = document.createElement('span');
          kind.className = 'kind-label';
          kind.textContent = entry.kind === 'note' ? 'A little note' : entry.done ? 'To-do · complete' : 'To-do · open';
          const date = document.createElement('time');
          date.dateTime = entry.createdAt;
          date.textContent = new Date(entry.createdAt).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
          top.append(kind, date);
          const title = document.createElement('h3');
          title.id = `entry-heading-${index}`;
          title.textContent = entry.title;
          const body = document.createElement('p');
          body.textContent = entry.body;
          const actions = document.createElement('div');
          actions.className = 'entry-actions';
          if (entry.kind === 'task') {
            const complete = button(entry.done ? 'Reopen' : 'Complete', () => {
              entry.done = !entry.done;
              send(); render();
              focusEntry(entry.id, 'complete');
              announce(`${entry.title} ${entry.done ? 'completed' : 'reopened'}.`);
            }, 'complete', `${entry.done ? 'Reopen' : 'Complete'} ${entry.title}`);
            complete.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(entry.done));
            actions.append(complete);
          }
          actions.append(button('Edit', () => {
            editing = entry.id;
            byId('title').value = entry.title;
            byId('body').value = entry.body;
            document.querySelector(`input[name="kind"][value="${entry.kind}"]`).checked = true;
            byId('composer-title').textContent = 'A little change';
            byId('submit').textContent = 'Save changes';
            byId('cancel').style.display = 'block';
            validation('');
            byId('title').focus();
            announce(`Editing ${entry.title}.`);
          }, 'edit', `Edit ${entry.title}`));
          actions.append(button('Remove', () => {
            removed = { entry: { ...entry }, index: entries.findIndex(item => item.id === entry.id) };
            entries = entries.filter(item => item.id !== entry.id);
            if (editing === entry.id) clearComposer();
            byId('undo-notice').style.display = 'flex';
            send(); render();
            byId('undo').focus();
            announce(`Removed ${entry.title}. Undo is available.`);
          }, 'remove', `Remove ${entry.title}`));
          card.append(top, title);
          if (entry.body) card.append(body);
          card.append(actions);
          list.append(card);
        });
      }
      window.addEventListener('message', event => {
        if (event.source !== parent || parent === window || !event.data || event.data.appId !== APP_ID) return;
        if (initialized && event.data.requestId === latestRequest && latestRequest > 0) {
          if (event.data.type === 'omi:saved') {
            status('Saved in your Personal Capsule.');
            byId('retry').style.display = 'none';
          } else if (event.data.type === 'omi:save-error') {
            status('Your latest changes aren’t saved. Try saving again before you leave.');
            byId('retry').style.display = 'block';
          }
        }
        if (event.data.type !== 'omi:init') return;
        // Readiness can be acknowledged repeatedly without overwriting unsaved local edits.
        if (initialized) return;
        parentOrigin = event.origin && event.origin !== 'null' ? event.origin : '*';
        entries = normalize(event.data.data);
        initialized = true;
        for (const name of ['title', 'body', 'submit']) byId(name).disabled = false;
        status('Your board is ready.');
        render();
      });
      byId('submit').addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!initialized) return;
        const title = byId('title').value.trim();
        const body = byId('body').value.trim();
        if (!title) { validation('Give your note or to-do a title.', true); byId('title').focus(); return; }
        if (!editing && entries.length >= MAX_ENTRIES) { validation('Your board holds 50 items. Remove an older item to make room.'); return; }
        const kind = document.querySelector('input[name="kind"]:checked').value;
        const wasEditing = Boolean(editing);
        if (editing) {
          const entry = entries.find(item => item.id === editing);
          if (!entry) { clearComposer(); validation('That item is no longer on the board.'); byId('title').focus(); return; }
          Object.assign(entry, { title, body, kind, done: kind === 'task' && entry.done });
        } else entries.unshift({ id: id(), title, body, kind, done: false, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() });
        clearComposer(); send(); render(); byId('title').focus();
        announce(`${wasEditing ? 'Updated' : 'Added'} ${title}.`);
      });
      byId('title').addEventListener('keydown', event => {
        if (event.key === 'Enter' && !event.isComposing) { event.preventDefault(); byId('submit').click(); }
      });
      byId('body').addEventListener('keydown', event => {
        if (event.key === 'Enter' && (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) && !event.isComposing) { event.preventDefault(); byId('submit').click(); }
      });
      byId('title').addEventListener('input', () => { if (byId('title').value.trim()) validation(''); });
      byId('cancel').addEventListener('click', () => {
        const entryId = editing;
        clearComposer();
        focusEntry(entryId);
        announce('Editing canceled.');
      });
      byId('retry').addEventListener('click', send);
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(tab => tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
        filter = tab.dataset.filter;
        document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(item => item.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(item === tab)));
        render();
        const visibleCount = entries.filter(entry => filter === 'all' || entry.kind === filter).length;
        announce(`${tab.textContent} selected. ${visibleCount} ${visibleCount === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'} shown.`);
      }));
      byId('undo').addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!removed) return;
        if (entries.length >= MAX_ENTRIES) { validation('Remove an item before restoring this one.'); return; }
        const restored = removed.entry;
        entries.splice(removed.index, 0, removed.entry);
        removed = null;
        byId('undo-notice').style.display = 'none';
        send(); render();
        focusEntry(restored.id);
        announce(`Restored ${restored.title}.`);
      });
      byId('dismiss-undo').addEventListener('click', () => {
        removed = null;
        byId('undo-notice').style.display = 'none';
        document.querySelector('.tab[aria-pressed="true"]').focus();
        announce('Undo dismissed.');
      });
      window.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
        if (event.key !== 'Escape' || event.defaultPrevented || event.isComposing || event.repeat || !initialized || parent === window) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        parent.postMessage({ type: 'omi:close', appId: APP_ID }, parentOrigin);
      });
      render();
      if (parent !== window) parent.postMessage({ type: 'omi:ready', appId: APP_ID }, '*');
      else status('Open Eagle’s Nest through OMI to use your Personal Capsule.');
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

===== BEGIN FILE source-review/omi-client/cartridges/eagles-nest/README.md | SHA256 2a6c0e52492bc2fae87ccb373c5dfafb70f17fb9c47885822f3e6de76eedb890 | BYTES 4652 =====
# Eagle’s Nest: local family board

This is a new, self-contained Eagle’s Nest cartridge implementation. It was not imported from the original ZIP referenced by the registry. Its working features are notes, editable to-dos, completion, filtering, removal and undo. A new board starts empty.

The OMI host must verify its signed HTML bundle and require an unlocked Personal Capsule before mounting it. Run the cartridge in an opaque-origin iframe with `sandbox="allow-scripts"`; do not add `allow-same-origin`. The cartridge does not request camera, microphone, network, top-navigation, storage or core-write permissions. It cannot sign in independently.

## Host bridge

The cartridge sends `{ type: 'omi:ready', appId: 'eagles-nest' }` after startup. The host responds to that message or the iframe load event with `{ type: 'omi:init', appId: 'eagles-nest', data }`.

The data shape is `{ version: 1, entries: [{ id, title, body, kind, done, createdAt }] }`, where `kind` is `note` or `task`. The host owns persistence in an encrypted, per-cartridge namespace of the current capsule. The cartridge sends `{ type: 'omi:save', appId: 'eagles-nest', requestId, data }` after each change. `requestId` is an incrementing positive integer. The host must verify `event.source` is the active iframe, validate the app ID and payload, reject writes when the capsule is locked, and serialize durable writes in their received order. Messages never contain the full capsule or PIN.

After a durable write finishes, the host replies `{ type: 'omi:saved', appId: 'eagles-nest', requestId }`. On failure it replies `{ type: 'omi:save-error', appId: 'eagles-nest', requestId, error }`. The board shows a saving status until the latest request is acknowledged. A failed save leaves the in-frame changes available and offers a retry button; it does not claim they were persisted. Older acknowledgments cannot overwrite the latest save status.

The cartridge accepts initialization only from its parent window and only once per mount. Titles are limited to 80 characters, bodies to 800 characters, and boards to 50 entries. User text is rendered with `textContent`. The host should enforce equivalent payload limits and reject extra capabilities.

Escape inside the initialized cartridge sends `{ type: 'omi:close', appId: 'eagles-nest' }` to its recorded parent origin and prevents the key’s default action. The host must validate the active iframe source and app ID before closing that mount. This request grants no core-write capability. There are no nested cartridge dialogs that intercept Escape.

Recommended iframe CSP:

```text
default-src 'none'; script-src 'unsafe-inline'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; img-src data:; connect-src 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'
```

This board has local persistence through OMI. It does not provide family synchronization, authenticated communications, cloud services, emergency messaging or peer resource consumption. Those features require independently tested Talky Spider and identity services.

## Accessibility and keyboard use

The board uses native headings, labeled inputs, grouped radio buttons and buttons with contextual names such as “Edit Weekend plans.” Filters expose their selected state with `aria-pressed`. Separate, always-present live regions announce board changes, validation errors and durable-save status without rereading the entire board. The title input references its validation message and exposes `aria-invalid` when empty submission fails.

Tab and Shift+Tab reach every action. Enter in the title adds or saves an item; Enter in details adds a new line, while Control+Enter or Command+Enter saves. Escape requests closure of this mount through the host. Editing moves focus to the title. Cancel returns focus to the item’s Edit button. Completing a task preserves focus on its toggle. Removal moves focus to Undo; restoring returns focus to the restored item, and dismissing Undo returns to the selected filter. Retrying a failed save moves focus to the title before hiding the retry button. If an item is hidden by a filter, focus falls back to that filter.

These actions use JavaScript buttons inside a named `role="form"` container because the intentionally restricted sandbox does not grant `allow-forms`. The cartridge preserves native input and button keyboard behavior without permitting a native form submission, navigation or network request. Mobile presentation follows DOM order; visible focus and reduced-motion preferences are supported. Automated and browser keyboard checks supplement, rather than establish, compatibility with every assistive technology.

===== END FILE source-review/omi-client/cartridges/eagles-nest/README.md =====

===== BEGIN FILE source-review/README.md | SHA256 2f06ca8d501f8a6cd1ff95f0fde9b4105b79113525464ee43677f486769b0c2d | BYTES 9352 =====
# OMI · KAOS LABS FOR HUMANITY

A runnable OMI universal client with an encrypted Personal Capsule, a signed isolated Eagle's Nest family board, a governance workbench, and the Dual Merge Codex numerical laboratory, informed by **Oracle Mechanism of Interlock — Master Consolidated Whitepaper** (May 2026).

## Run

Requires Node.js 24 or newer.

```sh
npm ci
npm run build
npm start
```

The complete client and forum API use port 8080. For client development, `npm run dev` uses port 5173 and proxies the forum API to the backend. Use localhost or HTTPS: browser cryptography requires a secure context. Create a Personal Capsule with a name and an **8–64 digit PIN**, then open Applications or Governance. Keep the PIN safe: this local edition has no account recovery or remote backup. Clearing browser storage deletes the capsule.

```sh
npm test             # encryption, sharing, governance and signature boundary tests
npm run build        # TypeScript check and production bundle
npm run preview      # serve production bundle on port 4173
npm run test:browser # production browser integration checks; run build first
npm run test:a11y    # automated accessibility and keyboard workflow checks
npm run test:relay   # outward HTTP relay integration checks
npm run test:forum-browser # forum UI against the real API; run build first
npm run test:git-relay # append-only Git mailbox and worker integration checks
npm run test:station # multi-agent headless scheduler and failure isolation
npm run test:advisory # durable team plans, assignments and status queues
npm run test:agents  # headless relay, mailroom, adapter, installer and packaging checks
npm run test:simulation # five synthetic seats with real Git and mock HTTP providers
```

Browser checks use Playwright and `/usr/bin/chromium` when available. Else install its browser with `npx playwright install chromium`, or set the non-secret `OMI_CHROMIUM_PATH` to a Chromium executable.

## What works

- **Personal Capsule:** schema-validated local profile and privacy settings, PBKDF2-SHA256/AES-256-GCM encryption, PIN unlock and lock. App data and audit records are encrypted together; plaintext names, PINs, and capsule contents are not stored in localStorage.
- **Eagle's Nest:** an original local notes/to-do board, separately implemented in `omi-client/cartridges/eagles-nest`. This is a new first cartridge, not an import of the absent ZIP named in the registry. Ed25519 signature verification precedes mounting. Its opaque iframe blocks network requests, parent DOM/storage access, camera, and microphone. The host persists only its bounded, namespaced board data.
- **Governance:** Pandora input checks, Dragon's explicit provisional risk/latency policy, Jiminy Cricket harm/reversibility inputs, Taipan's human-review gate, the fixed Wise Quorum perspective sequence and Solomon arbitration, separate human authorization, and eKernel containment/clearance. The only executable operation creates a local JSON artifact. Decisions are saved before promotion; all six perspectives must be recorded before authorization.
- **Canonization Chain:** canonical SHA-256-linked local records, integrity checks, encrypted persistence and JSON export. It is tamper-evident within the saved history; it has no external immutable anchor and cannot detect replacement of the whole chain.
- **Codex laboratory:** the document's exact Keeper–Tether ODEs and control injection, a bounded adaptive Dormand–Prince RK45 solver, editable parameters, capacity/protocol time plots, a phase portrait, full Jacobian and residual diagnostics. Complete parameters, solver settings, and sampled results are recorded before JSON/CSV export. Defaults are illustrative. The solver and Jacobian were cross-checked independently against SciPy and finite differences; numerical agreement is not a stability proof.
- **Resource sharing:** explicit time-limited authorization, default off, maximum 15% of currently idle resources, and immediate policy yield to foreground activity. Authorization resets on lock/unlock. This is a tested policy and UI; no OS/peer resources are measured or consumed.
- **Shared forum:** separate authenticated participant identities, durable SQLite topics/replies, server-assigned message IDs, cursor-based fetching, stable retry keys, revocation, and an accessible browser client. The outbound worker preserves inbox state and prevents duplicate delivery. See [API and deployment instructions](docs/forum-api.md).

Read [the keyboard and screen-reader guide](docs/accessibility.md) for OMI without relying on charts, color, or a mouse. Numerical results are available as a paginated table and full-precision CSV/JSON. Dialogs, task controls, navigation, and governance review include focus and status feedback.

For model coordination, Dot has verified real read/write access to the [omi-relay Git mailbox](https://github.com/nighteyes-neversleep/kaos_labs/tree/omi-relay). [The worker guide](relay/GIT-MAILBOX.md) provides POSIX and PowerShell setup for outward connections. DeepSeek, Claude, Muse, and other participants need their own authorized Git connection and state directory; a seat is connected only after its acknowledgement is observed. No model automatically executes a forum message.

For many agents on Station Node, use [the headless station launcher](relay/STATION.md). It manages separate identities and inbox/outbox directories with bounded concurrency, disabled-seat controls, retry backoff, and independent failure handling. It requires no browser and does not invoke models or execute incoming messages automatically.

[Team advisories](relay/ADVISORIES.md) carry plans, task ownership, progress, blockers, results, and acknowledgements through the same mailbox. Use the canonical `kaos_labs/omi-relay` branch; Firebrain is a separate connection probe. A report relayed by the owner is attributed as relayed, not presented as a direct model post. Muse's reported read-only connection and Route66's missing native Git login remain external access blockers.

The [per-user Ubuntu installer](node/README.md) discovers installed AI clients, records a roster, and preserves queues and existing configuration. The [mailroom](relay/MAILROOM.md) sorts personal assignments, explicit broadcasts, unaddressed mail and quarantine. An [optional model adapter](relay/MODEL-ADAPTER.md) invokes only an explicitly configured Ollama or OpenAI-compatible API; it does not control hosted chat windows or execute model text. Its durable results and stable IDs limit repeated work across restarts. Models still need their own verified connection.

The [all-AI bundle builder](bundle/README.md) issues a ZIP and a single source-containing text handoff after a matching clean-commit virtual simulation. The simulation uses synthetic providers and does not prove live model connections. [Corporate mail flows](docs/CORPORATE-MAIL-FLOW.md) explain personal inboxes, distribution lists and separate task ownership. The [owner-relayed Gemini review corrections](docs/RELAY-REVIEW-CORRECTIONS.md) distinguish source evidence from pasted claims.

NoMachine supplies optional remote desktop access, not a compute cluster, persistent cloud storage, or automatic model membership. The node kit includes read-only persistence diagnostics and an optional private canary to compare Station before and after a disconnect. It does not reset accounts, erase desktops, restart NoMachine, or activate licences.

## Boundaries

The master whitepaper is an architecture and validation proposal. It supplies no executable WACV formula, numerical risk thresholds, Taipan semantic-proof protocol, or deployed stability guarantee. This implementation identifies its local thresholds as provisional and records operator-supplied risk estimates. Human attestations and content hashes do not establish ethical truth or scientific safety.

The local development signing key is generated under ignored `.omi-dev/` by `scripts/sign-cartridges.mjs`. Its public key is pinned into the client build; signed bundles are generated under ignored `public/cartridges/`. Do not distribute the private key or treat this local signer as production module certification. Rebuild after changing a cartridge. Production requires a reviewed publisher trust/rotation process and independently reviewed deployment security.

Six additional app families remain registered with source unavailable. Cloud/peer Talky Spider transport, calls, biometrics/passkeys, actual resource pooling, production identity federation, formal alignment proofs, and external-system rollback are not implemented. Private capsule data never enters a resource pool.

To explore the model, create or unlock a capsule, open **Codex laboratory**, and run the default experiment. Change parameters and rerun to compare behavior. Inputs that violate a model assumption produce a warning; nonfinite or out-of-domain inputs are rejected. A difficult run may stop at a numerical limit and retain its valid partial trajectory. Exported runs include the model version, all parameters, solver settings, samples, and the audit-head reference.

See [the architecture notes](docs/architecture.md) and [the repository working contract](CLAUDE.md). The existing `omi-client/` manifests and capsule schema remain the source of client terminology and boundaries.

===== END FILE source-review/README.md =====

===== BEGIN FILE source-review/scripts/sign-cartridges.mjs | SHA256 d7565f4cd5333111d6d5e4713ea882a3610f42faf0cab9f131d09432dda77f2a | BYTES 2045 =====
import { generateKeyPairSync, createPublicKey, sign } from 'node:crypto';
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { resolve, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const privatePath = resolve(root, '.omi-dev/cartridge-signing-key.pem');
await mkdir(dirname(privatePath), { recursive: true });
let privateKey;
try {
  privateKey = await readFile(privatePath, 'utf8');
} catch (error) {
  if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
  const pair = generateKeyPairSync('ed25519');
  privateKey = pair.privateKey.export({ type: 'pkcs8', format: 'pem' });
  await writeFile(privatePath, privateKey, { mode: 0o600, flag: 'wx' });
}
const publicKey = createPublicKey(privateKey).export({ type: 'spki', format: 'der' }).toString('base64');
await mkdir(resolve(root, 'src/generated'), { recursive: true });
await writeFile(resolve(root, 'src/generated/cartridge-trust.json'), JSON.stringify({ publicKey, label: 'Local development signer' }, null, 2) + '\n');
const source = await readFile(resolve(root, 'omi-client/cartridges/eagles-nest/index.html'), 'utf8');
const csp = "default-src 'none'; script-src 'unsafe-inline'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; img-src data:; connect-src 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none';";
if (!source.includes('<head>')) throw new Error('Cartridge must have an explicit head element');
const html = source.replace('<head>', `<head><meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="${csp}">`);
const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ appId: 'eagles-nest', version: '0.1.0', html }), 'utf8');
const bundle = { payload: payload.toString('base64'), signature: sign(null, payload, privateKey).toString('base64') };
const out = resolve(root, 'public/cartridges');
await mkdir(out, { recursive: true });
await writeFile(resolve(out, 'eagles-nest.bundle.json'), JSON.stringify(bundle));
console.log('Signed Eagle’s Nest for local development. This is not a production trust attestation.');

===== END FILE source-review/scripts/sign-cartridges.mjs =====

===== BEGIN FILE source-review/server/credential-paths.test.ts | SHA256 8a1f937fcfd1dec9770e91b4236f6dc5004080e8ae952da7070a0893ca4dabf0 | BYTES 1817 =====
import { chmodSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ForumStore } from './forum-store';

it.skipIf(!process.getuid)('refuses shared credential directories before writing a key or registering an identity', () => {
  const directory = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'omi-private-path-'));
  const store = new ForumStore(join(directory, 'data', 'forum.sqlite'));
  const parent = join(directory, 'shared');
  mkdirSync(parent, { mode: 0o700 });
  chmodSync(parent, 0o777);
  const destination = join(parent, 'seat.json');
  try {
    const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['server/participant-cli.ts', '--id', 'shared-seat', '--name', 'Shared seat', '--output', destination], {
      cwd: process.cwd(), encoding: 'utf8', env: { ...process.env, OMI_FORUM_DATA_DIR: join(directory, 'data') },
    });
    expect(result.status).toBe(1);
    expect(result.stderr).toContain('owner-only');
    expect(existsSync(destination)).toBe(false);
    expect(store.listParticipants()).toEqual([]);
  } finally { store.close(); rmSync(directory, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
});

it('refuses a checkout ancestor as the private data directory without changing its permissions', () => {
  const ancestor = dirname(process.cwd());
  const before = statSync(ancestor).mode;
  const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['server/index.ts', '--prepare'], {
    cwd: process.cwd(), encoding: 'utf8', env: { ...process.env, OMI_FORUM_DATA_DIR: ancestor },
  });
  expect(result.status).toBe(1);
  expect(result.stderr).toContain('outside the checkout and its ancestors');
  expect(statSync(ancestor).mode).toBe(before);
});

===== END FILE source-review/server/credential-paths.test.ts =====

===== BEGIN FILE source-review/server/forum-store.test.ts | SHA256 710e7156fd67e22ef9a904e573a938c4c7182a3d164b3869d3b61fe3cc4721d3 | BYTES 20297 =====
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { chmodSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ForumStore, ForumStoreError, type Participant } from './forum-store';

let dir: string;
let path: string;
let store: ForumStore;
let alice: Participant;
let bob: Participant;
const ALICE_TOKEN = 'alice-secret-token-3e42b69cde';
const BOB_TOKEN = 'bob-secret-token-934ab93acda';

beforeEach(() => {
  dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'omi-forum-store-'));
  path = join(dir, 'private', 'forum.sqlite');
  store = new ForumStore(path);
  alice = store.createParticipant({ id: 'alice', name: 'Alice', role: 'admin', token: ALICE_TOKEN });
  bob = store.createParticipant({ id: 'route66', name: 'Route 66 worker', role: 'participant', token: BOB_TOKEN });
});
afterEach(() => { store.close(); rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); });

function topic(clientMessageId = 'topic-1') {
  return store.createTopic(alice, { title: 'OMI development', body: 'What should we build next?', clientMessageId });
}

describe('durable authenticated forum store', () => {
  it('authenticates separate participant identities and never returns or persists raw tokens', () => {
    expect(store.authenticate(ALICE_TOKEN)).toEqual(alice);
    expect(store.authenticate(BOB_TOKEN)).toEqual(bob);
    const namesake = store.createParticipant({ id: 'alice-namesake', name: 'Alice', role: 'participant', token: 'namesake-token-c2be84d9637d' });
    expect(namesake.name).toBe(alice.name);
    expect(namesake.id).not.toBe(alice.id);
    expect(store.authenticate('namesake-token-c2be84d9637d')).toEqual(namesake);
    expect(store.authenticate('wrong-token-with-enough-characters')).toBeNull();
    expect(store.authenticate('')).toBeNull();
    expect(Object.keys(alice).sort()).toEqual(['createdAt', 'id', 'name', 'role']);
    expect(readFileSync(path).toString('latin1')).not.toContain(ALICE_TOKEN);
    // Closing checkpoints the WAL, so inspect the complete database as well.
    store.close();
    expect(readFileSync(path).toString('latin1')).not.toContain(ALICE_TOKEN);
    expect(readFileSync(path).toString('latin1')).not.toContain(BOB_TOKEN);
    store = new ForumStore(path);
    expect(store.authenticate(ALICE_TOKEN)?.name).toBe('Alice');
    expect(statSync(path).mode & 0o777).toBe(0o600);
  });

  it('starts without seeded conversations and persists a topic plus independent replies', () => {
    expect(store.listTopics()).toEqual([]);
    expect(store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit: 20 })).toEqual({ messages: [], nextCursor: 0, hasMore: false });
    const created = topic();
    const reply = store.postReply(bob, created.topic.id, { body: 'I can work on the local worker.', clientMessageId: 'reply-1' });
    expect(created.message.participantId).toBe(alice.id);
    expect(reply.participantId).toBe(bob.id);
    expect(reply.sequence).toBeGreaterThan(created.message.sequence);
    expect(reply.id).not.toBe(created.message.id);
    store.close();
    store = new ForumStore(path);
    expect(store.getTopic(created.topic.id)).toEqual(created.topic);
    expect(store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit: 20 }).messages).toEqual([created.message, reply]);
  });

  it('returns the original result for identical retries without new messages or topics', () => {
    const created = topic();
    expect(topic()).toEqual(created);
    const input = { body: 'Acknowledged', clientMessageId: 'reply-1' };
    const reply = store.postReply(bob, created.topic.id, input);
    expect(store.postReply(bob, created.topic.id, input)).toEqual(reply);
    expect(store.listTopics()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit: 20 }).messages).toHaveLength(2);
    // Idempotency keys are scoped to the author, so a separate participant may use the same client ID.
    const other = store.postReply(alice, created.topic.id, input);
    expect(other.id).not.toBe(reply.id);
  });

  it('rejects reused IDs with different payloads across create and reply operations', () => {
    const created = topic();
    const conflict = () => store.createTopic(alice, { title: 'Changed', body: created.message.body, clientMessageId: 'topic-1' });
    expect(conflict).toThrow(ForumStoreError);
    try { conflict(); } catch (error) { expect((error as ForumStoreError).status).toBe(409); }
    expect(() => store.postReply(alice, created.topic.id, { body: created.message.body, clientMessageId: 'topic-1' })).toThrow('different content');
    const otherTopic = topic('topic-2');
    store.postReply(bob, created.topic.id, { body: 'Same text', clientMessageId: 'reply-1' });
    expect(() => store.postReply(bob, otherTopic.topic.id, { body: 'Same text', clientMessageId: 'reply-1' })).toThrow('different content');
    expect(store.listTopics()).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit: 20 }).messages).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  it('paginates a global monotone cursor without gaps, including messages appended between fetches', () => {
    const created = topic();
    const another = topic('topic-2');
    for (let n = 0; n < 8; n++) store.postReply(bob, n % 2 ? created.topic.id : another.topic.id, { body: `Reply ${n}`, clientMessageId: `reply-${n}` });
    const first = store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit: 3 });
    expect(first.hasMore).toBe(true);
    store.postReply(alice, created.topic.id, { body: 'Appended after the first fetch', clientMessageId: 'late-reply' });
    const messages = [...first.messages];
    let cursor = first.nextCursor;
    while (true) {
      const page = store.listMessages({ after: cursor, limit: 3 });
      messages.push(...page.messages);
      cursor = page.nextCursor;
      if (!page.hasMore) break;
    }
    expect(messages).toHaveLength(11);
    expect(new Set(messages.map((value) => value.id)).size).toBe(11);
    expect(messages.map((value) => value.sequence)).toEqual(Array.from({ length: 11 }, (_, index) => index + 1));
    expect(store.listMessages({ after: cursor, limit: 3 })).toEqual({ messages: [], nextCursor: cursor, hasMore: false });
    const selected = store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit: 200, topicId: created.topic.id });
    expect(selected.messages.every((value) => value.topicId === created.topic.id)).toBe(true);
    expect(selected.messages).toHaveLength(6);
  });

  it('rejects invalid content, cursor arguments, missing topics, and invented actors', () => {
    expect(() => store.createTopic(alice, { title: 'x'.repeat(201), body: 'Hello', clientMessageId: 'valid' })).toThrow('Topic title');
    expect(() => store.createTopic(alice, { title: 'Valid', body: ' '.repeat(12), clientMessageId: 'valid' })).toThrow('Message body');
    expect(() => store.createTopic(alice, { title: 'Valid', body: 'x'.repeat(12_001), clientMessageId: 'valid' })).toThrow('Message body');
    expect(() => store.createTopic(alice, { title: 'Valid', body: 'Hello', clientMessageId: "bad';DROP" })).toThrow('Client message ID');
    const created = topic();
    expect(() => store.postReply(bob, 'does-not-exist', { body: 'Hi', clientMessageId: 'reply' })).toThrow('does not exist');
    expect(() => store.postReply({ ...bob, role: 'admin' }, created.topic.id, { body: 'Hi', clientMessageId: 'reply' })).toThrow('identity is invalid');
    expect(() => store.postReply({ ...bob, id: 'unknown' }, created.topic.id, { body: 'Hi', clientMessageId: 'reply' })).toThrow('identity is invalid');
    for (const after of [-1, 1.5, NaN, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER + 1]) expect(() => store.listMessages({ after, limit: 20 })).toThrow('Cursor');
    for (const limit of [0, 201, NaN, 1.2]) expect(() => store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit })).toThrow('Page size');
    expect(store.listTopics()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('revokes tokens and future posting while preserving authored messages and safe identities', () => {
    const created = topic();
    const reply = store.postReply(bob, created.topic.id, { body: 'Keep my authorship', clientMessageId: 'reply' });
    store.revokeParticipant(bob.id);
    store.revokeParticipant(bob.id); // Revocation is safe to repeat.
    expect(store.authenticate(BOB_TOKEN)).toBeNull();
    expect(store.authenticate(ALICE_TOKEN)).toEqual(alice);
    expect(() => store.postReply(bob, created.topic.id, { body: 'Stale actor', clientMessageId: 'after-revoke' })).toThrow('identity is invalid');
    expect(store.listParticipants()).toContainEqual(bob);
    expect(store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit: 20 }).messages).toContainEqual(reply);
    expect(() => store.revokeParticipant('unknown')).toThrow('does not exist');
    expect(JSON.stringify(store.listParticipants())).not.toContain(BOB_TOKEN);
  });

  it('rotates an active token without changing identity, authorship or message history', () => {
    const created = topic();
    const reply = store.postReply(bob, created.topic.id, { body: 'Before rotation', clientMessageId: 'before-rotation' });
    const replacement = 'new-route66-token-1785e9a14aa983';
    expect(store.rotateParticipantToken(bob.id, replacement)).toEqual(bob);
    expect(store.authenticate(BOB_TOKEN)).toBeNull();
    expect(store.authenticate(replacement)).toEqual(bob);
    expect(store.authenticate(ALICE_TOKEN)).toEqual(alice);
    expect(store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit: 20 }).messages).toContainEqual(reply);
    store.close();
    store = new ForumStore(path);
    expect(store.authenticate(replacement)).toEqual(bob);
    expect(store.authenticate(BOB_TOKEN)).toBeNull();
    expect(readFileSync(path).toString('latin1')).not.toContain(replacement);
  });

  it('does not reactivate revoked identities or replace tokens after failed rotation', () => {
    const unchanged = store.authenticate(BOB_TOKEN);
    for (const replacement of ['', 'too-short', ALICE_TOKEN, BOB_TOKEN]) {
      expect(() => store.rotateParticipantToken(bob.id, replacement)).toThrow(ForumStoreError);
      expect(store.authenticate(BOB_TOKEN)).toEqual(unchanged);
      expect(store.authenticate(ALICE_TOKEN)).toEqual(alice);
    }
    expect(() => store.rotateParticipantToken('missing', 'new-route66-token-1785e9a14aa983')).toThrow('does not exist');
    store.revokeParticipant(bob.id);
    expect(() => store.rotateParticipantToken(bob.id, 'new-route66-token-1785e9a14aa983')).toThrow('revoked');
    expect(store.authenticate('new-route66-token-1785e9a14aa983')).toBeNull();
    expect(store.authenticate(BOB_TOKEN)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('writes private CLI credentials before creation and rotates into a new durable file without printing keys', () => {
    const output = join(dir, 'created.json');
    const run = (...args: string[]) => spawnSync(process.execPath, ['server/participant-cli.ts', ...args], {
      cwd: process.cwd(), encoding: 'utf8', env: { ...process.env, OMI_FORUM_DATA_DIR: join(dir, 'private') },
    });
    const created = run('--id', 'cli-seat', '--name', 'CLI seat', '--output', output);
    expect(created.status, created.stderr).toBe(0);
    const initial = JSON.parse(readFileSync(output, 'utf8'));
    expect(statSync(output).mode & 0o777).toBe(0o600);
    expect(store.authenticate(initial.token)?.id).toBe('cli-seat');
    expect(created.stdout + created.stderr).not.toContain(initial.token);
    const rotatedOutput = join(dir, 'rotated.json');
    const rotated = run('--id', 'cli-seat', '--rotate', '--output', rotatedOutput);
    expect(rotated.status, rotated.stderr).toBe(0);
    const replacement = JSON.parse(readFileSync(rotatedOutput, 'utf8'));
    expect(store.authenticate(initial.token)).toBeNull();
    expect(store.authenticate(replacement.token)).toEqual({ ...replacement.participant });
    expect(rotated.stdout + rotated.stderr).not.toContain(replacement.token);
    expect(readFileSync(output, 'utf8')).toBe(JSON.stringify(initial, null, 2) + '\n');
    const repeated = run('--id', 'cli-seat', '--name', 'Duplicate', '--output', join(dir, 'duplicate.json'));
    expect(repeated.status).toBe(1);
    expect(existsSync(join(dir, 'duplicate.json'))).toBe(false);
    expect(store.authenticate(replacement.token)?.name).toBe('CLI seat');
  });

  it('keeps the database unchanged when a private CLI credential file cannot be written', () => {
    // Directory permissions create a real post-validation write failure. Root
    // bypasses mode bits, so use an existing read-only system directory there.
    const destination = process.getuid?.() === 0 ? '/proc/omi-credential-write-denied.json' : join(dir, 'locked', 'credential.json');
    if (process.getuid?.() !== 0) {
      mkdirSync(join(dir, 'locked'), { mode: 0o500 });
    }
    const failed = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['server/participant-cli.ts', '--id', 'failed-seat', '--name', 'Failed seat', '--output', destination], {
      cwd: process.cwd(), encoding: 'utf8', env: { ...process.env, OMI_FORUM_DATA_DIR: join(dir, 'private') },
    });
    if (process.getuid?.() !== 0) chmodSync(join(dir, 'locked'), 0o700);
    expect(failed.status).not.toBe(0);
    expect(store.listParticipants().some(participant => participant.id === 'failed-seat')).toBe(false);
    expect(existsSync(destination)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('retains private candidate credentials if database validation fails after writing', () => {
    const output = join(dir, 'unregistered.json');
    const failed = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['server/participant-cli.ts', '--id', 'invalid-seat', '--name', 'x'.repeat(81), '--output', output], {
      cwd: process.cwd(), encoding: 'utf8', env: { ...process.env, OMI_FORUM_DATA_DIR: join(dir, 'private') },
    });
    expect(failed.status).toBe(1);
    const candidate = JSON.parse(readFileSync(output, 'utf8'));
    expect(statSync(output).mode & 0o777).toBe(0o600);
    expect(store.authenticate(candidate.token)).toBeNull();
    expect(failed.stderr).toContain('retained');
    expect(failed.stdout + failed.stderr).not.toContain(candidate.token);
    expect(store.listParticipants().some(participant => participant.id === 'invalid-seat')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('migrates a v1 database without losing participant identities or token access', () => {
    const legacyPath = join(dir, 'legacy.sqlite');
    const legacy = new DatabaseSync(legacyPath);
    legacy.exec(`CREATE TABLE participants (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL, role TEXT NOT NULL,
      token_hash TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, created_at TEXT NOT NULL
    ) STRICT; PRAGMA user_version = 1;`);
    legacy.prepare('INSERT INTO participants (id, name, role, token_hash, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)')
      .run('legacy', 'Legacy participant', 'participant', createHash('sha256').update(BOB_TOKEN).digest('hex'), '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z');
    legacy.close();
    const migrated = new ForumStore(legacyPath);
    try {
      const identity = migrated.authenticate(BOB_TOKEN)!;
      expect(identity.id).toBe('legacy');
      const created = migrated.createTopic(identity, { title: 'Retained access', body: 'Migration worked', clientMessageId: 'legacy-message' });
      expect(created.message.participantId).toBe('legacy');
      migrated.revokeParticipant(identity.id);
      expect(migrated.authenticate(BOB_TOKEN)).toBeNull();
    } finally { migrated.close(); }
    const inspect = new DatabaseSync(legacyPath);
    expect(inspect.prepare('PRAGMA user_version').get()?.user_version).toBe(2);
    inspect.close();
  });

  it('serializes simultaneous v1 migrations without duplicate-column startup failure', async () => {
    const legacyPath = join(dir, 'concurrent-legacy.sqlite');
    const legacy = new DatabaseSync(legacyPath);
    legacy.exec(`CREATE TABLE participants (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL, role TEXT NOT NULL,
      token_hash TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, created_at TEXT NOT NULL
    ) STRICT; PRAGMA user_version = 1;`);
    legacy.close();
    const childPath = join(dir, 'migration-child.mjs');
    const moduleURL = new URL('./forum-store.ts', import.meta.url).href;
    // Pause one real connection immediately before ALTER. An unprotected second
    // constructor can migrate first and make this one fail. With BEGIN IMMEDIATE,
    // the second connection waits before inspecting or changing the schema.
    writeFileSync(childPath, `
      import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
      import { existsSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
      import { ForumStore } from ${JSON.stringify(moduleURL)};
      const [database, directory, seat] = process.argv.slice(2);
      const original = DatabaseSync.prototype.exec;
      DatabaseSync.prototype.exec = function(sql) {
        if (seat === 'second' && sql.trim() === 'BEGIN IMMEDIATE') {
          writeFileSync(directory + '/second-begin', 'ready');
        }
        if (seat === 'first' && sql.startsWith('ALTER TABLE participants ADD COLUMN active')) {
          writeFileSync(directory + '/first-alter', 'ready');
          const deadline = Date.now() + 5000;
          while (!existsSync(directory + '/release-first')) {
            if (Date.now() >= deadline) throw new Error('Migration fixture timed out');
            Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, 10);
          }
        }
        return original.call(this, sql);
      };
      try { const store = new ForumStore(database); store.close(); }
      catch (error) { console.error(error.message); process.exitCode = 1; }
    `);
    const launch = (seat: string) => {
      const process = spawn(globalThis.process.execPath, [childPath, legacyPath, dir, seat], { stdio: ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe'] });
      let errorOutput = '';
      process.stderr.setEncoding('utf8');
      process.stderr.on('data', text => { errorOutput += text; });
      const done = new Promise<{ code: number | null; errorOutput: string }>((resolve, reject) => {
        process.once('error', reject);
        process.once('close', code => resolve({ code, errorOutput }));
      });
      return { process, done };
    };
    const waitFor = async (path: string) => {
      const deadline = Date.now() + 3000;
      while (!existsSync(path)) {
        if (Date.now() >= deadline) throw new Error('Migration coordination marker timed out');
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 10));
      }
    };
    const first = launch('first');
    let second: ReturnType<typeof launch> | undefined;
    try {
      await waitFor(join(dir, 'first-alter'));
      second = launch('second');
      await Promise.race([second.done, waitFor(join(dir, 'second-begin'))]);
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'release-first'), 'continue');
      const outcomes = await Promise.all([first.done, second.done]);
      for (const result of outcomes) expect(result.code, result.errorOutput).toBe(0);
      const inspect = new DatabaseSync(legacyPath);
      try {
        expect(inspect.prepare('PRAGMA user_version').get()?.user_version).toBe(2);
        expect(inspect.prepare('PRAGMA table_info(participants)').all().filter(column => column.name === 'active')).toHaveLength(1);
      } finally { inspect.close(); }
    } finally {
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'release-first'), 'continue');
      first.process.kill(); second?.process.kill();
      await Promise.allSettled([first.done, ...(second ? [second.done] : [])]);
    }
  }, 10_000);

  it('uses bound parameters for content and rejects duplicate participant IDs or tokens', () => {
    const title = "Robert'); DROP TABLE topics; --";
    const created = store.createTopic(alice, { title, body: "<script>localStorage.clear()</script> ' OR '1'='1", clientMessageId: 'sql-test' });
    expect(store.getTopic(created.topic.id)?.title).toBe(title);
    expect(store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit: 20 }).messages[0].body).toContain('<script>');
    expect(() => store.createParticipant({ id: 'alice', name: 'Other Alice', role: 'participant', token: 'another-random-token-123456789' })).toThrow('already exists');
    expect(() => store.createParticipant({ id: 'other', name: 'Other', role: 'participant', token: BOB_TOKEN })).toThrow('already exists');
    expect(store.authenticate(BOB_TOKEN)).toEqual(bob);
  });
});

===== BEGIN FILE source-review/server/forum-store.ts | SHA256 922c96dc6ff3e330bb8faf7d8f02f3b6c3504a7daf75a1957b3a7c801085d9c3 | BYTES 15799 =====
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { chmodSync, closeSync, existsSync, lstatSync, mkdirSync, openSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';

export type ParticipantRole = 'admin' | 'participant';
export type Participant = { id: string; name: string; role: ParticipantRole; createdAt: string };
export type Topic = { id: string; title: string; createdAt: string; createdBy: string };
export type Message = {
  id: string;
  sequence: number;
  topicId: string;
  participantId: string;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
  clientMessageId: string;
};
export type TopicCreated = { topic: Topic; message: Message };
export type MessagePage = { messages: Message[]; nextCursor: number; hasMore: boolean };

export class ForumStoreError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly code: string;

  constructor(status: number, code: string, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ForumStoreError';
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
  }
}

type Row = Record<string, unknown>;
const MAX_TITLE_LENGTH = 200;
const MAX_BODY_LENGTH = 12_000;
const MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 200;
const VALID_ID = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,99}$/;
const VALID_TOKEN = /^[A-Za-z0-9._~-]{24,512}$/;

function id(value: unknown, label: string): string {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !VALID_ID.test(value)) {
    throw new ForumStoreError(400, 'INVALID_ID', `${label} must be a 1–100 character slug or UUID.`);
  }
  return value;
}

function content(value: unknown, label: string, maxLength: number, singleLine = false): string {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') throw new ForumStoreError(400, 'INVALID_CONTENT', `${label} is required.`);
  const result = value.trim();
  if (!result || result.length > maxLength || /[\u0000-\u0008\u000b\u000c\u000e-\u001f\u007f]/.test(result) ||
      (singleLine && /[\r\n\t]/.test(result))) {
    throw new ForumStoreError(400, 'INVALID_CONTENT', `${label} must contain 1–${maxLength} characters${singleLine ? ' on one line' : ''}.`);
  }
  return result;
}

function hash(value: string): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(value, 'utf8').digest('hex');
}

function participant(row: Row): Participant {
  return { id: String(row.id), name: String(row.name), role: row.role as ParticipantRole, createdAt: String(row.created_at) };
}

function topic(row: Row): Topic {
  return { id: String(row.id), title: String(row.title), createdAt: String(row.created_at), createdBy: String(row.created_by) };
}

function message(row: Row): Message {
  return {
    id: String(row.id), sequence: Number(row.sequence), topicId: String(row.topic_id), participantId: String(row.participant_id),
    body: String(row.body), createdAt: String(row.created_at), clientMessageId: String(row.client_message_id),
  };
}

/** Durable append-only forum data. HTTP handlers must authenticate each request before passing its actor. */
export class ForumStore {
  private readonly db: DatabaseSync;

  constructor(databasePath: string) {
    if (typeof databasePath !== 'string' || !databasePath.trim()) throw new Error('A private forum database path is required.');
    const dbPath = databasePath === ':memory:' ? databasePath : resolve(databasePath);
    if (dbPath !== ':memory:') {
      mkdirSync(dirname(dbPath), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
      if (existsSync(dbPath)) {
        if (!lstatSync(dbPath).isFile() || lstatSync(dbPath).isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error('Forum database must be a regular private file.');
      } else {
        // SQLite inherits a private database file mode for its WAL and shared-memory files.
        try { closeSync(openSync(dbPath, 'wx', 0o600)); }
        catch (error) {
          if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== 'EEXIST') throw error;
          if (!lstatSync(dbPath).isFile() || lstatSync(dbPath).isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error('Forum database must be a regular private file.');
        }
      }
      chmodSync(dbPath, 0o600);
    }
    this.db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath);
    let migrating = false;
    try {
      this.db.exec('PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON; PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000; PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;');
      // Serialize the schema read and every migration step across processes.
      // Reading columns before acquiring the write lock permits two starters to
      // race the same ALTER, leaving one unable to start despite a valid schema.
      this.db.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');
      migrating = true;
      const schemaVersion = this.db.prepare('PRAGMA user_version').get()?.user_version;
      if (schemaVersion !== 0 && schemaVersion !== 1 && schemaVersion !== 2) throw new Error('Unsupported forum database schema version.');
      this.db.exec(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS participants (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          name TEXT NOT NULL,
          role TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'participant')),
          token_hash TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (active IN (0, 1))
        ) STRICT;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS topics (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          title TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(title) BETWEEN 1 AND 200),
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          created_by TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES participants(id)
        ) STRICT;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
          sequence INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
          topic_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES topics(id),
          participant_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES participants(id),
          body TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(body) BETWEEN 1 AND 12000),
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          client_message_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          request_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
          UNIQUE (participant_id, client_message_id)
        ) STRICT;
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS messages_by_topic_sequence ON messages(topic_id, sequence);
      `);
      const participantColumns = this.db.prepare('PRAGMA table_info(participants)').all();
      if (!participantColumns.some((column) => column.name === 'active')) {
        this.db.exec('ALTER TABLE participants ADD COLUMN active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (active IN (0, 1))');
      }
      this.db.exec('PRAGMA user_version = 2');
      this.db.exec('COMMIT');
      migrating = false;
    } catch (error) {
      if (migrating) {
        try { this.db.exec('ROLLBACK'); } catch { /* Preserve the migration failure. */ }
      }
      this.db.close();
      throw error;
    }
  }

  close(): void { this.db.close(); }

  createParticipant(input: { id: string; name: string; role: ParticipantRole; token: string }): Participant {
    const participantId = id(input.id, 'Participant ID');
    const name = content(input.name, 'Participant name', 80, true);
    if (input.role !== 'admin' && input.role !== 'participant') throw new ForumStoreError(400, 'INVALID_ROLE', 'Participant role is invalid.');
    if (typeof input.token !== 'string' || !VALID_TOKEN.test(input.token)) {
      throw new ForumStoreError(400, 'INVALID_TOKEN', 'Use an opaque token containing 24–512 URL-safe characters.');
    }
    const tokenHash = hash(input.token);
    return this.transaction(() => {
      if (this.db.prepare('SELECT id FROM participants WHERE id = ? OR token_hash = ?').get(participantId, tokenHash)) {
        throw new ForumStoreError(409, 'PARTICIPANT_EXISTS', 'Participant identity or token already exists.');
      }
      const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
      this.db.prepare('INSERT INTO participants (id, name, role, token_hash, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)')
        .run(participantId, name, input.role, tokenHash, createdAt);
      return { id: participantId, name, role: input.role, createdAt };
    });
  }

  authenticate(token: string): Participant | null {
    if (typeof token !== 'string' || !VALID_TOKEN.test(token)) return null;
    // Only random opaque token digests are persisted or queried. Never return authentication material.
    const row = this.db.prepare('SELECT id, name, role, created_at FROM participants WHERE token_hash = ? AND active = 1').get(hash(token));
    return row ? participant(row) : null;
  }

  listParticipants(): Participant[] {
    // Retain safe names for the authorship of messages posted before token revocation.
    return this.db.prepare('SELECT id, name, role, created_at FROM participants ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC').all().map(participant);
  }

  revokeParticipant(participantId: string): void {
    const target = id(participantId, 'Participant ID');
    if (!this.db.prepare('SELECT id FROM participants WHERE id = ?').get(target)) {
      throw new ForumStoreError(404, 'PARTICIPANT_NOT_FOUND', 'Participant does not exist.');
    }
    this.db.prepare('UPDATE participants SET active = 0 WHERE id = ?').run(target);
  }

  rotateParticipantToken(participantId: string, token: string): Participant {
    const target = id(participantId, 'Participant ID');
    if (typeof token !== 'string' || !VALID_TOKEN.test(token)) {
      throw new ForumStoreError(400, 'INVALID_TOKEN', 'Use an opaque token containing 24–512 URL-safe characters.');
    }
    const tokenHash = hash(token);
    return this.transaction(() => {
      const row = this.db.prepare('SELECT id, name, role, created_at, active FROM participants WHERE id = ?').get(target);
      if (!row) throw new ForumStoreError(404, 'PARTICIPANT_NOT_FOUND', 'Participant does not exist.');
      if (row.active !== 1) throw new ForumStoreError(409, 'PARTICIPANT_REVOKED', 'A revoked participant cannot rotate or reactivate its token.');
      if (this.db.prepare('SELECT id FROM participants WHERE token_hash = ?').get(tokenHash)) {
        throw new ForumStoreError(409, 'TOKEN_EXISTS', 'The replacement token must be new and not already in use.');
      }
      this.db.prepare('UPDATE participants SET token_hash = ? WHERE id = ? AND active = 1').run(tokenHash, target);
      return participant(row);
    });
  }

  createTopic(actor: Participant, input: { title: string; body: string; clientMessageId: string }): TopicCreated {
    const author = this.requireActor(actor);
    const title = content(input.title, 'Topic title', MAX_TITLE_LENGTH, true);
    const body = content(input.body, 'Message body', MAX_BODY_LENGTH);
    const clientMessageId = id(input.clientMessageId, 'Client message ID');
    const requestHash = hash(JSON.stringify({ operation: 'create-topic', title, body }));
    return this.transaction(() => {
      const previous = this.existingRequest(author.id, clientMessageId, requestHash);
      if (previous) {
        const previousTopic = this.getTopic(previous.topicId);
        if (!previousTopic) throw new Error('Stored forum topic is missing.');
        return { topic: previousTopic, message: previous };
      }
      const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
      const createdTopic: Topic = { id: randomUUID(), title, createdAt, createdBy: author.id };
      this.db.prepare('INSERT INTO topics (id, title, created_at, created_by) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)')
        .run(createdTopic.id, title, createdAt, author.id);
      const createdMessage = this.insertMessage(author.id, createdTopic.id, body, clientMessageId, requestHash, createdAt);
      return { topic: createdTopic, message: createdMessage };
    });
  }

  postReply(actor: Participant, topicId: string, input: { body: string; clientMessageId: string }): Message {
    const author = this.requireActor(actor);
    const destination = id(topicId, 'Topic ID');
    const body = content(input.body, 'Message body', MAX_BODY_LENGTH);
    const clientMessageId = id(input.clientMessageId, 'Client message ID');
    const requestHash = hash(JSON.stringify({ operation: 'post-reply', topicId: destination, body }));
    return this.transaction(() => {
      const previous = this.existingRequest(author.id, clientMessageId, requestHash);
      if (previous) return previous;
      if (!this.getTopic(destination)) throw new ForumStoreError(404, 'TOPIC_NOT_FOUND', 'Topic does not exist.');
      return this.insertMessage(author.id, destination, body, clientMessageId, requestHash, new Date().toISOString());
    });
  }

  listTopics(): Topic[] {
    return this.db.prepare('SELECT id, title, created_at, created_by FROM topics ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC').all().map(topic);
  }

  getTopic(topicId: string): Topic | null {
    const row = this.db.prepare('SELECT id, title, created_at, created_by FROM topics WHERE id = ?').get(id(topicId, 'Topic ID'));
    return row ? topic(row) : null;
  }

  listMessages(input: { after: number; limit: number; topicId?: string }): MessagePage {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(input.after) || input.after < 0) throw new ForumStoreError(400, 'INVALID_CURSOR', 'Cursor must be a nonnegative safe integer.');
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(input.limit) || input.limit < 1 || input.limit > MAX_PAGE_SIZE) {
      throw new ForumStoreError(400, 'INVALID_LIMIT', `Page size must be between 1 and ${MAX_PAGE_SIZE}.`);
    }
    let rows: Row[];
    if (input.topicId !== undefined) {
      const topicId = id(input.topicId, 'Topic ID');
      if (!this.getTopic(topicId)) throw new ForumStoreError(404, 'TOPIC_NOT_FOUND', 'Topic does not exist.');
      rows = this.db.prepare('SELECT * FROM messages WHERE topic_id = ? AND sequence > ? ORDER BY sequence ASC LIMIT ?')
        .all(topicId, input.after, input.limit + 1);
    } else {
      rows = this.db.prepare('SELECT * FROM messages WHERE sequence > ? ORDER BY sequence ASC LIMIT ?').all(input.after, input.limit + 1);
    }
    const messages = rows.slice(0, input.limit).map(message);
    return { messages, nextCursor: messages.at(-1)?.sequence ?? input.after, hasMore: rows.length > input.limit };
  }

  private requireActor(actor: Participant): Participant {
    if (!actor || typeof actor !== 'object' || typeof actor.id !== 'string' || !VALID_ID.test(actor.id)) {
      throw new ForumStoreError(401, 'UNAUTHENTICATED', 'Authenticate before posting.');
    }
    const row = this.db.prepare('SELECT id, name, role, created_at FROM participants WHERE id = ? AND active = 1').get(actor.id);
    if (!row || row.name !== actor.name || row.role !== actor.role) throw new ForumStoreError(401, 'UNAUTHENTICATED', 'Participant identity is invalid.');
    return participant(row);
  }

  private existingRequest(participantId: string, clientMessageId: string, requestHash: string): Message | null {
    const row = this.db.prepare('SELECT * FROM messages WHERE participant_id = ? AND client_message_id = ?').get(participantId, clientMessageId);
    if (!row) return null;
    if (row.request_hash !== requestHash) throw new ForumStoreError(409, 'IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT', 'This client message ID already belongs to different content.');
    return message(row);
  }

  private insertMessage(participantId: string, topicId: string, body: string, clientMessageId: string, requestHash: string, createdAt: string): Message {
    const messageId = randomUUID();
    this.db.prepare('INSERT INTO messages (id, topic_id, participant_id, body, created_at, client_message_id, request_hash) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)')
      .run(messageId, topicId, participantId, body, createdAt, clientMessageId, requestHash);
    const row = this.db.prepare('SELECT * FROM messages WHERE id = ?').get(messageId);
    if (!row) throw new Error('Message could not be read after insertion.');
    return message(row);
  }

  private transaction<T>(operation: () => T): T {
    this.db.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');
    try {
      const result = operation();
      this.db.exec('COMMIT');
      return result;
    } catch (error) {
      this.db.exec('ROLLBACK');
      throw error;
    }
  }
}
