import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, rmSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { request as httpRequest, type Server } from 'node:http';
import type { AddressInfo } from 'node:net';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ForumStore, type Message, type TopicCreated } from './forum-store';
import { createForumServer, type ForumServerOptions } from './http';

const API = '/api/forum/v1';
let directory: string;
let store: ForumStore;
let server: Server;
let base: string;
let adminToken: string;
let memberToken: string;
let otherToken: string;
let staticDir: string;

async function closeServer() {
  if (!server?.listening) return;
  server.closeAllConnections();
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => server.close(error => error ? reject(error) : resolve()));
}

async function startServer(overrides: Partial<ForumServerOptions> = {}) {
  await closeServer();
  server = createForumServer({ store, staticDir, rateLimit: { requests: 1000, failures: 1000 }, ...overrides });
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    server.once('error', reject);
    server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve);
  });
  base = `http://127.0.0.1:${(server.address() as AddressInfo).port}`;
}

beforeEach(async () => {
  directory = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'omi-forum-http-'));
  staticDir = join(directory, 'public');
  mkdirSync(staticDir);
  writeFileSync(join(staticDir, 'index.html'), '<!doctype html><title>OMI public client</title>');
  writeFileSync(join(staticDir, 'client.js'), 'console.log("public client");');
  store = new ForumStore(join(directory, 'private', 'forum.sqlite'));
  adminToken = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
  memberToken = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
  otherToken = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
  store.createParticipant({ id: 'admin', name: 'Administrator', role: 'admin', token: adminToken });
  store.createParticipant({ id: 'member', name: 'Member', role: 'participant', token: memberToken });
  store.createParticipant({ id: 'other', name: 'Other member', role: 'participant', token: otherToken });
  await startServer();
});

afterEach(async () => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  await closeServer();
  store?.close();
  if (directory) rmSync(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

function call(path: string, token: string | null = memberToken, method = 'GET', value?: unknown) {
  return fetch(`${base}${API}${path}`, {
    method,
    headers: { ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}), ...(value === undefined ? {} : { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }) },
    ...(value === undefined ? {} : { body: JSON.stringify(value) }),
  });
}

async function createTopic(clientMessageId = 'topic-1', token = memberToken): Promise<TopicCreated> {
  const response = await call('/topics', token, 'POST', { title: 'Shared development', body: 'A real stored message.', clientMessageId });
  expect(response.status).toBe(201);
  return response.json() as Promise<TopicCreated>;
}

function rawGet(path: string, headers: string[] = []) {
  return new Promise<{ status: number; body: string }>((resolve, reject) => {
    const target = new URL(base);
    const request = httpRequest({ hostname: target.hostname, port: target.port, path, headers: ['Host', target.host, ...headers] }, response => {
      let body = '';
      response.setEncoding('utf8');
      response.on('data', value => { body += value; });
      response.on('end', () => resolve({ status: response.statusCode!, body }));
    });
    request.on('error', reject);
    request.end();
  });
}

describe('authenticated shared forum HTTP boundary', () => {
  it('keeps health public and requires bearer authentication for every forum resource', async () => {
    const health = await fetch(`${base}/health`);
    expect(health.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await health.json()).toMatchObject({ ok: true, service: 'omi-forum' });
    for (const path of ['/identity', '/participants', '/topics', '/messages', '/not-a-route']) {
      expect((await call(path, null)).status).toBe(401);
    }
    const identity = await call('/identity');
    expect(identity.status).toBe(200);
    expect(identity.headers.get('cache-control')).toBe('no-store');
    expect(identity.headers.get('x-content-type-options')).toBe('nosniff');
    expect(await identity.json()).toMatchObject({ participant: { id: 'member', role: 'participant' } });
  });

  it('rejects query/cookie credentials, invalid tokens, and ambiguous Authorization headers', async () => {
    expect((await call(`/identity?token=${memberToken}`, null)).status).toBe(400);
    const cookie = await fetch(`${base}${API}/identity`, { headers: { Cookie: `omiToken=${memberToken}` } });
    expect(cookie.status).toBe(401);
    expect((await call('/identity', randomBytes(32).toString('base64url'))).status).toBe(401);
    const duplicate = await rawGet(`${API}/identity`, ['Authorization', `Bearer ${memberToken}`, 'Authorization', `Bearer ${otherToken}`]);
    expect(duplicate.status).toBe(401);
    expect(duplicate.body).not.toContain(memberToken);
    expect(duplicate.body).not.toContain(otherToken);
  });

  it('enforces administrator identity provisioning and never returns stored credentials', async () => {
    const input = { id: 'new-worker', name: 'New worker', role: 'participant' };
    expect((await call('/participants', memberToken, 'POST', input)).status).toBe(403);
    const created = await call('/participants', adminToken, 'POST', input);
    expect(created.status).toBe(201);
    expect(created.headers.get('cache-control')).toBe('no-store');
    const result = await created.json() as { participant: { id: string }; token: string };
    expect(result.participant.id).toBe(input.id);
    expect(result.token).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}$/);
    expect((await call('/identity', result.token)).status).toBe(200);
    const listing = await call('/participants', adminToken);
    const text = await listing.text();
    for (const token of [result.token, adminToken, memberToken, otherToken]) expect(text).not.toContain(token);
    expect(text).not.toContain('token_hash');
  });

  it('attributes messages from authentication and refuses forged identity fields', async () => {
    const created = await createTopic();
    expect(created.message.participantId).toBe('member');
    const forged = await call(`/topics/${created.topic.id}/messages`, memberToken, 'POST', { body: 'Forged author', clientMessageId: 'forged', participantId: 'admin' });
    expect(forged.status).toBe(400);
    const response = await call(`/topics/${created.topic.id}/messages`, otherToken, 'POST', { body: 'Independent author', clientMessageId: 'reply' });
    expect(response.status).toBe(201);
    const reply = await response.json() as { message: Message };
    expect(reply.message.participantId).toBe('other');
    expect(reply.message.id).not.toBe(created.message.id);
  });

  it('preserves author-scoped idempotency and rejects changed retry payloads', async () => {
    const first = await createTopic('same-client-id');
    expect(await createTopic('same-client-id')).toEqual(first);
    expect((await call('/topics', memberToken, 'POST', { title: 'Changed', body: first.message.body, clientMessageId: 'same-client-id' })).status).toBe(409);
    const secondAuthor = await createTopic('same-client-id', otherToken);
    expect(secondAuthor.message.id).not.toBe(first.message.id);
    expect((await call(`/topics/${first.topic.id}/messages`, memberToken, 'POST', { body: first.message.body, clientMessageId: 'same-client-id' })).status).toBe(409);
    expect(store.listTopics()).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit: 100 }).messages).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('provides global monotone cursor pages without gaps or duplicates', async () => {
    const first = await createTopic('first-topic');
    await createTopic('second-topic');
    for (let index = 0; index < 4; index++) {
      expect((await call(`/topics/${first.topic.id}/messages`, otherToken, 'POST', { body: `Reply ${index}`, clientMessageId: `reply-${index}` })).status).toBe(201);
    }
    const collected: Message[] = [];
    let cursor = 0;
    while (true) {
      const response = await call(`/messages?after=${cursor}&limit=2`);
      expect(response.status).toBe(200);
      const page = await response.json() as { messages: Message[]; nextCursor: number; hasMore: boolean };
      collected.push(...page.messages);
      expect(page.nextCursor).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(cursor);
      cursor = page.nextCursor;
      if (!page.hasMore) break;
    }
    expect(collected.map(message => message.sequence)).toEqual([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]);
    expect(new Set(collected.map(message => message.id)).size).toBe(6);
    const empty = await call(`/messages?after=${cursor}`);
    expect(await empty.json()).toEqual({ messages: [], nextCursor: cursor, hasMore: false });
    for (const query of ['after=-1', 'after=1.5', 'after=9007199254740992', 'limit=0', 'limit=201', 'after=0&after=2', 'callback=steal']) {
      expect((await call(`/messages?${query}`)).status).toBe(400);
    }
  });

  it('rejects cross-origin browser requests and provides no wildcard CORS policy', async () => {
    const denied = await fetch(`${base}${API}/identity`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${memberToken}`, Origin: 'https://unrelated.example' } });
    expect(denied.status).toBe(403);
    expect(denied.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin')).toBeNull();
    const sameOrigin = await fetch(`${base}${API}/identity`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${memberToken}`, Origin: base } });
    expect(sameOrigin.status).toBe(200);
    const preflight = await fetch(`${base}${API}/topics`, { method: 'OPTIONS', headers: { Origin: 'https://unrelated.example', 'Access-Control-Request-Method': 'POST', 'Access-Control-Request-Headers': 'authorization,content-type' } });
    expect(preflight.status).toBe(403);
    expect(preflight.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('bounds body parsing and accepts only valid UTF-8 JSON objects', async () => {
    const url = `${base}${API}/topics`;
    const authorization = { Authorization: `Bearer ${memberToken}` };
    const cases: [string, string, number][] = [
      ['text/plain', '{}', 415],
      ['application/json', '{"title":', 400],
      ['application/json', '[]', 400],
      ['application/json', 'null', 400],
      ['application/json', JSON.stringify({ title: 'Large', body: 'x'.repeat(70_000), clientMessageId: 'too-large' }), 413],
    ];
    for (const [type, body, status] of cases) {
      const response = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { ...authorization, 'Content-Type': type }, body });
      expect(response.status).toBe(status);
    }
    const compressed = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { ...authorization, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Content-Encoding': 'gzip' }, body: '{}' });
    expect(compressed.status).toBe(415);
    const invalidUtf8 = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { ...authorization, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: new Uint8Array([0xff]) });
    expect(invalidUtf8.status).toBe(400);
    expect(store.listTopics()).toHaveLength(0);
    expect((await fetch(`${base}/health`)).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it('rate limits failed authentication using the socket address rather than spoofable forwarded headers', async () => {
    await startServer({ rateLimit: { requests: 100, failures: 2, windowMs: 60_000 } });
    const request = (forwarded: string) => fetch(`${base}${API}/identity`, { headers: { 'X-Forwarded-For': forwarded } });
    expect((await request('192.0.2.1')).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await request('192.0.2.2')).status).toBe(401);
    const limited = await request('192.0.2.3');
    expect(limited.status).toBe(429);
    expect(Number(limited.headers.get('retry-after'))).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect((await fetch(`${base}/health`)).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it('applies revocation immediately while retaining prior authorship', async () => {
    const created = await createTopic();
    expect((await call('/participants/member/revoke', otherToken, 'POST', {})).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await call('/participants/member/revoke', adminToken, 'POST', {})).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await call('/identity', memberToken)).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await call(`/topics/${created.topic.id}/messages`, memberToken, 'POST', { body: 'Stale credentials', clientMessageId: 'after-revoke' })).status).toBe(401);
    const identities = await call('/participants', otherToken);
    expect(await identities.json()).toMatchObject({ participants: expect.arrayContaining([expect.objectContaining({ id: 'member', name: 'Member' })]) });
    expect(store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit: 100 }).messages[0].participantId).toBe('member');
  });

  it('requires an administrator to rotate credentials and invalidates the old token immediately', async () => {
    const created = await createTopic();
    expect((await call('/participants/member/rotate', otherToken, 'POST', {})).status).toBe(403);
    const response = await call('/participants/member/rotate', adminToken, 'POST', {});
    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
    const value = await response.json() as { participant: { id: string }; token: string };
    expect(value.participant.id).toBe('member');
    expect(value.token).not.toBe(memberToken);
    expect((await call('/identity', memberToken)).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await call('/identity', value.token)).status).toBe(200);
    expect(store.listMessages({ after: 0, limit: 100 }).messages[0].topicId).toBe(created.topic.id);
    expect((await call('/participants/member/revoke', adminToken, 'POST', {})).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await call('/participants/member/rotate', adminToken, 'POST', {})).status).toBe(409);
  });

  it('rechecks administrator authorization after a slow body before issuing a credential', async () => {
    let authenticated!: () => void;
    const observed = new Promise<void>(resolve => { authenticated = resolve; });
    const original = store.authenticate.bind(store);
    vi.spyOn(store, 'authenticate').mockImplementation(token => {
      const actor = original(token);
      if (token === adminToken) authenticated();
      return actor;
    });
    const value = JSON.stringify({ id: 'must-not-exist', name: 'Unapproved worker', role: 'admin' });
    const target = new URL(base);
    let request!: ReturnType<typeof httpRequest>;
    const result = new Promise<number>((resolve, reject) => {
      request = httpRequest({ hostname: target.hostname, port: target.port, path: `${API}/participants`, method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${adminToken}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Content-Length': Buffer.byteLength(value) } }, response => {
        response.resume();
        response.on('end', () => resolve(response.statusCode!));
      });
      request.on('error', reject);
      request.write(value.slice(0, 1));
    });
    await observed;
    store.revokeParticipant('admin');
    request.end(value.slice(1));
    expect(await result).toBe(401);
    expect(store.listParticipants().some(actor => actor.id === 'must-not-exist')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('serves only compiled public assets and refuses traversal, hidden files, and escaping symlinks', async () => {
    const secret = 'PRIVATE-TEST-MARKER-NOT-A-CREDENTIAL';
    const privateFile = join(directory, 'private', 'private.txt');
    writeFileSync(privateFile, secret);
    writeFileSync(join(staticDir, '.hidden'), secret);
    symlinkSync(privateFile, join(staticDir, 'escape.txt'));
    const client = await fetch(`${base}/client.js`);
    expect(client.status).toBe(200);
    expect(client.headers.get('content-type')).toContain('javascript');
    const head = await fetch(`${base}/client.js`, { method: 'HEAD' });
    expect(head.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await head.text()).toBe('');
    for (const path of ['/.hidden', '/%2e%2e%2fprivate%2fprivate.txt', '/%2e%2e%5cprivate%5cprivate.txt', '/escape.txt', '/.omi-dev/cartridge-signing-key.pem']) {
      const response = await rawGet(path);
      expect(response.status).toBe(404);
      expect(response.body).not.toContain(secret);
    }
    const shell = await fetch(`${base}/`);
    expect(shell.headers.get('content-security-policy')).toContain("frame-ancestors 'none'");
  });
});

import { createServer, type IncomingMessage, type ServerResponse } from 'node:http';
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { readFile, realpath, stat } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { extname, resolve, sep } from 'node:path';
import { ForumStore, ForumStoreError, type Participant } from './forum-store.ts';

export type ForumServerOptions = {
  store: ForumStore;
  staticDir?: string;
  publicOrigin?: string;
  rateLimit?: { requests?: number; failures?: number; windowMs?: number };
};
const API = '/api/forum/v1';
const MAX_BODY = 65_536;
const mime: Record<string, string> = { '.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', '.js': 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8', '.css': 'text/css; charset=utf-8', '.json': 'application/json; charset=utf-8', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.png': 'image/png', '.ico': 'image/x-icon', '.woff2': 'font/woff2' };

function json(response: ServerResponse, status: number, data: unknown) {
  response.writeHead(status, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
  response.end(JSON.stringify(data));
}
function fault(status: number, code: string, message: string): never { throw new ForumStoreError(status, code, message); }
async function body(request: IncomingMessage): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  if (request.headers['content-type']?.split(';')[0].trim().toLowerCase() !== 'application/json') fault(415, 'JSON_REQUIRED', 'Send application/json.');
  if (request.headers['content-encoding'] && request.headers['content-encoding'] !== 'identity') fault(415, 'ENCODING_UNSUPPORTED', 'Compressed request bodies are not accepted.');
  const declared = Number(request.headers['content-length'] ?? 0);
  if (!Number.isFinite(declared) || declared > MAX_BODY) fault(413, 'BODY_TOO_LARGE', 'Request body is too large.');
  const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
  let size = 0;
  for await (const chunk of request) {
    const bytes = Buffer.isBuffer(chunk) ? chunk : Buffer.from(chunk);
    size += bytes.length;
    if (size > MAX_BODY) fault(413, 'BODY_TOO_LARGE', 'Request body is too large.');
    chunks.push(bytes);
  }
  let value: unknown;
  try { value = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(Buffer.concat(chunks))); }
  catch { fault(400, 'INVALID_JSON', 'Request body must be valid UTF-8 JSON.'); }
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) fault(400, 'INVALID_JSON', 'Request body must be an object.');
  return value as Record<string, unknown>;
}
function fields(value: Record<string, unknown>, names: string[]) {
  if (Object.keys(value).some(key => !names.includes(key))) fault(400, 'UNKNOWN_FIELD', 'Request includes an unsupported field.');
}
function integer(value: string | null, fallback: number, label: string): number {
  if (value === null) return fallback;
  if (!/^\d{1,16}$/.test(value) || !Number.isSafeInteger(Number(value))) fault(400, 'INVALID_NUMBER', `${label} must be a nonnegative safe integer.`);
  return Number(value);
}

/** Authenticated, same-origin forum API and public compiled client. Never executes message content. */
export function createForumServer(options: ForumServerOptions) {
  const { store } = options;
  const staticRoot = options.staticDir ? resolve(options.staticDir) : null;
  const origin = options.publicOrigin ? new URL(options.publicOrigin).origin : null;
  const requestsPerWindow = options.rateLimit?.requests ?? 300;
  const failuresPerWindow = options.rateLimit?.failures ?? 20;
  const windowMs = options.rateLimit?.windowMs ?? 60_000;
  const clients = new Map<string, { start: number; requests: number; failures: number }>();
  function bucket(request: IncomingMessage) {
    const address = request.socket.remoteAddress ?? 'unknown';
    const now = Date.now();
    let entry = clients.get(address);
    if (!entry || now - entry.start >= windowMs) {
      if (clients.size >= 4096) {
        for (const [key, value] of clients) if (now - value.start >= windowMs) clients.delete(key);
        if (clients.size >= 4096) clients.delete(clients.keys().next().value!);
      }
      entry = { start: now, requests: 0, failures: 0 };
      clients.set(address, entry);
    }
    entry.requests++;
    return entry;
  }
  function authenticate(request: IncomingMessage, entry: ReturnType<typeof bucket>): Participant {
    const authorizationCount = request.rawHeaders.filter((_, index) => index % 2 === 0 && request.rawHeaders[index].toLowerCase() === 'authorization').length;
    const match = authorizationCount === 1 && /^Bearer ([A-Za-z0-9._~-]{24,512})$/i.exec(request.headers.authorization ?? '');
    const actor = match ? store.authenticate(match[1]) : null;
    if (!actor) { entry.failures++; fault(401, 'UNAUTHENTICATED', 'A valid participant bearer token is required.'); }
    return actor;
  }
  return createServer({ maxHeaderSize: 16_384, requestTimeout: 15_000, headersTimeout: 10_000, keepAliveTimeout: 5_000 }, async (request, response) => {
    response.setHeader('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
    response.setHeader('Referrer-Policy', 'no-referrer');
    response.setHeader('Permissions-Policy', 'camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=()');
    response.setHeader('Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy', 'same-origin');
    if (origin?.startsWith('https://')) response.setHeader('Strict-Transport-Security', 'max-age=31536000');
    try {
      const url = new URL(request.url ?? '/', 'http://omi.invalid');
      const method = request.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.pathname === '/health' && method === 'GET') { json(response, 200, { ok: true, service: 'omi-forum', version: '1.0.0' }); return; }
      if (url.pathname === API || url.pathname.startsWith(`${API}/`)) {
        const entry = bucket(request);
        if (entry.requests > requestsPerWindow || entry.failures >= failuresPerWindow) {
          response.setHeader('Retry-After', String(Math.ceil(windowMs / 1000)));
          fault(429, 'RATE_LIMITED', 'Too many requests. Retry later.');
        }
        if (request.headers.origin) {
          let same = false;
          try {
            const supplied = new URL(request.headers.origin);
            same = supplied.origin !== 'null' && (origin ? supplied.origin === origin : ['http:', 'https:'].includes(supplied.protocol) && supplied.host === request.headers.host);
          } catch {}
          if (!same) fault(403, 'ORIGIN_DENIED', 'This API accepts same-origin browser requests only.');
        }
        if (method === 'OPTIONS') fault(403, 'CROSS_ORIGIN_UNSUPPORTED', 'Cross-origin browser access is not enabled.');
        if ([...url.searchParams.keys()].some(key => /token|key|authorization/i.test(key))) fault(400, 'QUERY_CREDENTIALS_DENIED', 'Credentials must use the Authorization header.');
        const actor = authenticate(request, entry);
        if (url.pathname === `${API}/identity` && method === 'GET') { json(response, 200, { participant: actor }); return; }
        if (url.pathname === `${API}/participants` && method === 'GET') { json(response, 200, { participants: store.listParticipants() }); return; }
        if (url.pathname === `${API}/participants` && method === 'POST') {
          if (actor.role !== 'admin') fault(403, 'ADMIN_REQUIRED', 'An administrator must create participant identities.');
          const input = await body(request); fields(input, ['id', 'name', 'role']);
          if (authenticate(request, entry).role !== 'admin') fault(403, 'ADMIN_REQUIRED', 'An active administrator is required.');
          const token = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
          const participant = store.createParticipant({ id: input.id as string, name: input.name as string, role: (input.role ?? 'participant') as 'admin' | 'participant', token });
          json(response, 201, { participant, token }); return;
        }
        const revoke = url.pathname.match(new RegExp(`^${API}/participants/([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)/revoke$`));
        const rotate = url.pathname.match(new RegExp(`^${API}/participants/([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)/rotate$`));
        if (rotate && method === 'POST') {
          if (actor.role !== 'admin') fault(403, 'ADMIN_REQUIRED', 'An administrator must rotate participant credentials.');
          const input = await body(request); fields(input, []);
          if (authenticate(request, entry).role !== 'admin') fault(403, 'ADMIN_REQUIRED', 'An active administrator is required.');
          const token = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
          const participant = store.rotateParticipantToken(rotate[1], token);
          json(response, 200, { participant, token }); return;
        }
        if (revoke && method === 'POST') {
          if (actor.role !== 'admin') fault(403, 'ADMIN_REQUIRED', 'An administrator must revoke a participant.');
          if (revoke[1] === actor.id) fault(409, 'SELF_REVOCATION_DENIED', 'Use another administrator to revoke this administrator.');
          const input = await body(request); fields(input, []);
          if (authenticate(request, entry).role !== 'admin') fault(403, 'ADMIN_REQUIRED', 'An active administrator is required.');
          store.revokeParticipant(revoke[1]); json(response, 200, { revoked: revoke[1] }); return;
        }
        if (url.pathname === `${API}/topics` && method === 'GET') { json(response, 200, { topics: store.listTopics() }); return; }
        if (url.pathname === `${API}/topics` && method === 'POST') {
          const input = await body(request); fields(input, ['title', 'body', 'clientMessageId']);
          authenticate(request, entry);
          json(response, 201, store.createTopic(actor, input as { title: string; body: string; clientMessageId: string })); return;
        }
        const reply = url.pathname.match(new RegExp(`^${API}/topics/([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)/messages$`));
        if (reply && method === 'POST') {
          const input = await body(request); fields(input, ['body', 'clientMessageId']);
          authenticate(request, entry);
          json(response, 201, { message: store.postReply(actor, reply[1], input as { body: string; clientMessageId: string }) }); return;
        }
        if (url.pathname === `${API}/messages` && method === 'GET') {
          const allowed = new Set(['after', 'limit', 'topicId']);
          if ([...url.searchParams.keys()].some(key => !allowed.has(key) || url.searchParams.getAll(key).length > 1)) fault(400, 'INVALID_QUERY', 'Unsupported or repeated message query.');
          json(response, 200, store.listMessages({ after: integer(url.searchParams.get('after'), 0, 'Cursor'), limit: integer(url.searchParams.get('limit'), 100, 'Limit'), ...(url.searchParams.has('topicId') ? { topicId: url.searchParams.get('topicId')! } : {}) })); return;
        }
        fault(404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Forum endpoint does not exist.');
      }
      if (method !== 'GET' && method !== 'HEAD') fault(405, 'METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED', 'Only GET and HEAD can read the client.');
      if (!staticRoot) fault(404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Client assets are not configured.');
      let decoded: string;
      try { decoded = decodeURIComponent(url.pathname); } catch { fault(400, 'INVALID_PATH', 'Invalid path encoding.'); }
      if (decoded.includes('\0') || decoded.includes('\\') || decoded.split('/').some(part => part.startsWith('.'))) fault(404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'File not found.');
      let file = resolve(staticRoot, `.${decoded}`);
      if (file !== staticRoot && !file.startsWith(staticRoot + sep)) fault(404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'File not found.');
      try { if (!(await stat(file)).isFile()) file = resolve(staticRoot, 'index.html'); }
      catch { if (extname(decoded)) fault(404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'File not found.'); file = resolve(staticRoot, 'index.html'); }
      const canonicalRoot = await realpath(staticRoot);
      const canonicalFile = await realpath(file);
      if (!canonicalFile.startsWith(canonicalRoot + sep)) fault(404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'File not found.');
      const contents = await readFile(canonicalFile);
      response.setHeader('Content-Security-Policy', "frame-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'; form-action 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'");
      response.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': mime[extname(file)] ?? 'application/octet-stream', 'Cache-Control': extname(file) === '.html' || file.endsWith('.bundle.json') ? 'no-store' : 'public, max-age=3600' });
      response.end(method === 'HEAD' ? undefined : contents);
    } catch (error) {
      if (response.headersSent) { response.end(); return; }
      if (error instanceof ForumStoreError) json(response, error.status, { error: { code: error.code, message: error.message } });
      else json(response, 500, { error: { code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR', message: 'The request could not be completed.' } });
    }
  });
}

import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, chmodSync, realpathSync, lstatSync, openSync, fsyncSync, closeSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, resolve, sep } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { ForumStore } from './forum-store.ts';
import { createForumServer } from './http.ts';

process.umask(0o077);
const projectRoot = realpathSync(resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..'));
const requestedDir = resolve(process.env.OMI_FORUM_DATA_DIR || resolve(projectRoot, '../omi-forum-data'));
const projectAncestors = new Set<string>();
for (let path = projectRoot; ; path = dirname(path)) {
  projectAncestors.add(path);
  if (dirname(path) === path) break;
}
const invalidDataDirectory = (path: string) => projectAncestors.has(path) || path.startsWith(projectRoot + sep);
if (invalidDataDirectory(requestedDir)) throw new Error('Use a dedicated private forum directory outside the checkout and its ancestors.');
mkdirSync(requestedDir, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
const dataDir = realpathSync(requestedDir);
if (invalidDataDirectory(dataDir)) throw new Error('Use a dedicated private forum directory outside the checkout and its ancestors.');
chmodSync(dataDir, 0o700);
const store = new ForumStore(resolve(dataDir, 'forum.sqlite'));
const bootstrapPath = resolve(dataDir, 'bootstrap-admin.json');
if (!store.listParticipants().length) {
  let credentials;
  if (existsSync(bootstrapPath)) {
    if (lstatSync(bootstrapPath).isSymbolicLink() || !lstatSync(bootstrapPath).isFile()) throw new Error('Administrator credentials must be a regular private file.');
    credentials = JSON.parse(readFileSync(bootstrapPath, 'utf8'));
    if (credentials?.participant?.id !== 'dot' || credentials?.participant?.role !== 'admin' || typeof credentials?.token !== 'string') throw new Error('Private bootstrap credentials are damaged.');
  } else {
    credentials = { participant: { id: 'dot', name: 'Dot', role: 'admin' as const }, token: randomBytes(32).toString('base64url') };
    const descriptor = openSync(bootstrapPath, 'wx', 0o600);
    try { writeFileSync(descriptor, JSON.stringify(credentials, null, 2) + '\n'); fsyncSync(descriptor); }
    finally { closeSync(descriptor); }
    const directory = openSync(dataDir, 'r');
    try { fsyncSync(directory); } finally { closeSync(directory); }
  }
  store.createParticipant({ ...credentials.participant, token: credentials.token });
}
if (process.argv.includes('--prepare')) {
  store.close();
  console.log(`Forum database and private administrator credentials are prepared in ${dataDir}.`);
  process.exit(0);
}
const port = Number(process.env.OMI_PORT || 8080);
if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) throw new Error('OMI_PORT must be between 1 and 65535.');
const publicOrigin = process.env.OMI_PUBLIC_ORIGIN;
if (publicOrigin) {
  const parsed = new URL(publicOrigin);
  if (parsed.protocol !== 'https:' || parsed.origin !== publicOrigin) throw new Error('OMI_PUBLIC_ORIGIN must be an HTTPS origin with no trailing slash or path.');
}
if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && !publicOrigin) throw new Error('Production requires OMI_PUBLIC_ORIGIN and a configured HTTPS reverse proxy.');
const server = createForumServer({ store, staticDir: resolve(projectRoot, 'dist'), publicOrigin });
server.listen(port, process.env.OMI_BIND || '127.0.0.1', () => {
  console.log(`OMI client and authenticated forum are listening on port ${port}.`);
  console.log(`Private administrator credentials: ${bootstrapPath} (contents withheld).`);
  if (!publicOrigin) console.log('Local HTTP mode. A public HTTPS deployment has not been configured.');
});
let closing = false;
function close() {
  if (closing) return;
  closing = true;
  server.close(() => { store.close(); process.exit(0); });
  setTimeout(() => { server.closeAllConnections(); }, 3000).unref();
}
process.on('SIGINT', close);
process.on('SIGTERM', close);
server.on('error', error => { console.error(error.message); store.close(); process.exitCode = 1; });

import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { closeSync, existsSync, fsyncSync, lstatSync, mkdirSync, openSync, realpathSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { basename, dirname, resolve, sep } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { ForumStore } from './forum-store.ts';

process.umask(0o077);
const root = realpathSync(resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..'));
const args = process.argv.slice(2);
function option(name: string): string | undefined {
  const position = args.indexOf(`--${name}`);
  return position >= 0 ? args[position + 1] : undefined;
}
const participantId = option('id');
if (!participantId) throw new Error('Supply --id participant-id, --name and --output; --id, --rotate and --output; or --id and --revoke.');
if (args.includes('--revoke') && args.includes('--rotate')) throw new Error('Choose revocation or rotation, not both.');
const dataDir = realpathSync(resolve(process.env.OMI_FORUM_DATA_DIR || resolve(root, '../omi-forum-data')));
if (dataDir === root || dataDir.startsWith(root + sep)) throw new Error('Keep forum data outside the checkout.');
const database = resolve(dataDir, 'forum.sqlite');
if (!existsSync(database)) throw new Error('Start the forum once to create its administrator and database.');
const store = new ForumStore(database);
let retainedCredential: string | undefined;
try {
  if (args.includes('--revoke')) {
    store.revokeParticipant(participantId);
    console.log(`Participant ${participantId} has been revoked. Historical messages were preserved.`);
  } else {
    const rotating = args.includes('--rotate');
    const existing = store.listParticipants().find(participant => participant.id === participantId);
    if (rotating && !existing) throw new Error('Participant does not exist.');
    if (!rotating && existing) throw new Error('Participant already exists; use --rotate with a new output file to replace its token.');
    const name = rotating ? existing!.name : option('name'); const output = option('output');
    if (!name || !output) throw new Error('Supply --name and --output for creation, or --rotate and --output for rotation.');
    const destination = resolve(output);
    if (existsSync(destination)) throw new Error('The output file already exists; refusing to replace credentials.');
    mkdirSync(dirname(destination), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
    const parent = realpathSync(dirname(destination));
    if (parent === root || parent.startsWith(root + sep)) throw new Error('Write credentials outside the checkout and public assets.');
    const filename = resolve(parent, basename(destination));
    if (existsSync(filename)) throw new Error('The output file already exists; refusing to replace credentials.');
    const parentMetadata = lstatSync(parent);
    if (!parentMetadata.isDirectory() || (process.getuid && (parentMetadata.uid !== process.getuid() || (parentMetadata.mode & 0o077) !== 0))) {
      throw new Error('The credential output parent must be an owner-only private directory.');
    }
    const token = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
    const identity = existing ?? { id: participantId, name, role: args.includes('--admin') ? 'admin' as const : 'participant' as const };
    // Keep the only raw key durably before changing the database's accepted hash.
    // If registration later fails, retain this private candidate for diagnosis;
    // never undo a successfully rotated hash by losing its only credential file.
    const descriptor = openSync(filename, 'wx', 0o600);
    try {
      writeFileSync(descriptor, JSON.stringify({ participant: identity, token }, null, 2) + '\n');
      fsyncSync(descriptor);
    } finally { closeSync(descriptor); }
    retainedCredential = filename;
    const directory = openSync(parent, 'r');
    try { fsyncSync(directory); } finally { closeSync(directory); }
    const participant = rotating
      ? store.rotateParticipantToken(participantId, token)
      : store.createParticipant({ id: participantId, name, role: identity.role, token });
    console.log(`Participant ${participant.id} ${rotating ? 'token rotated' : 'created'}. Credentials saved privately to ${filename}; token withheld.`);
  }
} catch (error) {
  console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Participant operation failed.');
  if (retainedCredential) console.error(`Private candidate credentials were retained at ${retainedCredential}; token withheld. Check the operation result before using them.`);
  process.exitCode = 1;
} finally { store.close(); }

===== BEGIN FILE source-review/src/core/capsule.test.ts | SHA256 b6d1a2b190385924d6eecea16ee99d760c8c431155304cff201f37f9d57285ab | BYTES 5635 =====
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createVault, hasVault, removeVault, saveVault, unlockVault, VAULT_STORAGE_KEY } from './capsule';

class MemoryStorage implements Storage {
  private values = new Map<string, string>();
  get length(): number { return this.values.size; }
  clear(): void { this.values.clear(); }
  getItem(key: string): string | null { return this.values.get(key) ?? null; }
  key(index: number): string | null { return [...this.values.keys()][index] ?? null; }
  removeItem(key: string): void { this.values.delete(key); }
  setItem(key: string, value: string): void { this.values.set(key, String(value)); }
}

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.stubGlobal('localStorage', new MemoryStorage());
});

const PIN = '70439182';

describe('encrypted Personal Capsule vault', () => {
  it('creates a private capsule, saves app data, and decrypts it after locking', async () => {
    expect(hasVault()).toBe(false);
    const { data, key } = await createVault('River', PIN);
    expect(hasVault()).toBe(true);
    expect(data.capsule.sharing.enabled).toBe(false);
    expect(data.capsule.identity.raw_biometrics_retained).toBe(false);
    expect(Object.values(data.capsule.privacy).every((privacy) => privacy.mode === 'private')).toBe(true);
    data.appData['eagles-nest'] = { notes: ['Pick up groceries'] };
    await saveVault(data, key);
    const unlocked = await unlockVault(PIN);
    expect(unlocked.data).toEqual(data);
    expect(key.extractable).toBe(false);
    expect(unlocked.key.extractable).toBe(false);
  });

  it('stores no plaintext capsule, name, app content, or PIN and uses a fresh IV for every save', async () => {
    const { data, key } = await createVault('Sensitive family name', PIN);
    data.appData.family = { secret: 'Private pickup location' };
    await saveVault(data, key);
    const before = localStorage.getItem(VAULT_STORAGE_KEY)!;
    for (const secret of [PIN, data.displayName, data.capsule.owner_id, 'Private pickup location', 'appData', 'capsule_id']) {
      expect(before).not.toContain(secret);
    }
    await saveVault(data, key);
    const after = localStorage.getItem(VAULT_STORAGE_KEY)!;
    expect(JSON.parse(after).iv).not.toEqual(JSON.parse(before).iv);
    expect(JSON.parse(after).ciphertext).not.toEqual(JSON.parse(before).ciphertext);
  });

  it('rejects wrong PINs and modified ciphertext without revealing plaintext', async () => {
    await createVault('River', PIN);
    await expect(unlockVault('00000000')).rejects.toThrow('Could not unlock the capsule');
    const envelope = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(VAULT_STORAGE_KEY)!);
    envelope.ciphertext = (envelope.ciphertext[0] === 'A' ? 'B' : 'A') + envelope.ciphertext.slice(1);
    localStorage.setItem(VAULT_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(envelope));
    await expect(unlockVault(PIN)).rejects.toThrow('Could not unlock the capsule');
  });

  it('requires fresh sharing authorization whenever the capsule is unlocked', async () => {
    const { data, key } = await createVault('River', PIN);
    data.capsule.sharing = {
      enabled: true, max_idle_share_percent: 10, load_balance: true,
      share_window: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000).toISOString(), auto_manage: true, turbo_manual: true,
    };
    await saveVault(data, key);
    const unlocked = await unlockVault(PIN);
    expect(unlocked.data.capsule.sharing).toEqual({
      enabled: false, max_idle_share_percent: 10, load_balance: false,
      share_window: null, auto_manage: false, turbo_manual: false,
    });
  });

  it('refuses invalid PINs, invalid capsule schemas, and accidental replacement', async () => {
    await expect(createVault('River', '1234')).rejects.toThrow('8 to 64 digits');
    await expect(createVault('River', 'abcdefgh')).rejects.toThrow('8 to 64 digits');
    await expect(createVault(' ', PIN)).rejects.toThrow('Enter a name');
    const { data, key } = await createVault('River', PIN);
    await expect(createVault('Other person', PIN)).rejects.toThrow('already exists');
    data.capsule.sharing.max_idle_share_percent = 16;
    await expect(saveVault(data, key)).rejects.toThrow('schema');
  });

  it('reports corrupt envelopes without overwriting them', async () => {
    localStorage.setItem(VAULT_STORAGE_KEY, '{broken');
    await expect(unlockVault(PIN)).rejects.toThrow('damaged');
    await expect(createVault('River', PIN)).rejects.toThrow('already exists');
    expect(localStorage.getItem(VAULT_STORAGE_KEY)).toBe('{broken');
  });

  it('rejects forged key-derivation settings before attempting to unlock', async () => {
    await createVault('River', PIN);
    const envelope = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(VAULT_STORAGE_KEY)!);
    envelope.iterations = 1;
    localStorage.setItem(VAULT_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(envelope));
    await expect(unlockVault(PIN)).rejects.toThrow('unsupported or damaged');
  });

  it('does not recreate a deleted capsule from a stale unlocked session', async () => {
    const { data, key } = await createVault('River', PIN);
    removeVault();
    expect(hasVault()).toBe(false);
    await expect(saveVault(data, key)).rejects.toThrow('missing or damaged');
    expect(hasVault()).toBe(false);
  });

  it('serializes overlapping saves so the latest requested edit persists', async () => {
    const { data, key } = await createVault('River', PIN);
    data.displayName = 'First edit';
    const first = saveVault(data, key);
    data.displayName = 'Latest edit';
    const second = saveVault(data, key);
    await Promise.all([first, second]);
    expect((await unlockVault(PIN)).data.displayName).toBe('Latest edit');
  });
});

===== END FILE source-review/src/core/capsule.test.ts =====

===== BEGIN FILE source-review/src/core/capsule.ts | SHA256 b7104cd53ff0053256ac27dd73c2a1b18e86f394a535ccf429d58c8beeccf811 | BYTES 10102 =====
import Ajv2020 from 'ajv/dist/2020';
import addFormats from 'ajv-formats';
import capsuleSchema from '../../omi-client/capsules/schema.json';

export type Audience = { mode: 'private' | 'selected' | 'family'; allowed_ids?: string[]; expires_at?: string | null };
export type PrivacyArea = 'location' | 'presence' | 'timeline' | 'media' | 'messages' | 'travel';
export type Capsule = {
  capsule_id: string;
  owner_id: string;
  version: string;
  identity: {
    auth_methods: Array<'passkey' | 'pin' | 'voice_template' | 'camera_template' | 'reliance_watch'>;
    raw_biometrics_retained: false;
    voice_template_ref?: string | null;
    camera_template_ref?: string | null;
    preferred_language?: string;
    accessibility_profile_ref?: string | null;
  };
  privacy: Record<PrivacyArea, Audience>;
  services: Array<{ app_id: string; enabled: boolean; profile?: string | null }>;
  travel?: {
    travel_partner_ids?: string[];
    trip_only_location_share?: boolean;
    arrival_flags?: boolean;
    regional_emergency_profile?: boolean;
    translation_enabled?: boolean;
  };
  emergency: {
    enabled: boolean;
    contacts: string[];
    location_share_on_sos: boolean;
    medical_summary_ref?: string | null;
    allow_regional_emergency_relay?: boolean;
  };
  sharing: {
    enabled: boolean;
    max_idle_share_percent: number;
    load_balance: boolean;
    share_window?: string | null;
    auto_manage?: boolean;
    turbo_manual?: boolean;
    resource_types?: Array<'cpu' | 'vgpu' | 'storage' | 'bandwidth'>;
  };
};

export type VaultData = { capsule: Capsule; displayName: string; appData: Record<string, unknown> };
export const VAULT_STORAGE_KEY = 'omi.personal-capsule.v1';
export const PIN_MIN_LENGTH = 8;
const ITERATIONS = 310_000;
const MAX_PLAINTEXT_BYTES = 1_000_000;
const encoder = new TextEncoder();
const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true });
const authenticatedContext = encoder.encode('OMI Personal Capsule Vault v1');
const keySalts = new WeakMap<CryptoKey, Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>>();
let saveQueue = Promise.resolve();
const ajv = new Ajv2020({ allErrors: true, strict: false });
addFormats(ajv);
const validateCapsule = ajv.compile<Capsule>(capsuleSchema);

type Envelope = {
  version: 1;
  kdf: 'PBKDF2-SHA256';
  iterations: number;
  salt: string;
  iv: string;
  ciphertext: string;
};

function storage(): Storage {
  try {
    if (!globalThis.localStorage) throw new Error();
    return globalThis.localStorage;
  } catch {
    throw new Error('This browser does not allow local capsule storage.');
  }
}

function requireCrypto(): void {
  if (!globalThis.crypto?.subtle) {
    throw new Error('Capsule encryption requires a secure browser connection (HTTPS or localhost).');
  }
}

function validatePin(pin: string): void {
  if (!/^\d{8,64}$/.test(pin)) throw new Error('Use a PIN containing 8 to 64 digits.');
}

function bytesToBase64(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  let binary = '';
  for (const byte of bytes) binary += String.fromCharCode(byte);
  return btoa(binary);
}

function base64ToBytes(value: string): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> {
  if (!/^(?:[A-Za-z0-9+/]{4})*(?:[A-Za-z0-9+/]{2}==|[A-Za-z0-9+/]{3}=)?$/.test(value)) {
    throw new Error('Invalid capsule encoding.');
  }
  return Uint8Array.from(atob(value), (character) => character.charCodeAt(0));
}

function readEnvelope(): Envelope {
  const raw = storage().getItem(VAULT_STORAGE_KEY);
  if (!raw || raw.length > 1_400_000) throw new Error('Capsule data is missing or damaged.');
  let parsed: unknown;
  try { parsed = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { throw new Error('Capsule data is damaged.'); }
  const value = parsed as Partial<Envelope> | null;
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value) ||
      value.version !== 1 || value.kdf !== 'PBKDF2-SHA256' || value.iterations !== ITERATIONS ||
      typeof value.salt !== 'string' || typeof value.iv !== 'string' || typeof value.ciphertext !== 'string') {
    throw new Error('Capsule format is unsupported or damaged.');
  }
  const salt = base64ToBytes(value.salt);
  const iv = base64ToBytes(value.iv);
  const ciphertext = base64ToBytes(value.ciphertext);
  if (salt.length !== 16 || iv.length !== 12 || ciphertext.length < 16) {
    throw new Error('Capsule data is damaged.');
  }
  return value as Envelope;
}

function validateData(value: unknown): asserts value is VaultData {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error('Invalid capsule data.');
  const data = value as VaultData;
  const keys = Object.keys(data).sort().join(',');
  if (keys !== 'appData,capsule,displayName' || typeof data.displayName !== 'string' ||
      !data.displayName.trim() || data.displayName.length > 80 ||
      !data.appData || typeof data.appData !== 'object' || Array.isArray(data.appData) ||
      !validateCapsule(data.capsule)) {
    throw new Error('Capsule data does not match the OMI Personal Capsule schema.');
  }
}

async function deriveKey(pin: string, salt: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>): Promise<CryptoKey> {
  requireCrypto();
  const material = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', encoder.encode(pin), 'PBKDF2', false, ['deriveKey']);
  const key = await crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
    { name: 'PBKDF2', hash: 'SHA-256', salt, iterations: ITERATIONS },
    material, { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 }, false, ['encrypt', 'decrypt'],
  );
  keySalts.set(key, salt);
  return key;
}

function encodeData(data: VaultData): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> {
  validateData(data);
  const bytes = encoder.encode(JSON.stringify(data));
  if (bytes.length > MAX_PLAINTEXT_BYTES) throw new Error('Your capsule is full. Remove some local app data before saving.');
  return bytes;
}

async function encryptAndStore(bytes: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>, key: CryptoKey, salt: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>, creating = false): Promise<void> {
  const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
  const ciphertext = await crypto.subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv, additionalData: authenticatedContext }, key, bytes);
  const envelope: Envelope = {
    version: 1, kdf: 'PBKDF2-SHA256', iterations: ITERATIONS,
    salt: bytesToBase64(salt), iv: bytesToBase64(iv), ciphertext: bytesToBase64(new Uint8Array(ciphertext)),
  };
  // Encryption is asynchronous; recheck ownership immediately before committing the write.
  if (creating) {
    if (hasVault()) throw new Error('A capsule already exists. Unlock it to continue.');
  } else if (readEnvelope().salt !== bytesToBase64(salt)) {
    throw new Error('The capsule changed in another tab. Unlock it again before saving.');
  }
  try { storage().setItem(VAULT_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(envelope)); }
  catch { throw new Error('Could not save the capsule. Browser storage may be full or unavailable.'); }
}

export function hasVault(): boolean {
  return storage().getItem(VAULT_STORAGE_KEY) !== null;
}

export async function createVault(displayName: string, pin: string): Promise<{ data: VaultData; key: CryptoKey }> {
  requireCrypto();
  validatePin(pin);
  if (hasVault()) throw new Error('A capsule already exists. Unlock it to continue.');
  const name = displayName.trim();
  if (!name || name.length > 80) throw new Error('Enter a name between 1 and 80 characters.');
  const data: VaultData = {
    displayName: name,
    appData: {},
    capsule: {
      capsule_id: crypto.randomUUID(), owner_id: crypto.randomUUID(), version: '0.1.0',
      identity: { auth_methods: ['pin'], raw_biometrics_retained: false, preferred_language: 'en' },
      privacy: {
        location: { mode: 'private' }, presence: { mode: 'private' }, timeline: { mode: 'private' },
        media: { mode: 'private' }, messages: { mode: 'private' }, travel: { mode: 'private' },
      },
      services: [],
      emergency: { enabled: false, contacts: [], location_share_on_sos: false },
      sharing: {
        enabled: false, max_idle_share_percent: 15, load_balance: false, share_window: null,
        auto_manage: false, turbo_manual: false, resource_types: [],
      },
    },
  };
  const bytes = encodeData(data);
  const salt = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
  const key = await deriveKey(pin, salt);
  // Do not replace a capsule created by another tab while key derivation was running.
  if (hasVault()) throw new Error('A capsule already exists. Unlock it to continue.');
  await encryptAndStore(bytes, key, salt, true);
  return { data, key };
}

export async function unlockVault(pin: string): Promise<{ data: VaultData; key: CryptoKey }> {
  requireCrypto();
  validatePin(pin);
  const envelope = readEnvelope();
  const key = await deriveKey(pin, base64ToBytes(envelope.salt));
  let data: unknown;
  try {
    const plaintext = await crypto.subtle.decrypt(
      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: base64ToBytes(envelope.iv), additionalData: authenticatedContext },
      key, base64ToBytes(envelope.ciphertext),
    );
    data = JSON.parse(decoder.decode(plaintext));
  } catch {
    throw new Error('Could not unlock the capsule. Check your PIN; the saved data may also be damaged.');
  }
  validateData(data);
  // Authorization belongs to the current session. Never resume a saved share window.
  data.capsule.sharing = {
    ...data.capsule.sharing, enabled: false, load_balance: false, share_window: null,
    auto_manage: false, turbo_manual: false,
  };
  return { data, key };
}

export async function saveVault(data: VaultData, key: CryptoKey): Promise<void> {
  requireCrypto();
  const salt = keySalts.get(key);
  if (!salt) throw new Error('Unlock your capsule again before saving.');
  // Capture this specific edit before awaiting other queued writes.
  const bytes = encodeData(data);
  const operation = saveQueue.then(async () => {
    const current = readEnvelope();
    if (current.salt !== bytesToBase64(salt)) throw new Error('The capsule changed in another tab. Unlock it again before saving.');
    await encryptAndStore(bytes, key, salt);
  });
  saveQueue = operation.catch(() => undefined);
  return operation;
}

export function removeVault(): void {
  storage().removeItem(VAULT_STORAGE_KEY);
}

import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { verifyCartridge, validCartridgeData } from './cartridges';

const bundle = JSON.parse(readFileSync('public/cartridges/eagles-nest.bundle.json', 'utf8'));
describe('signed cartridge boundary', () => {
  it('verifies locally signed Eagle’s Nest and its restrictions', async () => {
    const mounted = await verifyCartridge(bundle, 'eagles-nest');
    expect(mounted.html).toContain("connect-src 'none'");
    expect(mounted.appId).toBe('eagles-nest');
  });
  it('rejects a modified payload before rendering', async () => {
    const modified = { ...bundle, payload: Buffer.from(Buffer.from(bundle.payload, 'base64').toString() + ' ').toString('base64') };
    await expect(verifyCartridge(modified, 'eagles-nest')).rejects.toThrow(/Signature/);
  });
  it('rejects unsigned content and unavailable registrations', async () => {
    await expect(verifyCartridge({ payload: bundle.payload }, 'eagles-nest')).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(verifyCartridge(bundle, 'corvid')).rejects.toThrow(/no installed/);
  });
  it('bounds namespaced cartridge data and rejects malformed values', () => {
    const entry = { id: '1', title: 'Family plan', body: 'Private', kind: 'note', done: false, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() };
    expect(validCartridgeData({ version: 1, entries: [entry] })).toBe(true);
    expect(validCartridgeData({ version: 1, entries: [entry, entry] })).toBe(false);
    expect(validCartridgeData({ version: 1, entries: [{ ...entry, body: 'x'.repeat(801) }] })).toBe(false);
    expect(validCartridgeData({ version: 1, entries: [{ ...entry, capsule: {} }] })).toBe(false);
    expect(validCartridgeData({ version: 1, entries: [entry], __omiGovernance: [] })).toBe(false);
  });
});

===== BEGIN FILE source-review/src/core/cartridges.ts | SHA256 d50cff0ca19d758d4416199759bd9864c3f8dde5fbcf6e4e655b8e20e52624f0 | BYTES 3927 =====
import registry from '../../omi-client/registry/apps.json';
import trust from '../generated/cartridge-trust.json';

export const registeredApps = registry.apps.map(app => ({ ...app, available: app.app_id === 'eagles-nest' }));
export type RegisteredApp = typeof registeredApps[number];
export type VerifiedCartridge = { appId: string; version: string; html: string; signer: string };

function bytes(base64: string): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> {
  return Uint8Array.from(atob(base64), c => c.charCodeAt(0));
}

export async function verifyCartridge(bundle: unknown, expectedAppId: string): Promise<VerifiedCartridge> {
  const app = registeredApps.find(app => app.app_id === expectedAppId && app.enabled && app.available);
  if (!app) throw new Error('This cartridge has no installed implementation.');
  if (!bundle || typeof bundle !== 'object') throw new Error('Invalid cartridge bundle.');
  const candidate = bundle as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (typeof candidate.payload !== 'string' || typeof candidate.signature !== 'string' || candidate.payload.length > 700_000 || candidate.signature.length > 128) {
    throw new Error('Invalid or oversized signed cartridge.');
  }
  const payload = bytes(candidate.payload);
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('spki', bytes(trust.publicKey), { name: 'Ed25519' }, false, ['verify']);
  if (!await crypto.subtle.verify('Ed25519', key, bytes(candidate.signature), payload)) {
    throw new Error('Signature verification failed. OMI refused to mount this cartridge.');
  }
  const decoded: unknown = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(payload));
  if (!decoded || typeof decoded !== 'object') throw new Error('Invalid signed payload.');
  const content = decoded as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (content.appId !== expectedAppId || typeof content.version !== 'string' || typeof content.html !== 'string') {
    throw new Error('The signed cartridge identity does not match its registration.');
  }
  if (!content.html.includes('http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy"')) throw new Error('Cartridge network restrictions missing.');
  return { appId: expectedAppId, version: content.version, html: content.html, signer: trust.label };
}

export async function loadSignedCartridge(appId: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<VerifiedCartridge> {
  if (!registeredApps.some(app => app.app_id === appId && app.available)) throw new Error('Cartridge source is not installed.');
  const response = await fetch(`/cartridges/${encodeURIComponent(appId)}.bundle.json`, { signal, cache: 'no-store' });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('The signed cartridge bundle could not be loaded.');
  return verifyCartridge(await response.json(), appId);
}

export function validCartridgeData(value: unknown): boolean {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return false;
  const data = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (Object.keys(data).sort().join(',') !== 'entries,version') return false;
  if (data.version !== 1 || !Array.isArray(data.entries) || data.entries.length > 50) return false;
  const ids = new Set<string>();
  return data.entries.every((entry: unknown) => {
    if (!entry || typeof entry !== 'object' || Array.isArray(entry)) return false;
    const note = entry as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (Object.keys(note).some(key => !['id', 'title', 'body', 'kind', 'done', 'createdAt'].includes(key))) return false;
    if (typeof note.id !== 'string' || !note.id || note.id.length > 100 || ids.has(note.id)) return false;
    ids.add(note.id);
    return typeof note.title === 'string' && note.title.length > 0 && note.title.length <= 80 &&
      typeof note.body === 'string' && note.body.length <= 800 && (note.kind === 'note' || note.kind === 'task') &&
      typeof note.done === 'boolean' && typeof note.createdAt === 'string' && note.createdAt.length <= 40 && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(note.createdAt));
  });
}

===== BEGIN FILE source-review/src/core/codex.test.ts | SHA256 dd39c9e35d512f14dcb7b84be0c8f4ef21b30398b04d87743802594b03427d4a | BYTES 8705 =====
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  analyzeState, codexDerivative, DEFAULT_PARAMETERS, DEFAULT_SETTINGS, parameterWarnings,
  simulateCodex, validateCodexParameters, type CodexParameters,
} from './codex';

const parameters = (changes: Partial<CodexParameters> = {}): CodexParameters => ({ ...DEFAULT_PARAMETERS, ...changes });

describe('Dual Merge Codex exact equations', () => {
  it('matches both ODEs and every control-injection term', () => {
    const p = parameters();
    const K = 3; const T = 2;
    const phi = p.beta1 * K / p.P + p.beta2 * p.W / (1 + p.W) + p.beta3 * T / (1 + T) - p.beta4 * p.R * T / (1 + K);
    const actual = codexDerivative(p, K, T);
    expect(actual.phi).toBeCloseTo(phi, 14);
    expect(actual.dK).toBeCloseTo(p.V * K * (1 - K / p.P) - p.R * K * T - p.D * K, 14);
    expect(actual.dT).toBeCloseTo(p.M * K * T - p.S * T - p.D * T + p.alpha * phi, 14);
  });

  it('has the exact positive interior equilibrium for the uncontrolled base system', () => {
    const p = parameters({ alpha: 0 });
    const K = (p.S + p.D) / p.M;
    const T = (p.V * (1 - K / p.P) - p.D) / p.R;
    expect(K).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(T).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(analyzeState(p, K, T).residual).toBeLessThan(1e-14);
    const result = simulateCodex(p, { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, initialK: K, initialT: T });
    expect(result.status).toBe('completed');
    for (const sample of result.samples) {
      expect(sample.K).toBeCloseTo(K, 11);
      expect(sample.T).toBeCloseTo(T, 11);
    }
  });

  it('uses the full arbitrary-state Jacobian and matches centered finite differences', () => {
    const p = parameters();
    const K = 1.7; const T = 0.8; const epsilon = 1e-5;
    const analysis = analyzeState(p, K, T);
    const plusK = codexDerivative(p, K + epsilon, T);
    const minusK = codexDerivative(p, K - epsilon, T);
    const plusT = codexDerivative(p, K, T + epsilon);
    const minusT = codexDerivative(p, K, T - epsilon);
    expect(analysis.jacobian[0][0]).toBeCloseTo((plusK.dK - minusK.dK) / (2 * epsilon), 8);
    expect(analysis.jacobian[1][0]).toBeCloseTo((plusK.dT - minusK.dT) / (2 * epsilon), 8);
    expect(analysis.jacobian[0][1]).toBeCloseTo((plusT.dK - minusT.dK) / (2 * epsilon), 8);
    expect(analysis.jacobian[1][1]).toBeCloseTo((plusT.dT - minusT.dT) / (2 * epsilon), 8);
    expect(analysis.jacobian[0][0]).not.toBeCloseTo(-p.V * K / p.P, 4);
    expect(analysis.trace).toBe(analysis.jacobian[0][0] + analysis.jacobian[1][1]);
    expect(analysis.residual).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('separates hard-invalid inputs from conditional-model warnings', () => {
    expect(validateCodexParameters(parameters())).toEqual([]);
    const p = parameters({ V: 0, alpha: 0, beta4: 1 });
    expect(validateCodexParameters(p)).toEqual([]);
    expect(parameterWarnings(p).join(' ')).toContain('V ≤ D');
    expect(parameterWarnings(p).join(' ')).toContain('beta4 <');
    expect(parameterWarnings(p).join(' ')).toContain('uncontrolled');
    for (const invalid of [parameters({ P: 0 }), parameters({ D: 0 }), parameters({ W: 0 }), parameters({ M: -1 }), parameters({ beta1: NaN }), parameters({ V: Infinity }), parameters({ R: 1e5 })]) {
      expect(validateCodexParameters(invalid).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(() => simulateCodex(invalid, DEFAULT_SETTINGS)).toThrow(RangeError);
    }
  });
});

describe('bounded adaptive Dormand–Prince integration', () => {
  it('completes illustrative defaults with finite positive fixed-grid output including endpoints', () => {
    const result = simulateCodex(DEFAULT_PARAMETERS, DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(result.status).toBe('completed');
    expect(result.samples).toHaveLength(401);
    expect(result.samples[0].t).toBe(0);
    expect(result.samples.at(-1)?.t).toBe(DEFAULT_SETTINGS.duration);
    expect(result.samples.every(sample => sample.K > 0 && sample.T > 0 && Object.values(sample).every(Number.isFinite))).toBe(true);
    expect(result.samples.every((sample, i) => i === 0 || sample.t > result.samples[i - 1].t)).toBe(true);
    expect(result.acceptedSteps).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(400);
    expect(result.reason).toContain('not a stability proof');
  });

  it('converges toward a tighter adaptive reference and actively rejects oversized trials', () => {
    const p = parameters({ V: 50, R: 1.5, M: 0.5, alpha: 0.03 });
    const s = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, initialK: 8, initialT: 0.5, duration: 20 };
    const loose = simulateCodex(p, { ...s, tolerance: 1e-3 });
    const tight = simulateCodex(p, { ...s, tolerance: 1e-7 });
    const reference = simulateCodex(p, { ...s, tolerance: 1e-10 });
    expect([loose.status, tight.status, reference.status]).toEqual(['completed', 'completed', 'completed']);
    const error = (result: typeof loose) => Math.max(...result.samples.map((sample, index) => Math.hypot(sample.K - reference.samples[index].K, sample.T - reference.samples[index].T)));
    expect(error(tight)).toBeLessThan(error(loose));
    expect(error(tight)).toBeLessThan(1e-5);
    expect(tight.rejectedSteps).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(reference.acceptedSteps).toBeGreaterThan(tight.acceptedSteps);
  });

  it('matches an analytic decoupled logistic/decay solution without control', () => {
    const p = parameters({ R: 0, M: 0, alpha: 0 });
    const s = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, duration: 3, tolerance: 1e-9 };
    const result = simulateCodex(p, s);
    const last = result.samples.at(-1)!;
    const growth = p.V - p.D;
    const capacity = p.P * growth / p.V;
    const exactK = capacity / (1 + (capacity / s.initialK - 1) * Math.exp(-growth * s.duration));
    const exactT = s.initialT * Math.exp(-(p.S + p.D) * s.duration);
    expect(result.status).toBe('completed');
    expect(last.K).toBeCloseTo(exactK, 8);
    expect(last.T).toBeCloseTo(exactT, 9);
  });

  it('is deterministic and does not mutate caller settings or parameters', () => {
    const p = parameters(); const s = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
    const before = JSON.stringify({ p, s });
    const first = simulateCodex(p, s);
    const second = simulateCodex(p, s);
    expect(second).toEqual(first);
    expect(JSON.stringify({ p, s })).toBe(before);
    p.V = 9; s.duration = 50;
    expect(first.parameters.V).toBe(DEFAULT_PARAMETERS.V);
    expect(first.settings.duration).toBe(DEFAULT_SETTINGS.duration);
  });

  it('rejects invalid initial states, tolerances, and unreasonable horizons', () => {
    for (const s of [
      { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, initialK: 0 }, { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, initialT: -1 },
      { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, initialK: NaN }, { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, initialT: Infinity },
      { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, initialK: 1e7 }, { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, duration: 0 },
      { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, duration: 1001 }, { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, tolerance: 1e-15 },
    ]) expect(() => simulateCodex(DEFAULT_PARAMETERS, s)).toThrow(RangeError);
    expect(() => codexDerivative(DEFAULT_PARAMETERS, -1, 1)).toThrow(RangeError);
    expect(() => analyzeState(DEFAULT_PARAMETERS, 1, NaN)).toThrow(RangeError);
    // Positive finite inputs can still exceed floating-point representability.
    // Refuse them before any non-finite initial sample can reach JSON export.
    expect(() => simulateCodex(parameters({ P: Number.MIN_VALUE }), DEFAULT_SETTINGS)).toThrow(/Initial derivative is non-finite/);
  });

  it('exhausts a fixed derivative budget on a long stiff system instead of blocking indefinitely', () => {
    const result = simulateCodex(parameters({ V: 1e4, R: 0, M: 0, alpha: 0 }), { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, duration: 1000 });
    expect(result.status).toBe('contained');
    expect(result.reason).toContain('derivative budget exhausted');
    expect(result.acceptedSteps + result.rejectedSteps).toBeLessThanOrEqual(20_000);
    expect(result.samples.at(-1)!.t).toBeLessThan(1000);
    expect(result.samples.every(sample => sample.K > 0 && sample.T > 0 && Object.values(sample).every(Number.isFinite))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('contains an extreme stiff run with bounded work and preserves only valid states', () => {
    const p = parameters({ V: 1e4, P: 1e-9, M: 1e4 });
    const result = simulateCodex(p, { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, initialK: 1e6, initialT: 1e6, duration: 1000, tolerance: 1e-10 });
    expect(result.status).toBe('contained');
    expect(result.acceptedSteps + result.rejectedSteps).toBeLessThanOrEqual(20_000);
    expect(result.samples.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1000);
    expect(result.samples.every(sample => sample.K > 0 && sample.T > 0 && Object.values(sample).every(Number.isFinite))).toBe(true);
    expect(result.samples.at(-1)!.t).toBeLessThan(1000);
    expect(result.reason).toMatch(/budget|boundary|tolerance/);
  });
});
