===== BEGIN FILE source-review/src/core/codex.ts | SHA256 a3e954fd45e1fe45d9586471a3387a5a207bcfb31b50580e4efca9a6d0e322f5 | BYTES 12375 =====
/**
 * Dual Merge Codex, master whitepaper §6.1. Numerical exploration only.
 * Defaults are illustrative; only the beta baseline is taken from the paper.
 * Adaptive Dormand–Prince 5(4) never evaluates outside the positive orthant,
 * clamps no state, and contains a run if its numerical/domain budget is reached.
 */
export type CodexParameters = {
  V: number; P: number; D: number; R: number; M: number; S: number;
  alpha: number; W: number; beta1: number; beta2: number; beta3: number; beta4: number;
};
export type SimulationSettings = { initialK: number; initialT: number; duration: number; tolerance: number };
export type Sample = { t: number; K: number; T: number; phi: number; dK: number; dT: number };
export type SimulationResult = {
  parameters: CodexParameters;
  settings: SimulationSettings;
  samples: Sample[];
  status: 'completed' | 'contained';
  reason: string;
  acceptedSteps: number;
  rejectedSteps: number;
  modelVersion: string;
};

export const DEFAULT_PARAMETERS: Readonly<CodexParameters> = Object.freeze({
  V: 1.2, P: 10, D: 0.1, R: 0.2, M: 0.15, S: 0.3,
  alpha: 0.1, W: 1, beta1: 1.5, beta2: 3, beta3: 1.5, beta4: 7,
});
export const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: Readonly<SimulationSettings> = Object.freeze({
  initialK: 2, initialT: 0.5, duration: 30, tolerance: 1e-6,
});

const MODEL_VERSION = 'dual-merge-codex/1.0.0-dormand-prince-54';
const MAX_EVALUATIONS = 100_000;
const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 20_000;
const OUTPUT_INTERVALS = 400;
const MAX_STATE = 1e9;
const MAX_INITIAL_STATE = 1e6;
const parameterKeys = ['V', 'P', 'D', 'R', 'M', 'S', 'alpha', 'W', 'beta1', 'beta2', 'beta3', 'beta4'] as const;
const positiveKeys = new Set<keyof CodexParameters>(['P', 'D', 'S', 'W']);
const isFiniteNumber = (value: unknown): value is number => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);

/** Hard numeric/domain errors only; model-regime warnings are separate. */
export function validateCodexParameters(parameters: CodexParameters): string[] {
  if (!parameters || typeof parameters !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parameters)) return ['Parameters must be an object.'];
  const errors: string[] = [];
  for (const key of parameterKeys) {
    const value = parameters[key];
    const maximum = key === 'P' || key === 'W' ? 1e6 : 1e4;
    if (!isFiniteNumber(value)) errors.push(`${key} must be a finite number.`);
    else if (positiveKeys.has(key) ? value <= 0 || value > maximum : value < 0 || value > maximum) {
      errors.push(`${key} must be ${positiveKeys.has(key) ? 'greater than zero' : 'nonnegative'} and at most ${maximum}.`);
    }
  }
  return errors;
}

export function parameterWarnings(parameters: CodexParameters): string[] {
  assertParameters(parameters);
  const warnings: string[] = [];
  if (parameters.V <= parameters.D) warnings.push('V ≤ D: the paper’s necessary growth condition for a positive Harmony equilibrium is not satisfied.');
  if (parameters.beta4 < parameters.beta1 + parameters.beta2 + parameters.beta3) {
    warnings.push('beta4 < beta1 + beta2 + beta3: the paper’s proposed control-weight condition is not satisfied.');
  }
  if (parameters.alpha === 0) warnings.push('alpha = 0: this is the uncontrolled base system; control injection is disabled.');
  if (parameters.M === 0 || parameters.R === 0) warnings.push('A zero coupling parameter makes the paper’s interior base-equilibrium formulas degenerate.');
  warnings.push('Numerical trajectories and local Jacobians do not establish a global stability or deployment-safety proof.');
  return warnings;
}

function assertParameters(parameters: CodexParameters): void {
  const errors = validateCodexParameters(parameters);
  if (errors.length) throw new RangeError(errors.join(' '));
}
function assertSettings(settings: SimulationSettings): void {
  if (!settings || typeof settings !== 'object') throw new RangeError('Simulation settings must be an object.');
  for (const key of ['initialK', 'initialT'] as const) {
    if (!isFiniteNumber(settings[key]) || settings[key] <= 0 || settings[key] > MAX_INITIAL_STATE) {
      throw new RangeError(`${key} must be greater than zero and at most ${MAX_INITIAL_STATE}.`);
    }
  }
  if (!isFiniteNumber(settings.duration) || settings.duration < 0.01 || settings.duration > 1000) {
    throw new RangeError('Duration must be between 0.01 and 1000 model time units.');
  }
  if (!isFiniteNumber(settings.tolerance) || settings.tolerance < 1e-10 || settings.tolerance > 1e-3) {
    throw new RangeError('Tolerance must be between 1e-10 and 1e-3.');
  }
}

type State = readonly [number, number];
type Derivative = { dK: number; dT: number; phi: number };
function stateProblem(K: number, T: number): string | null {
  if (!Number.isFinite(K) || !Number.isFinite(T)) return 'non-finite state';
  if (K <= 0 || T <= 0) return 'positive-orthant boundary';
  if (K > MAX_STATE || T > MAX_STATE) return 'numerical magnitude boundary (1e9)';
  return null;
}
function derivative(parameters: CodexParameters, K: number, T: number): Derivative {
  const { V, P, R, D, M, S, alpha, W, beta1, beta2, beta3, beta4 } = parameters;
  const phi = beta1 * (K / P) + beta2 * (W / (1 + W)) + beta3 * (T / (1 + T)) - beta4 * (R * T / (1 + K));
  const dK = V * K * (1 - K / P) - R * K * T - D * K;
  const dT = M * K * T - S * T - D * T + alpha * phi;
  return { dK, dT, phi };
}

/** Exact §6.1 right-hand side, with explicit positive-domain validation. */
export function codexDerivative(parameters: CodexParameters, K: number, T: number): Derivative {
  assertParameters(parameters);
  const problem = stateProblem(K, T);
  if (problem) throw new RangeError(`Cannot evaluate the Codex outside its domain: ${problem}.`);
  const result = derivative(parameters, K, T);
  if (!Object.values(result).every(Number.isFinite)) throw new RangeError('The Codex derivative is non-finite.');
  return result;
}

/** Full arbitrary-point Jacobian. This does not assert the point is an equilibrium. */
export function analyzeState(parameters: CodexParameters, K: number, T: number): {
  jacobian: number[][]; trace: number; determinant: number; residual: number;
} {
  const rhs = codexDerivative(parameters, K, T);
  const { V, P, R, D, M, S, alpha, beta1, beta3, beta4 } = parameters;
  const j11 = V * (1 - 2 * K / P) - R * T - D;
  const j12 = -R * K;
  const j21 = M * T + alpha * (beta1 / P + beta4 * R * T / ((1 + K) ** 2));
  const j22 = M * K - S - D + alpha * (beta3 / ((1 + T) ** 2) - beta4 * R / (1 + K));
  return {
    jacobian: [[j11, j12], [j21, j22]],
    trace: j11 + j22,
    determinant: j11 * j22 - j12 * j21,
    residual: Math.hypot(rhs.dK, rhs.dT),
  };
}

/**
 * Dormand–Prince embedded RK5(4), adaptive relative/absolute error control.
 * Output times are fixed, but internal accepted/rejected steps are adaptive.
 * Relative tolerance = settings.tolerance; absolute tolerance = tolerance/1000.
 * W is held fixed for this experiment, not modeled as an endogenous variable.
 */
export function simulateCodex(parameters: CodexParameters, settings: SimulationSettings): SimulationResult {
  assertParameters(parameters);
  assertSettings(settings);
  const p = { ...parameters };
  const s = { ...settings };
  const samples: Sample[] = [];
  let t = 0;
  let state: State = [s.initialK, s.initialT];
  let acceptedSteps = 0;
  let rejectedSteps = 0;
  let evaluations = 0;
  let h = Math.min(s.duration / OUTPUT_INTERVALS, 0.01);
  let outputIndex = 1;
  let lastProblem = 'requested error tolerance';
  const evaluate = (candidate: State): Derivative => {
    if (evaluations >= MAX_EVALUATIONS) throw new Error('Derivative evaluation budget exhausted.');
    evaluations++;
    return derivative(p, candidate[0], candidate[1]);
  };
  let currentDerivative = evaluate(state);
  if (!Object.values(currentDerivative).every(Number.isFinite)) {
    throw new RangeError('Initial derivative is non-finite; the supplied parameters and state exceed the numerical range.');
  }
  const record = () => samples.push({ t, K: state[0], T: state[1], ...currentDerivative });
  const finish = (status: SimulationResult['status'], reason: string): SimulationResult => {
    // Include the final accepted state on an interrupted output interval.
    if (samples.at(-1)?.t !== t) record();
    return { parameters: p, settings: s, samples, status, reason, acceptedSteps, rejectedSteps, modelVersion: MODEL_VERSION };
  };
  record();

  // Each stage is checked BEFORE derivative evaluation. A rejected stage changes
  // only h; it never changes the accepted state or clips it to an allowed value.
  const combine = (step: number, terms: readonly (readonly [number, Derivative])[]): State => [
    state[0] + step * terms.reduce((sum, [weight, rhs]) => sum + weight * rhs.dK, 0),
    state[1] + step * terms.reduce((sum, [weight, rhs]) => sum + weight * rhs.dT, 0),
  ];
  const tryStage = (candidate: State): Derivative | null => {
    const problem = stateProblem(candidate[0], candidate[1]);
    if (problem) { lastProblem = problem; return null; }
    const result = evaluate(candidate);
    if (!Object.values(result).every(Number.isFinite)) { lastProblem = 'non-finite derivative'; return null; }
    return result;
  };

  while (outputIndex <= OUTPUT_INTERVALS) {
    if (acceptedSteps + rejectedSteps >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) return finish('contained', 'Adaptive solver step budget exhausted; partial trajectory retained.');
    // Reserve the six new derivative evaluations needed for a full RK trial.
    // The first stage is cached from the last accepted step (FSAL property).
    if (evaluations + 6 > MAX_EVALUATIONS) return finish('contained', 'Adaptive solver derivative budget exhausted; partial trajectory retained.');
    const target = outputIndex === OUTPUT_INTERVALS ? s.duration : s.duration * outputIndex / OUTPUT_INTERVALS;
    const remaining = target - t;
    const boundarySlack = Number.EPSILON * Math.max(1, Math.abs(target)) * 16;
    const step = h >= remaining - boundarySlack ? remaining : h;
    const minimumStep = Math.max(1e-14, Number.EPSILON * Math.max(1, Math.abs(t)) * 16);
    if (!Number.isFinite(step) || step < minimumStep || t + step === t) {
      return finish('contained', `Adaptive step cannot be reduced safely near the ${lastProblem}; no state was clamped.`);
    }
    const k1 = currentDerivative;
    const k2 = tryStage(combine(step, [[1 / 5, k1]]));
    const k3 = k2 && tryStage(combine(step, [[3 / 40, k1], [9 / 40, k2]]));
    const k4 = k3 && tryStage(combine(step, [[44 / 45, k1], [-56 / 15, k2!], [32 / 9, k3]]));
    const k5 = k4 && tryStage(combine(step, [[19372 / 6561, k1], [-25360 / 2187, k2!], [64448 / 6561, k3!], [-212 / 729, k4]]));
    const k6 = k5 && tryStage(combine(step, [[9017 / 3168, k1], [-355 / 33, k2!], [46732 / 5247, k3!], [49 / 176, k4!], [-5103 / 18656, k5]]));
    const fifth = k6 && combine(step, [[35 / 384, k1], [500 / 1113, k3!], [125 / 192, k4!], [-2187 / 6784, k5!], [11 / 84, k6]]);
    const k7 = fifth && tryStage(fifth);
    if (!k7 || !fifth) {
      rejectedSteps++;
      h = step * 0.2;
      continue;
    }
    const fourth = combine(step, [[5179 / 57600, k1], [7571 / 16695, k3!], [393 / 640, k4!], [-92097 / 339200, k5!], [187 / 2100, k6!], [1 / 40, k7]]);
    const absoluteTolerance = s.tolerance / 1000;
    const error = Math.max(
      Math.abs(fifth[0] - fourth[0]) / (absoluteTolerance + s.tolerance * Math.max(Math.abs(state[0]), Math.abs(fifth[0]))),
      Math.abs(fifth[1] - fourth[1]) / (absoluteTolerance + s.tolerance * Math.max(Math.abs(state[1]), Math.abs(fifth[1]))),
    );
    if (!Number.isFinite(error) || error > 1) {
      rejectedSteps++;
      lastProblem = 'requested error tolerance';
      h = step * (Number.isFinite(error) ? Math.max(0.1, Math.min(0.5, 0.9 * error ** -0.2)) : 0.1);
      continue;
    }
    state = fifth;
    currentDerivative = k7;
    // A boundary-limited step mathematically ends at target; assign that same
    // time exactly to avoid spurious machine-precision tail steps.
    t = step === remaining ? target : t + step;
    acceptedSteps++;
    h = step * (error === 0 ? 5 : Math.max(0.2, Math.min(5, 0.9 * error ** -0.2)));
    if (t === target) {
      record();
      outputIndex++;
    }
  }
  return finish('completed', 'Requested horizon completed with adaptive error control. W was fixed; this numerical trajectory is not a stability proof.');
}

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { appendAudit, canonicalize, evaluateProposal, LOCAL_POLICY, QUORUM, verifyAudit, type Proposal } from './governance';

const proposal = (overrides: Partial<Proposal> = {}): Proposal => ({
  id: 'local-artifact-1', title: 'Draft a family checklist', intent: 'Create a local sandbox artifact',
  volatility: 0.1, harmProbability: 0, harmSeverity: 0, reversible: true,
  evidence: 'Local synthetic inputs; artifact stays on this device.', operator: 'Test operator', ...overrides,
});

describe('local governance decisions', () => {
  it('never substitutes low risk for human authorization', () => {
    const decision = evaluateProposal(proposal());
    expect(decision.region).toBe('safe');
    expect(decision.outcome).toBe('review');
    expect(decision.trace.join(' ')).toContain('human authorization');
    expect(decision.trace.join(' ')).toContain('proof is not implemented');
    expect(LOCAL_POLICY.posture).toContain('Provisional');
  });

  it('fails closed for missing evidence, identity, and malformed inputs', () => {
    for (const invalid of [
      proposal({ evidence: ' ' }), proposal({ operator: '' }), proposal({ title: '' }),
      proposal({ volatility: Number.NaN }), proposal({ harmProbability: -0.1 }),
      proposal({ harmSeverity: 1.01 }), proposal({ reversible: 'yes' as unknown as boolean }),
      null as unknown as Proposal,
    ]) expect(evaluateProposal(invalid).outcome).toBe('abstain');
  });

  it('does not let missing evidence or invalid fields conceal a known critical signal', () => {
    for (const input of [
      proposal({ volatility: 1, evidence: '' }),
      proposal({ volatility: .75, harmProbability: Number.NaN }),
      proposal({ volatility: Number.NaN, harmProbability: 1, harmSeverity: .5 }),
      proposal({ operator: '', harmProbability: .8, harmSeverity: 1 }),
    ]) {
      const decision = evaluateProposal(input);
      expect(decision.outcome).toBe('contained');
      expect(decision.region).toBe('red');
      expect(decision.trace.join(' ')).toContain('human clearance');
    }
  });

  it('enforces exact provisional WACV region boundaries', () => {
    expect([0, .249, .25, .499, .5, .749, .75, 1].map(volatility =>
      evaluateProposal(proposal({ volatility })).region,
    )).toEqual(['safe', 'safe', 'warn', 'warn', 'guard', 'guard', 'red', 'red']);
    expect(evaluateProposal(proposal({ volatility: .75 })).outcome).toBe('contained');
  });

  it('contains high projected harm regardless of WACV and guards irreversibility', () => {
    expect(evaluateProposal(proposal({ harmProbability: .5, harmSeverity: 1 })).outcome).toBe('contained');
    expect(evaluateProposal(proposal({ harmProbability: .2, harmSeverity: 1 })).region).toBe('guard');
    expect(evaluateProposal(proposal({ reversible: false })).region).toBe('guard');
  });

  it('makes latency monotone and bounded for every valid WACV input', () => {
    const latencies = Array.from({ length: 101 }, (_, i) => evaluateProposal(proposal({ volatility: i / 100 })).tauMs);
    expect(latencies[0]).toBe(250);
    expect(latencies.at(-1)).toBe(5000);
    expect(latencies.every((value, i) => i === 0 || value >= latencies[i - 1])).toBe(true);
  });

  it('snapshots the proposal so later form edits cannot rewrite a review', () => {
    const input = proposal();
    const decision = evaluateProposal(input);
    input.volatility = 1;
    input.title = 'Changed';
    expect(decision.proposal.title).toBe('Draft a family checklist');
    expect(decision.proposal.volatility).toBe(.1);
  });

  it('preserves the fixed noncommutative Wise Quorum order', () => {
    expect(QUORUM.map(member => member.name)).toEqual([
      'Marcus Aurelius', 'Gandhi', 'Sun Tzu', 'Socrates', 'Michelangelo', 'King Solomon',
    ]);
    expect(QUORUM[5].perspective).toContain('Final arbiter');
  });
});

describe('Canonization Chain local tamper evidence', () => {
  it('appends linked records without mutating earlier chain values', async () => {
    const first = await appendAudit([], 'proposal.reviewed', { b: 2, a: 1 });
    const second = await appendAudit(first, 'human.authorized', { decisionId: 'local-artifact-1' });
    expect(first).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(second).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(second[1].previousHash).toBe(second[0].hash);
    expect(second.map(entry => entry.sequence)).toEqual([1, 2]);
    expect(await verifyAudit(second)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('detects detail tampering, event changes, missing middle records and reordering', async () => {
    let chain = await appendAudit([], 'first', { intent: 'original' });
    chain = await appendAudit(chain, 'second', {});
    chain = await appendAudit(chain, 'third', {});
    const altered = structuredClone(chain);
    altered[0].detail = { intent: 'tampered' };
    expect(await verifyAudit(altered)).toBe(false);
    expect(await verifyAudit([{ ...chain[0], event: 'forged' }, ...chain.slice(1)])).toBe(false);
    expect(await verifyAudit([chain[0], chain[2]])).toBe(false);
    expect(await verifyAudit([chain[1], chain[0], chain[2]])).toBe(false);
    await expect(appendAudit(altered, 'fourth', {})).rejects.toThrow('integrity failed');
  });

  it('canonicalizes key order and refuses values JSON would silently change', () => {
    expect(canonicalize({ b: [3, { z: 1, a: 2 }], a: 'x' })).toBe(canonicalize({ a: 'x', b: [3, { a: 2, z: 1 }] }));
    for (const value of [undefined, NaN, Infinity, 1n, new Date(), { missing: undefined }, [undefined], Array(1)]) {
      expect(() => canonicalize(value)).toThrow();
    }
    const cycle: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    cycle.self = cycle;
    expect(() => canonicalize(cycle)).toThrow('cycles');
  });

  it('binds appended details to their original snapshot', async () => {
    const detail = { title: 'original' };
    const pending = appendAudit([], 'proposal', detail);
    detail.title = 'changed';
    const chain = await pending;
    expect(chain[0].detail).toEqual({ title: 'original' });
    expect(await verifyAudit(chain)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects invalid event names and malformed entry metadata', async () => {
    await expect(appendAudit([], ' ', {})).rejects.toThrow('Audit event');
    const chain = await appendAudit([], 'proposal', {});
    expect(await verifyAudit([{ ...chain[0], sequence: 2 }])).toBe(false);
    expect(await verifyAudit([{ ...chain[0], timestamp: 'not-a-time' }])).toBe(false);
    expect(await verifyAudit([{ ...chain[0], extra: true } as typeof chain[0]])).toBe(false);
  });
});

===== BEGIN FILE source-review/src/core/governance.ts | SHA256 7eade4e10d917cdbcab1ca3826802e5f452d45df90d8d82d0a425cbe00e167f1 | BYTES 11246 =====
/**
 * Local OMI governance workbench, informed by the master whitepaper chapters 3–5.
 * These provisional numeric thresholds are implementation policy, not validated
 * WACV measurements, an alignment proof, or certification of real-world safety.
 * The only executable target is a separately authorized local sandbox artifact.
 */
export type Proposal = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  intent: string;
  volatility: number;
  harmProbability: number;
  harmSeverity: number;
  reversible: boolean;
  evidence: string;
  operator: string;
};

export type Decision = {
  proposal: Proposal;
  region: 'safe' | 'warn' | 'guard' | 'red';
  wacv: number;
  tauMs: number;
  outcome: 'review' | 'contained' | 'abstain';
  reasons: string[];
  trace: string[];
  createdAt: string;
};

export const LOCAL_POLICY = Object.freeze({
  posture: 'Provisional local policy; not validated scientific thresholds',
  warnAt: 0.25,
  guardAt: 0.5,
  containAt: 0.75,
  harmGuardAt: 0.2,
  harmContainAt: 0.5,
  minimumLatencyMs: 250,
  latencyRangeMs: 4750,
});

export const QUORUM = Object.freeze([
  Object.freeze({ name: 'Marcus Aurelius', perspective: 'Stoic discipline, long-term thinking, personal accountability' }),
  Object.freeze({ name: 'Gandhi', perspective: 'Nonviolence, moral courage, equity of consequence' }),
  Object.freeze({ name: 'Sun Tzu', perspective: 'Strategic clarity, resource economy, adversarial awareness' }),
  Object.freeze({ name: 'Socrates', perspective: 'Epistemic humility, rigorous questioning, examined assumptions' }),
  Object.freeze({ name: 'Michelangelo', perspective: 'Creative integrity, craft, the cost of excellence' }),
  Object.freeze({ name: 'King Solomon', perspective: 'Final arbiter after the five-voice review; wisdom, equity, and sovereign judgment' }),
]);

const isUnit = (value: unknown): value is number =>
  typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0 && value <= 1;
const fieldLimits = { id: 128, title: 200, intent: 4000, evidence: 8000, operator: 200 } as const;

export function evaluateProposal(input: Proposal): Decision {
  const record = input && typeof input === 'object' ? input as Partial<Proposal> : {};
  const invalid: string[] = [];
  const text = {} as Pick<Proposal, keyof typeof fieldLimits>;
  for (const [key, maximum] of Object.entries(fieldLimits) as [keyof typeof fieldLimits, number][]) {
    const value = record[key];
    if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value.trim() || value.length > maximum) {
      invalid.push(`${key} must be nonempty text of at most ${maximum} characters`);
      text[key] = typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim().slice(0, maximum) : '';
    } else {
      text[key] = value.trim();
    }
  }
  for (const key of ['volatility', 'harmProbability', 'harmSeverity'] as const) {
    if (!isUnit(record[key])) invalid.push(`${key} must be a finite number from 0 to 1`);
  }
  if (typeof record.reversible !== 'boolean') invalid.push('reversible must be a boolean');

  // Snapshot primitives so later edits to the form cannot rewrite this decision.
  const proposal: Proposal = {
    ...text,
    volatility: isUnit(record.volatility) ? record.volatility : 1,
    harmProbability: isUnit(record.harmProbability) ? record.harmProbability : 1,
    harmSeverity: isUnit(record.harmSeverity) ? record.harmSeverity : 1,
    reversible: record.reversible === true,
  };
  const wacv = proposal.volatility;
  const tauMs = LOCAL_POLICY.minimumLatencyMs + Math.ceil(wacv * LOCAL_POLICY.latencyRangeMs);
  const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
  if (invalid.length) {
    // Incomplete evidence must never hide an independently supplied critical
    // risk signal or turn a containment event into an ordinary abstention.
    const criticalSignal = (isUnit(record.volatility) && record.volatility >= LOCAL_POLICY.containAt)
      || (isUnit(record.harmProbability) && isUnit(record.harmSeverity)
        && record.harmProbability * record.harmSeverity >= LOCAL_POLICY.harmContainAt);
    return {
      proposal, region: 'red', wacv, tauMs, outcome: criticalSignal ? 'contained' : 'abstain', createdAt,
      reasons: criticalSignal ? [...invalid, 'A supplied critical risk signal requires containment even when other fields are invalid.'] : invalid,
      trace: ['Pandora: incomplete or invalid proposal rejected.', criticalSignal
        ? 'eKernel: critical risk contained; human clearance and a corrected proposal are required.'
        : 'eKernel: promotion blocked; correct the proposal before review.'],
    };
  }

  const harm = proposal.harmProbability * proposal.harmSeverity;
  let region: Decision['region'] = wacv >= LOCAL_POLICY.containAt ? 'red'
    : wacv >= LOCAL_POLICY.guardAt ? 'guard'
      : wacv >= LOCAL_POLICY.warnAt ? 'warn' : 'safe';
  const reasons: string[] = [];
  if (harm >= LOCAL_POLICY.harmContainAt) {
    region = 'red';
    reasons.push('Jiminy Cricket: estimated downstream harm reaches the local containment threshold.');
  } else if (harm >= LOCAL_POLICY.harmGuardAt && region !== 'red') {
    region = 'guard';
    reasons.push('Jiminy Cricket: estimated downstream harm requires restricted review.');
  }
  if (!proposal.reversible) {
    if (region === 'safe' || region === 'warn') region = 'guard';
    reasons.push('Jiminy Cricket: this action is not reversible; a mitigation pathway requires human review.');
  }
  if (wacv >= LOCAL_POLICY.containAt) reasons.push('Dragon Guardian: WACV proxy reaches the local containment threshold.');
  else if (wacv >= LOCAL_POLICY.guardAt) reasons.push('Dragon Guardian: elevated WACV proxy requires restricted review.');
  else if (wacv >= LOCAL_POLICY.warnAt) reasons.push('Dragon Guardian: WACV proxy requires corrective review.');
  else reasons.push('Dragon Guardian: WACV proxy is within the local safe region.');
  reasons.push('Taipan Guardian: evidence is supplied, but cryptographic semantic alignment is unproven.');
  reasons.push('Human authorization is required; no real-world action is executed by this workbench.');

  const outcome = region === 'red' ? 'contained' : 'review';
  return {
    proposal, region, wacv, tauMs, outcome, createdAt, reasons,
    trace: [
      'Pandora: normalized the proposal and checked required evidence.',
      `Dragon Guardian: operator-supplied WACV proxy ${wacv.toFixed(3)}; provisional tau_network ${tauMs} ms.`,
      `Jiminy Cricket: estimated harm score ${(harm * 100).toFixed(1)}/100; reversible ${proposal.reversible ? 'yes' : 'no'}.`,
      'Taipan Guardian: cryptographic alignment proof is not implemented; human review remains mandatory.',
      'Canonization Chain: caller must append and verify this decision before any sandbox artifact.',
      outcome === 'contained'
        ? 'eKernel: local promotion contained; no sandbox artifact may be executed.'
        : 'Wise Quorum: await five-voice review, Solomon arbitration, and explicit human authorization.',
    ],
  };
}

/** JSON-only canonical serialization; rejects lossy values rather than hiding them. */
export function canonicalize(value: unknown): string {
  const active = new Set<object>();
  const walk = (item: unknown): string => {
    if (item === null) return 'null';
    if (typeof item === 'string' || typeof item === 'boolean') return JSON.stringify(item);
    if (typeof item === 'number' && Number.isFinite(item)) return JSON.stringify(item);
    if (typeof item !== 'object') throw new TypeError('Audit details must contain only JSON values');
    if (active.has(item)) throw new TypeError('Audit details cannot contain cycles');
    active.add(item);
    let result: string;
    if (Array.isArray(item)) {
      const parts: string[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < item.length; i++) {
        if (!Object.hasOwn(item, i)) throw new TypeError('Sparse arrays are not valid audit details');
        parts.push(walk(item[i]));
      }
      result = `[${parts.join(',')}]`;
    } else {
      const prototype = Object.getPrototypeOf(item);
      if (prototype !== Object.prototype && prototype !== null) throw new TypeError('Audit details must use plain objects');
      if (Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(item).length) throw new TypeError('Symbol keys are not valid audit details');
      const object = item as Record<string, unknown>;
      result = `{${Object.keys(object).sort().map(key => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${walk(object[key])}`).join(',')}}`;
    }
    active.delete(item);
    return result;
  };
  return walk(value);
}

export type AuditEntry = {
  sequence: number;
  previousHash: string;
  hash: string;
  timestamp: string;
  event: string;
  detail: unknown;
};

const GENESIS = '0'.repeat(64);
const hashPattern = /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/;
const auditKeys = ['detail', 'event', 'hash', 'previousHash', 'sequence', 'timestamp'];
const eventIsValid = (value: unknown): value is string => typeof value === 'string' && value.trim().length > 0 && value.length <= 128;
async function digest(value: unknown): Promise<string> {
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(`omi.canonization.v1\0${canonicalize(value)}`);
  const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', bytes);
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hash), byte => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

/**
 * Verifies the supplied local chain. Without an externally anchored head, a
 * rewritten or truncated whole chain cannot be distinguished from an older one.
 * This is tamper evidence, not immutable storage or an ethical-truth proof.
 */
export async function verifyAudit(chain: AuditEntry[]): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!Array.isArray(chain)) return false;
  try {
    let previousHash = GENESIS;
    for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i++) {
      const entry = chain[i];
      if (!entry || Object.keys(entry).sort().join(',') !== auditKeys.join(',')) return false;
      if (entry.sequence !== i + 1 || entry.previousHash !== previousHash || !hashPattern.test(entry.hash)) return false;
      if (!eventIsValid(entry.event) || typeof entry.timestamp !== 'string') return false;
      const time = new Date(entry.timestamp);
      if (!Number.isFinite(time.getTime()) || time.toISOString() !== entry.timestamp) return false;
      const { hash, ...unsigned } = entry;
      if (await digest(unsigned) !== hash) return false;
      previousHash = hash;
    }
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export async function appendAudit(chain: AuditEntry[], event: string, detail: unknown): Promise<AuditEntry[]> {
  // Snapshot before the first await so concurrent mutation cannot replace the
  // verified prefix or change what was requested for inclusion in the record.
  const snapshot = JSON.parse(canonicalize(chain)) as AuditEntry[];
  const frozenDetail = JSON.parse(canonicalize(detail)) as unknown;
  if (!eventIsValid(event)) throw new TypeError('Audit event must be nonempty text of at most 128 characters');
  if (!await verifyAudit(snapshot)) throw new Error('Canonization Chain integrity failed; append refused');
  const unsigned = {
    sequence: snapshot.length + 1,
    previousHash: snapshot.at(-1)?.hash ?? GENESIS,
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    event,
    detail: frozenDetail,
  };
  const hash = await digest(unsigned);
  return [...snapshot, { ...unsigned, hash }];
}

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  authorizeSharing,
  effectiveSharing,
  freshSharing,
  idleBudget,
  revokeSharing,
  setSharingOption,
  type ShareSession,
  type SharingOption,
} from './sharing';

const now = 1_800_000_000_000;

describe('sharing authorization', () => {
  it('starts off, without an active window or dependent modes', () => {
    expect(freshSharing()).toEqual({
      enabled: false,
      expiresAt: null,
      percent: 15,
      autoManage: false,
      turbo: false,
      loadBalance: false,
    });
    expect(freshSharing()).not.toBe(freshSharing());
    expect(idleBudget(100, freshSharing(), false, now)).toBe(0);
  });

  it('creates only the explicit window and requested idle-resource ceiling', () => {
    const session = authorizeSharing(30, 10, now);
    expect(session).toEqual({
      enabled: true,
      expiresAt: now + 30 * 60_000,
      percent: 10,
      autoManage: false,
      turbo: false,
      loadBalance: false,
    });
    expect(authorizeSharing(60, 15, now).expiresAt).toBe(now + 3_600_000);
  });

  it.each([0, -1, 61, NaN, Infinity, -Infinity])('rejects invalid window %s', minutes => {
    expect(() => authorizeSharing(minutes, 15, now)).toThrow(RangeError);
  });

  it.each([0, -1, 15.01, 100, NaN, Infinity, -Infinity])('rejects invalid share ceiling %s', percent => {
    expect(() => authorizeSharing(15, percent, now)).toThrow(RangeError);
  });

  it.each([-1, NaN, Infinity, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER])('rejects invalid authorization time %s', time => {
    expect(() => authorizeSharing(15, 15, time)).toThrow(RangeError);
  });

  it('turns every mode off at the exact expiry and revokes explicitly', () => {
    let session = authorizeSharing(1, 15, now);
    for (const option of ['autoManage', 'turbo', 'loadBalance'] as const) {
      session = setSharingOption(session, option, true, now);
    }
    expect(effectiveSharing(session, now + 59_999).enabled).toBe(true);
    expect(effectiveSharing(session, now + 60_000)).toEqual(freshSharing());
    expect(effectiveSharing(session, now + 60_001)).toEqual(freshSharing());
    expect(revokeSharing()).toEqual(freshSharing());
  });

  it.each(['autoManage', 'turbo', 'loadBalance'] as const)(
    '%s requires an active, explicitly authorized window',
    option => {
      expect(() => setSharingOption(freshSharing(), option, true, now)).toThrow(/authorized share window/);
      const session = authorizeSharing(1, 15, now);
      expect(() => setSharingOption(session, option, true, now + 60_000)).toThrow(/authorized share window/);
      expect(setSharingOption(session, option, false, now + 60_000)).toEqual(freshSharing());
      expect(setSharingOption(session, option, true, now)[option]).toBe(true);
      expect(session[option]).toBe(false);
    },
  );

  it('fails closed for malformed or stale authorization', () => {
    const session = authorizeSharing(1, 15, now);
    for (const invalid of [
      null,
      undefined,
      { ...session, enabled: 'yes' },
      { ...session, expiresAt: null },
      { ...session, expiresAt: Infinity },
      { ...session, expiresAt: now + 3_600_001 },
      { ...session, percent: 16 },
      { ...session, percent: '15' },
      { ...session, autoManage: 'true' },
      { ...session, turbo: undefined },
      { ...session, loadBalance: 1 },
    ]) {
      expect(effectiveSharing(invalid as ShareSession, now)).toEqual(freshSharing());
      expect(idleBudget(100, invalid as ShareSession, false, now)).toBe(0);
    }
    expect(effectiveSharing(session, NaN)).toEqual(freshSharing());
    expect(effectiveSharing(session, -1)).toEqual(freshSharing());
  });

  it('rejects unrecognized options and non-boolean mode changes', () => {
    const session = authorizeSharing(1, 15, now);
    expect(() => setSharingOption(session, 'enabled' as SharingOption, true, now)).toThrow(TypeError);
    expect(() => setSharingOption(session, 'turbo', 'yes' as unknown as boolean, now)).toThrow(TypeError);
  });
});

describe('current idle-resource budget', () => {
  it('uses only currently idle resources and shrinks as idle capacity falls', () => {
    const session = authorizeSharing(15, 15, now);
    expect(idleBudget(80, session, false, now)).toBe(12);
    expect(idleBudget(20, session, false, now)).toBe(3);
    expect(idleBudget(0, session, false, now)).toBe(0);
  });

  it('yields immediately to foreground activity and after the window expires', () => {
    const session = authorizeSharing(1, 15, now);
    expect(idleBudget(100, session, true, now)).toBe(0);
    expect(idleBudget(100, session, false, now + 60_000)).toBe(0);
  });

  it('keeps TURBO and AUTO MANAGE under the same idle ceiling', () => {
    let session = authorizeSharing(15, 7, now);
    session = setSharingOption(session, 'turbo', true, now);
    session = setSharingOption(session, 'autoManage', true, now);
    session = setSharingOption(session, 'loadBalance', true, now);
    expect(idleBudget(200, session, false, now)).toBeCloseTo(14);
  });

  it.each([-1, NaN, Infinity, -Infinity])('never produces a budget from invalid idle capacity %s', idle => {
    expect(idleBudget(idle, authorizeSharing(1, 15, now), false, now)).toBe(0);
  });

  it('fails closed without a known boolean foreground state', () => {
    expect(idleBudget(100, authorizeSharing(1, 15, now), undefined as unknown as boolean, now)).toBe(0);
  });

  it('computes a finite bounded budget for very large resource quantities', () => {
    const budget = idleBudget(Number.MAX_VALUE, authorizeSharing(1, 15, now), false, now);
    expect(Number.isFinite(budget)).toBe(true);
    expect(budget).toBeLessThanOrEqual(Number.MAX_VALUE * 0.15);
  });
});

/**
 * A local, explicit authorization window. This module never measures or consumes
 * CPU, GPU, storage, bandwidth, or peer resources.
 */
export type ShareSession = {
  enabled: boolean;
  expiresAt: number | null;
  percent: number;
  autoManage: boolean;
  turbo: boolean;
  loadBalance: boolean;
};

export type SharingOption = 'autoManage' | 'turbo' | 'loadBalance';

const MAX_IDLE_SHARE_PERCENT = 15;
const MAX_WINDOW_MINUTES = 60;
const MINUTE_MS = 60_000;
const options: readonly SharingOption[] = ['autoManage', 'turbo', 'loadBalance'];

function validTime(value: number): boolean {
  return Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0 && value <= Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
}

function validPercent(value: number): boolean {
  return Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0 && value <= MAX_IDLE_SHARE_PERCENT;
}

/** Each new session requires fresh authorization. Sharing is always off initially. */
export const freshSharing = (): ShareSession => ({
  enabled: false,
  expiresAt: null,
  percent: MAX_IDLE_SHARE_PERCENT,
  autoManage: false,
  turbo: false,
  loadBalance: false,
});

/** Only an explicit user action should call this function. */
export function authorizeSharing(
  minutes: number,
  percent: number,
  now = Date.now(),
): ShareSession {
  if (!Number.isFinite(minutes) || minutes <= 0 || minutes > MAX_WINDOW_MINUTES) {
    throw new RangeError('Choose a share window greater than 0 and at most 60 minutes.');
  }
  if (!validPercent(percent)) {
    throw new RangeError('Choose more than 0 and at most 15% of currently idle resources.');
  }
  const expiresAt = now + minutes * MINUTE_MS;
  if (!validTime(now) || !validTime(expiresAt) || expiresAt <= now) {
    throw new RangeError('A valid current time and share expiry are required.');
  }
  return { ...freshSharing(), enabled: true, expiresAt, percent };
}

/** Revocation also removes every dependent sharing mode and the old expiry. */
export function revokeSharing(): ShareSession {
  return freshSharing();
}

/**
 * Recheck this at the point of use. Stale or malformed authorization fails closed;
 * an earlier UI state is never proof that sharing is still authorized.
 */
export function effectiveSharing(session: ShareSession, now = Date.now()): ShareSession {
  if (
    !session ||
    typeof session !== 'object' ||
    session.enabled !== true ||
    !validTime(now) ||
    typeof session.expiresAt !== 'number' ||
    !validTime(session.expiresAt) ||
    session.expiresAt <= now ||
    session.expiresAt - now > MAX_WINDOW_MINUTES * MINUTE_MS ||
    !validPercent(session.percent) ||
    typeof session.autoManage !== 'boolean' ||
    typeof session.turbo !== 'boolean' ||
    typeof session.loadBalance !== 'boolean'
  ) {
    return freshSharing();
  }
  return { ...session };
}

export function setSharingOption(
  session: ShareSession,
  option: SharingOption,
  enabled: boolean,
  now = Date.now(),
): ShareSession {
  if (!options.includes(option) || typeof enabled !== 'boolean') {
    throw new TypeError('A valid sharing option and boolean value are required.');
  }
  const current = effectiveSharing(session, now);
  if (enabled && !current.enabled) {
    throw new Error('Enable an authorized share window before enabling this mode.');
  }
  return { ...current, [option]: enabled };
}

/**
 * Maximum permitted amount from a supplied CURRENTLY IDLE resource quantity.
 * Foreground activity immediately yields the entire budget. This is a policy
 * calculation, not a resource allocator or a claim that sharing is running.
 */
export function idleBudget(
  idle: number,
  session: ShareSession,
  foregroundActive: boolean,
  now = Date.now(),
): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(idle) || idle <= 0 || foregroundActive !== false) {
    return 0;
  }
  const current = effectiveSharing(session, now);
  return current.enabled ? idle * (current.percent / 100) : 0;
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
import{createHash}from'node:crypto';import*as fs from'node:fs/promises';import{dirname,resolve,relative,isAbsolute,sep}from'node:path';import{fileURLToPath}from'node:url';
const root=dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));const manifest=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(resolve(root,'manifest.json'),'utf8'));let checked=0;
for(const file of manifest.files){if(typeof file.path!=='string'||file.path.includes('\\')||file.path.split('/').some(p=>!p||p==='.'||p==='..'||p.startsWith('.')))throw Error('Invalid manifest path');const path=resolve(root,file.path);const rel=relative(root,path);if(isAbsolute(rel)||rel==='..'||rel.startsWith('..'+sep))throw Error('Escaping path');let current=root;for(const part of file.path.split('/')){current=resolve(current,part);if((await fs.lstat(current)).isSymbolicLink())throw Error('Symlink refused');}const info=await fs.stat(path);if(!info.isFile())throw Error('Non-file refused');const bytes=await fs.readFile(path);if(bytes.length!==file.bytes||createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex')!==file.sha256)throw Error('Integrity mismatch: '+file.path);checked++;}
